export default function App() {
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<string>('home');
  const [tabHistory, setTabHistory] = useState<string[]>(['home']);
  const [isDashboardOpen, setIsDashboardOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isAuthModalOpen, setIsAuthModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isInstallModalOpen, setIsInstallModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isInviteModalOpen, setIsInviteModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [installBannerDismissed, setInstallBannerDismissed] = useState(false);
  const [isOnline, setIsOnline] = useState<boolean>(typeof navigator !== 'undefined' ? navigator.onLine : true);
  const [authChecked, setAuthChecked] = useState(false);
  const [engagement, setEngagement] = useState<CommunityEngagement>({
    likesCount: 0,
    sharesCount: 0,
    contactsCount: 0,
  });
  const [hasLiked, setHasLiked] = useState<boolean>(() => {
    try {
      return localStorage.getItem('qh_site_liked_v1') === '1';
    } catch {
      return false;
    }
  });
  const [shareToast, setShareToast] = useState<string>('');

  // Immediately hydrate saved authenticated user synchronously so closing & reopening NEVER asks to re-register
  const [user, setUser] = useState<UserProfile>(() => getSavedUserSnapshot() || EMPTY_GUEST_USER);

  // Confession bookings
  const [bookings, setBookings] = useState<ConfessionBooking[]>([]);

  // Wallet & Bank Accounts
  const [walletState, setWalletState] = useState<WalletState>({
    totalBalance: 0,
    tithes: 0,
    charity: 0,
    churchSupport: 0,
    withdrawnTotal: 0,
  });
  const [transactions, setTransactions] = useState<WalletTransaction[]>([]);
  const [ministryBanks, setMinistryBanks] = useState<MinistryBankAccounts | undefined>(undefined);
  const [userBankAccount, setUserBankAccount] = useState<BankAccountInfo | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleOnline = () => setIsOnline(true);
    const handleOffline = () => setIsOnline(false);

    window.addEventListener('online', handleOnline);
    window.addEventListener('offline', handleOffline);

    // Sync/re-hydrate verified user with backend without erasing local session on Render cold restarts
    fetchCurrentUser()
      .then((loggedInUser) => {
        if (loggedInUser) {
          setUser(loggedInUser);
        } else {
          const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
          if (params.get('inviteName') || params.get('invitePhone')) {
            setIsAuthModalOpen(true);
          }
        }
      })
      .finally(() => setAuthChecked(true));

    fetchCommunityEngagement().then(setEngagement).catch(() => {});

    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('online', handleOnline);
      window.removeEventListener('offline', handleOffline);
    };
  }, []);

  // Only load bookings and wallet data when the user is signed in
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!user.isAuthenticated) return;
    fetchConfessions().then(setBookings);
    fetchWalletData().then((data) => {
      if (data.balance) setWalletState(data.balance);
      if (data.transactions) setTransactions(data.transactions);
      if (data.ministryBanks) setMinistryBanks(data.ministryBanks);
      if (data.userBankAccount !== undefined) setUserBankAccount(data.userBankAccount);
    });
  }, [user.isAuthenticated]);

  // Browser Hardware/Chrome Back Button (`popstate`) Integration
  useEffect(() => {
    const onPopState = (event: PopStateEvent) => {
      // Close open overlays first before popping main tab
      if (isDashboardOpen) {
        setIsDashboardOpen(false);
        return;
      }
      if (isAuthModalOpen) {
        setIsAuthModalOpen(false);
        return;
      }
      if (isInviteModalOpen) {
        setIsInviteModalOpen(false);
        return;
      }
      if (isInstallModalOpen) {
        setIsInstallModalOpen(false);
        return;
      }
      const targetTab = event.state?.tab;
      if (targetTab && typeof targetTab === 'string') {
        setActiveTab(targetTab);
        setTabHistory((prev) => (prev.length > 1 ? prev.slice(0, -1) : ['home']));
      } else {
        setActiveTab('home');
        setTabHistory(['home']);
      }
    };

    window.addEventListener('popstate', onPopState);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('popstate', onPopState);
  }, [isDashboardOpen, isAuthModalOpen, isInstallModalOpen, isInviteModalOpen]);

  const handleUpdateUser = (updated: UserProfile) => {
    setUser(updated);
  };

  const navigateToTab = useCallback(
    (tab: string) => {
      if (tab === activeTab) return;
      setTabHistory((prev) => [...prev, tab]);
      setActiveTab(tab);
      try {
        window.history.pushState({ tab }, '', `#${tab}`);
      } catch {}
      window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
    },
    [activeTab]
  );

  const handleNavigate = (tab: string) => {
    const protectedTabs = ['assembly', 'secret-room', 'wallet'];
    if (protectedTabs.includes(tab) && !user.isAuthenticated) {
      navigateToTab(tab);
      setIsAuthModalOpen(true);
      return;
    }
    navigateToTab(tab);
  };

  const handleGoBack = useCallback(() => {
    if (isDashboardOpen) {
      setIsDashboardOpen(false);
      return;
    }
    if (isAuthModalOpen) {
      setIsAuthModalOpen(false);
      return;
    }
    if (isInviteModalOpen) {
      setIsInviteModalOpen(false);
      return;
    }
    if (isInstallModalOpen) {
      setIsInstallModalOpen(false);
      return;
    }
    if (tabHistory.length > 1) {
      const nextHistory = tabHistory.slice(0, -1);
      const prevTab = nextHistory[nextHistory.length - 1] || 'home';
      setTabHistory(nextHistory);
      setActiveTab(prevTab);
    } else {
      setTabHistory(['home']);
      setActiveTab('home');
    }
    window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
  }, [isDashboardOpen, isAuthModalOpen, isInstallModalOpen, isInviteModalOpen, tabHistory]);

  const handleOpenDashboard = () => {
    if (!user.isAuthenticated) {
      setIsAuthModalOpen(true);
      return;
    }
    try {
      window.history.pushState({ tab: activeTab, modal: 'dashboard' }, '');
    } catch {}
    setIsDashboardOpen(true);
  };

  const handleUpdateBookingStatus = async (id: string, status: 'confirmed' | 'completed' | 'cancelled') => {
    setBookings((prev) => prev.map((b) => (b.id === id ? { ...b, status } : b)));
    try {
      await updateConfessionStatus(id, status);
    } catch {}
  };

  const handleGivingSuccess = (newTx: WalletTransaction, newBal: WalletState) => {
    setTransactions((prev) => [newTx, ...prev]);
    setWalletState(newBal);
  };

  const handleBankConfigUpdated = (mBanks: MinistryBankAccounts, uBank: BankAccountInfo | null) => {
    setMinistryBanks(mBanks);
    setUserBankAccount(uBank);
  };

  const { isInstalled, isInstallable, install } = usePWAInstall();

  const handleQuickInstall = async () => {
    if (isInstallable) {
      const outcome = await install();
      if (outcome === 'accepted') return;
    }
    setIsInstallModalOpen(true);
  };

  const handleToggleLike = async () => {
    const nextLiked = !hasLiked;
    setHasLiked(nextLiked);
    setEngagement((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      likesCount: Math.max(0, prev.likesCount + (nextLiked ? 1 : -1)),
    }));
    try {
      localStorage.setItem('qh_site_liked_v1', nextLiked ? '1' : '0');
    } catch {}
    const updated = await toggleWebsiteLike(nextLiked ? 'like' : 'unlike');
    setEngagement(updated);
  };

  const handleShareSite = async () => {
    const url = window.location.origin;
    const text = `በ"ቀሲስ ሄኖክ" የኢትዮጵያ ኦርቶዶክስ ተዋሕዶ የቀጥታ ዙም ጉባኤ፣ የንስሐ ሚስጥር ቤት እና መንፈሳዊ ኤአይ ሊቅ ይቀላቀሉ፦ ${url}`;
    try {
      if (navigator.share) {
        await navigator.share({
          title: 'ቀሲስ ሄኖክ — መንፈሳዊ ጉባኤና ኤአይ',
          text,
          url,
        });
      } else {
        await navigator.clipboard.writeText(url);
        setShareToast('የድህረገጹ ሊንክ ተቀድቷል! ለወዳጆችዎ ይላኩ።');
        setTimeout(() => setShareToast(''), 3500);
      }
      const updated = await recordWebsiteShare();
      setEngagement(updated);
    } catch {}
  };

  const renderAuthGate = (title: string) => (
    <div className="max-w-xl mx-auto px-4 py-16">
      <div className="p-8 rounded-3xl bg-stone-900/80 border-2 border-amber-700/50 shadow-2xl text-center space-y-5">
        <div className="w-16 h-16 mx-auto rounded-2xl bg-amber-950 border border-amber-600/50 flex items-center justify-center text-amber-300">
          <Lock className="w-8 h-8" />
        </div>
        <h2 className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-heading font-bold text-amber-100">{title}</h2>
        <p className="text-xs text-stone-300 leading-relaxed">
          እያንዳንዱ ተጠቃሚ በራሱ ስም እና ማንነት እንዲገባ በቅድሚያ ወደ አካውንትዎ ይግቡ ወይም አዲስ ይመዝገቡ።
        </p>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap justify-center gap-3">
          <button
            onClick={() => setIsAuthModalOpen(true)}
            className="px-6 py-3 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-r from-red-900 via-amber-900 to-red-900 border border-amber-500/60 text-white font-bold text-sm inline-flex items-center gap-2 hover:scale-[1.02] transition"
          >
            <LogIn className="w-4 h-4" />
            <span>ግባ / አዲስ ተመዝገብ</span>
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={handleGoBack}
            className="px-5 py-3 rounded-xl bg-stone-900 border border-stone-700 text-stone-200 font-bold text-sm inline-flex items-center gap-2 hover:bg-stone-800 transition"
          >
            <ArrowLeft className="w-4 h-4" />
            <span>ወደ ኋላ ተመለስ</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen flex flex-col bg-stone-950 text-stone-100 selection:bg-amber-700/30 selection:text-amber-200">
      
      {!isOnline && (
        <div className="bg-amber-600 text-stone-950 text-xs py-2 px-4 text-center font-bold flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
          <WifiOff className="w-4 h-4" />
          <span>የኢንተርኔት ግንኙነት የለም — መተግበሪያው በስልክዎ ኦፍላይን ማህደረ-ትውስታ እየሰራ ነው።</span>
        </div>
      )}

      <Navbar
        activeTab={activeTab}
        setActiveTab={handleNavigate}
        user={user}
        onOpenAuth={() => setIsAuthModalOpen(true)}
        onOpenDashboard={handleOpenDashboard}
        onOpenInstall={() => setIsInstallModalOpen(true)}
        isOnline={isOnline}
        canGoBack={activeTab !== 'home'}
        onGoBack={handleGoBack}
      />

      {/* Sticky In-App Back Bar whenever user enters ANY sub-page */}
      {activeTab !== 'home' && (
        <div className="sticky top-16 sm:top-18 z-30 bg-stone-900/95 backdrop-blur-md border-b border-amber-800/30 px-3 sm:px-6 py-2">
          <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
            <button
              onClick={handleGoBack}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-amber-950/80 hover:bg-amber-900 border border-amber-600/50 text-amber-200 text-xs font-bold transition shadow-sm"
            >
              <ArrowLeft className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>ወደ ኋላ ተመለስ (Back)</span>
            </button>

            <span className="text-xs font-heading font-semibold text-amber-300 truncate">
              {TAB_TITLES[activeTab] || ''}
            </span>

            <button
              onClick={() => navigateToTab('home')}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-stone-950 hover:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-700 text-stone-300 text-xs font-medium transition shrink-0"
              title="ወደ ዋና መነሻ"
            >
              <Home className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-400" />
              <span className="hidden sm:inline">ዋና መነሻ</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {!isInstalled && !installBannerDismissed && activeTab === 'home' && (
        <div className="sm:hidden bg-gradient-to-r from-red-950 via-stone-900 to-amber-950 border-b border-amber-800/40 px-3 py-2 flex items-center justify-between gap-2 text-xs">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-stone-200 min-w-0">
            <img src="/pwa-192x192.png" alt="ቀሲስ ሄኖክ" className="w-6 h-6 rounded-md border border-amber-500/50 shrink-0" />
            <span className="font-semibold text-[11px] truncate">ቀሲስ ሄኖክ አፕን ወደ ስልክ ጫን</span>
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0">
            <button
              onClick={handleQuickInstall}
              className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-md bg-amber-500 hover:bg-amber-400 text-stone-950 font-bold text-[11px] shadow transition inline-flex items-center gap-1"
            >
              <Smartphone className="w-3 h-3" />
              <span>{isInstallable ? 'አሁን ጫን' : 'መጫኛ'}</span>
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => setInstallBannerDismissed(true)}
              aria-label="Dismiss install banner"
              className="p-1 text-stone-400 hover:text-stone-200 text-xs leading-none"
            >
              ✕
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {shareToast && (
        <div className="fixed bottom-20 right-4 z-50 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-emerald-950 border border-emerald-500 text-emerald-200 text-xs font-bold shadow-2xl">
          {shareToast}
        </div>
      )}

      <main className="flex-1 pb-20 lg:pb-12">
        {activeTab === 'home' && (
          <>
            <HeroSection
              onNavigate={handleNavigate}
              engagement={engagement}
              hasLiked={hasLiked}
              onToggleLike={handleToggleLike}
              onShareSite={handleShareSite}
              onOpenContactsInvite={() => setIsInviteModalOpen(true)}
            />

            <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-6">
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
                
                <div
                  onClick={() => handleNavigate('assembly')}
                  className="p-4 sm:p-5 rounded-2xl bg-stone-900/60 border border-stone-800 hover:border-amber-600/60 transition-all cursor-pointer group shadow-lg flex items-center justify-between"
                >
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                    <div className="w-11 h-11 rounded-xl bg-amber-950/70 border border-amber-600/40 flex items-center justify-center text-amber-400 group-hover:scale-105 transition-transform shrink-0">
                      <Users className="w-5 h-5" />
                    </div>
                    <h4 className="text-sm font-heading font-bold text-amber-200">የቀጥታ ዙም ጉባኤ (Zoom Meeting)</h4>
                  </div>
                  <span className="text-amber-400 font-bold group-hover:translate-x-1 transition-transform">→</span>
                </div>

                <div
                  onClick={() => handleNavigate('secret-room')}
                  className="p-4 sm:p-5 rounded-2xl bg-stone-900/60 border border-stone-800 hover:border-red-600/60 transition-all cursor-pointer group shadow-lg flex items-center justify-between"
                >
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                    <div className="w-11 h-11 rounded-xl bg-red-950/70 border border-red-600/40 flex items-center justify-center text-red-400 group-hover:scale-105 transition-transform shrink-0">
                      <Shield className="w-5 h-5" />
                    </div>
                    <h4 className="text-sm font-heading font-bold text-amber-200">የንስሐ ሚስጥር ቤት</h4>
                  </div>
                  <span className="text-red-400 font-bold group-hover:translate-x-1 transition-transform">→</span>
                </div>

                <div
                  onClick={() => handleNavigate('ai-advisor')}
                  className="p-4 sm:p-5 rounded-2xl bg-stone-900/60 border border-stone-800 hover:border-amber-600/60 transition-all cursor-pointer group shadow-lg flex items-center justify-between"
                >
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                    <div className="w-11 h-11 rounded-xl bg-amber-950/70 border border-amber-600/40 flex items-center justify-center text-amber-400 group-hover:scale-105 transition-transform shrink-0">
                      <Sparkles className="w-5 h-5" />
                    </div>
                    <h4 className="text-sm font-heading font-bold text-amber-200">የኤአይ መንፈሳዊ ጭንቅላት</h4>
                  </div>
                  <span className="text-amber-400 font-bold group-hover:translate-x-1 transition-transform">→</span>
                </div>

                <div
                  onClick={() => handleNavigate('wallet')}
                  className="p-4 sm:p-5 rounded-2xl bg-stone-900/60 border border-stone-800 hover:border-emerald-600/60 transition-all cursor-pointer group shadow-lg flex items-center justify-between"
                >
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                    <div className="w-11 h-11 rounded-xl bg-emerald-950/70 border border-emerald-600/40 flex items-center justify-center text-emerald-400 group-hover:scale-105 transition-transform shrink-0">
                      <Wallet className="w-5 h-5" />
                    </div>
                    <h4 className="text-sm font-heading font-bold text-amber-200">መንፈሳዊ ዋሌት</h4>
                  </div>
                  <span className="text-emerald-400 font-bold group-hover:translate-x-1 transition-transform">→</span>
                </div>

              </div>
            </div>
          </>
        )}

        <Suspense fallback={<TabLoader />}>
          {activeTab === 'assembly' &&
            (user.isAuthenticated ? (
              <AssemblyHall user={user} onLeaveMeeting={handleGoBack} />
            ) : authChecked ? (
              renderAuthGate('ወደ ቀጥታ የዙም ጉባኤ ለመግባት ይግቡ')
            ) : <TabLoader />)}

          {activeTab === 'secret-room' &&
            (user.isAuthenticated ? (
              <SecretConfessionRoom
                user={user}
                bookings={bookings}
                onCompleteConfession={(id) => handleUpdateBookingStatus(id, 'completed')}
              />
            ) : authChecked ? (
              renderAuthGate('ወደ ንስሐ ሚስጥር ቤት ለመግባት ይግቡ')
            ) : <TabLoader />)}

          {activeTab === 'ai-advisor' && <SpiritualAIAdvisor user={user} />}

          {activeTab === 'wallet' &&
            (user.isAuthenticated ? (
              <SpiritualWallet
                user={user}
                walletState={walletState}
                transactions={transactions}
                ministryBanks={ministryBanks}
                userBankAccount={userBankAccount}
                onGivingSuccess={handleGivingSuccess}
                onBankConfigUpdated={handleBankConfigUpdated}
              />
            ) : authChecked ? (
              renderAuthGate('መንፈሳዊ ዋሌት ለመጠቀም ይግቡ')
            ) : <TabLoader />)}

          {activeTab === 'offline-books' && <OfflineLibrary />}
        </Suspense>
      </main>

      <div className="lg:hidden fixed bottom-0 left-0 right-0 z-40 bg-stone-950/95 backdrop-blur-md border-t border-amber-900/30 px-3 py-2 flex items-center justify-around text-[10px]">
        <button
          onClick={() => handleNavigate('home')}
          className={`flex flex-col items-center gap-1 py-1 px-2 ${activeTab === 'home' ? 'text-amber-300 font-bold' : 'text-stone-400'}`}
        >
          <Flame className="w-4 h-4" />
          <span>መነሻ</span>
        </button>

        <button
          onClick={() => handleNavigate('assembly')}
          className={`flex flex-col items-center gap-1 py-1 px-2 ${activeTab === 'assembly' ? 'text-amber-300 font-bold' : 'text-stone-400'}`}
        >
          <Users className="w-4 h-4" />
          <span>ዙም</span>
        </button>

        <button
          onClick={() => handleNavigate('secret-room')}
          className={`flex flex-col items-center gap-1 py-1 px-2 ${activeTab === 'secret-room' ? 'text-red-400 font-bold' : 'text-stone-400'}`}
        >
          <Shield className="w-4 h-4 text-red-400" />
          <span>ሚስጥር ቤት</span>
        </button>

        <button
          onClick={() => handleNavigate('ai-advisor')}
          className={`flex flex-col items-center gap-1 py-1 px-2 ${activeTab === 'ai-advisor' ? 'text-amber-300 font-bold' : 'text-stone-400'}`}
        >
          <Sparkles className="w-4 h-4" />
          <span>ኤአይ</span>
        </button>

        <button
          onClick={() => handleNavigate('wallet')}
          className={`flex flex-col items-center gap-1 py-1 px-2 ${activeTab === 'wallet' ? 'text-amber-300 font-bold' : 'text-stone-400'}`}
        >
          <Wallet className="w-4 h-4" />
          <span>ዋሌት</span>
        </button>

        <button
          onClick={() => handleNavigate('offline-books')}
          className={`flex flex-col items-center gap-1 py-1 px-2 ${activeTab === 'offline-books' ? 'text-amber-300 font-bold' : 'text-stone-400'}`}
        >
          <BookOpen className="w-4 h-4" />
          <span>ጸሎት</span>
        </button>
      </div>

      <footer className="border-t border-amber-900/20 bg-stone-950 py-8 text-center text-xs text-stone-400 space-y-3 px-4">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-center gap-2 pb-1">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleToggleLike}
            className={`px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl border text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5 transition ${
              hasLiked
                ? 'bg-red-950/90 border-red-500 text-red-200'
                : 'bg-stone-900 border-stone-700 text-stone-200 hover:border-red-500/60'
            }`}
          >
            <Heart className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 ${hasLiked ? 'fill-red-400 text-red-400' : 'text-red-400'}`} />
            <span>ላይክ ({engagement.likesCount})</span>
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleShareSite}
            className="px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-amber-950/70 hover:bg-amber-900 border border-amber-700/60 text-amber-200 text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5 transition"
          >
            <Share2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-400" />
            <span>ሼር ({engagement.sharesCount})</span>
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setIsInviteModalOpen(true)}
            className="px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-emerald-950/70 hover:bg-emerald-900 border border-emerald-700/60 text-emerald-200 text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5 transition"
          >
            <UserPlus className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-400" />
            <span>ከስልክ ሰው ጨምር ({engagement.contactsCount})</span>
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
          <Flame className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-500 animate-candle" />
          <span className="font-heading font-bold text-amber-200">ቀሲስ ሄኖክ — የንስሐና የመንፈሳዊ አገልግሎት ሥርዓት</span>
        </div>
        <p className="max-w-md mx-auto text-stone-400 font-semibold">
          "አቤቱ፥ ንጹሕ ልብን ፍጠርልኝ፥ የቀናውንም መንፈስ በውስጤ አድስ።" (መዝሙረ ዳዊት ፶፩:፲)
        </p>
        <p className="text-[11px] text-stone-500 font-semibold">
          © {new Date().getFullYear()} ቀሲስ ሄኖክ. ሁሉም መብቱ የተጠበቀ ነው።
        </p>
      </footer>

      <Suspense fallback={null}>
        {isDashboardOpen && (
          <SuperDashboardDrawer
            isOpen={isDashboardOpen}
            onClose={() => setIsDashboardOpen(false)}
            bookings={bookings}
            onUpdateBookingStatus={handleUpdateBookingStatus}
            walletState={walletState}
            transactions={transactions}
            currentUser={user}
            onUpdateUser={handleUpdateUser}
            onOpenAuthModal={() => setIsAuthModalOpen(true)}
          />
        )}

        {isAuthModalOpen && (
          <AuthProfileModal
            isOpen={isAuthModalOpen}
            onClose={() => setIsAuthModalOpen(false)}
            currentUser={user}
            onUpdateUser={handleUpdateUser}
          />
        )}

        {isInstallModalOpen && (
          <PWAInstallModal
            isOpen={isInstallModalOpen}
            onClose={() => setIsInstallModalOpen(false)}
          />
        )}

        {isInviteModalOpen && (
          <CommunityInviteModal
            isOpen={isInviteModalOpen}
            onClose={() => setIsInviteModalOpen(false)}
            user={user}
            engagement={engagement}
            onEngagementChange={setEngagement}
            onShareSite={handleShareSite}
          />
        )}
      </Suspense>

    </div>
  );
}

import {
  ConfessionBooking,
  WalletState,
  WalletTransaction,
  UserProfile,
  BankAccountInfo,
  MinistryBankAccounts,
  CommunityEngagement,
  PhoneContactRecord,
} from '../types';

const AUTH_TOKEN_KEY = 'qh_auth_token';
const SAVED_USER_KEY = 'qh_saved_user_v1';
const RECOVERY_RECORDS_KEY = 'qh_recovery_records_v1';
const WALLET_BACKUP_KEY = 'qh_wallet_backup_v1';
const BANK_BACKUP_KEY = 'qh_bank_backup_v1';

// Optional external backend URL (used if frontend is on Vercel and backend is on Render)
const RAW_API_BASE = (import.meta.env.VITE_API_URL || '').trim().replace(/\/+$/, '');

export function apiUrl(path: string): string {
  const normalizedPath = path.startsWith('/') ? path : `/${path}`;
  return RAW_API_BASE ? `${RAW_API_BASE}${normalizedPath}` : normalizedPath;
}

export function getRealtimeWsUrl(): string {
  if (RAW_API_BASE) {
    try {
      const parsed = new URL(RAW_API_BASE);
      const wsProto = parsed.protocol === 'https:' ? 'wss:' : 'ws:';
      return `${wsProto}//${parsed.host}/ws/realtime`;
    } catch {}
  }
  const protocol = window.location.protocol === 'https:' ? 'wss:' : 'ws:';
  return `${protocol}//${window.location.host}/ws/realtime`;
}

export function getAuthToken(): string | null {
  try {
    return localStorage.getItem(AUTH_TOKEN_KEY);
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

export function setAuthToken(token: string | null) {
  try {
    if (token) {
      localStorage.setItem(AUTH_TOKEN_KEY, token);
    } else {
      localStorage.removeItem(AUTH_TOKEN_KEY);
    }
  } catch {}
}

export function getSavedUserSnapshot(): UserProfile | null {
  try {
    const token = getAuthToken();
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(SAVED_USER_KEY);
    if (!token || !raw) return null;
    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
    if (parsed && parsed.isAuthenticated && parsed.id) {
      return parsed as UserProfile;
    }
  } catch {}
  return null;
}

function saveUserSnapshot(user: UserProfile | null, recoveryRecord?: string) {
  try {
    if (user && user.isAuthenticated) {
      localStorage.setItem(SAVED_USER_KEY, JSON.stringify(user));
      if (recoveryRecord) {
        const mapRaw = localStorage.getItem(RECOVERY_RECORDS_KEY);
        const map: Record<string, string> = mapRaw ? JSON.parse(mapRaw) : {};
        if (user.phone) map[user.phone.trim()] = recoveryRecord;
        if (user.email) map[user.email.trim().toLowerCase()] = recoveryRecord;
        map.__latest__ = recoveryRecord;
        localStorage.setItem(RECOVERY_RECORDS_KEY, JSON.stringify(map));
      }
    } else {
      localStorage.removeItem(SAVED_USER_KEY);
    }
  } catch {}
}

function findRecoveryRecord(identifier?: string): string | null {
  try {
    const mapRaw = localStorage.getItem(RECOVERY_RECORDS_KEY);
    if (!mapRaw) return null;
    const map: Record<string, string> = JSON.parse(mapRaw);
    if (identifier) {
      const key = identifier.trim();
      return map[key] || map[key.toLowerCase()] || map.__latest__ || null;
    }
    return map.__latest__ || null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

async function safeFetchJson<T = any>(
  path: string,
  options: RequestInit = {},
  timeoutMs = 25000
): Promise<T> {
  const controller = new AbortController();
  const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), timeoutMs);

  let res: Response;
  try {
    res = await fetch(apiUrl(path), {
      ...options,
      signal: controller.signal,
    });
  } catch (err: any) {
    clearTimeout(timer);
    if (err?.name === 'AbortError') {
      throw new Error('ሰርቨሩ መልስ ለመስጠት ዘግይቷል (Timeout)። የRender ሰርቨር ከእንቅልፍ እየተነሳ ሊሆን ስለሚችል ከ30 ሰከንድ በኋላ ደግመው ይሞክሩ።');
    }
    throw new Error('ከሰርቨሩ ጋር መገናኘት አልተቻለም። ኢንተርኔትዎን ወይም የሰርቨሩን መስራት ያረጋግጡ።');
  }
  clearTimeout(timer);

  const rawText = await res.text();
  let data: any = null;
  try {
    data = rawText ? JSON.parse(rawText) : {};
  } catch {
    const isVercelHost =
      typeof window !== 'undefined' && window.location.hostname.includes('vercel.app');
    if (res.status === 404 || rawText.includes('The page could not be found')) {
      if (isVercelHost && !RAW_API_BASE) {
        throw new Error(
          'ይህ የVercel ገፅ ከRender ሰርቨር ጋር አልተገናኘም! በቀጥታ የRender ሊንክዎን (.onrender.com) ይጠቀሙ፣ ወይም Vercel ላይ VITE_API_URL ሞልተው Redeploy ያድርጉ።'
        );
      }
      throw new Error('የተጠየቀው የሰርቨር አገልግሎት (API 404) አልተገኘም። ሰርቨሩ ላይ አዲሱ ኮድ መጫኑን ያረጋግጡ።');
    }
    if (res.status === 502 || res.status === 503 || res.status === 504) {
      throw new Error('የRender ሰርቨሩ ከእንቅልፍ እየተነሳ ነው (503)። እባክዎ 30 ሰከንድ ጠብቀው ደግመው ይሞክሩ።');
    }
    throw new Error('ሰርቨሩ ትክክለኛ መረጃ አልመለሰም። እባክዎ የRender ሊንክዎን ወይም የሰርቨሩን ሁኔታ ያረጋግጡ።');
  }

  if (!res.ok) {
    const errorObj: any = new Error(data?.message || data?.error || `የሰርቨር ስህተት (${res.status})`);
    errorObj.status = res.status;
    errorObj.code = data?.code;
    throw errorObj;
  }

  return data as T;
}

// --- 1. REAL PERMANENT AUTHENTICATION API ---
export async function registerUser(payload: {
  fullName: string;
  baptismalName: string;
  phone: string;
  email?: string;
  password: string;
  role?: 'child' | 'priest' | 'deacon';
  confessorPriest?: string;
  confessionStage?: string;
  priestCode?: string;
}): Promise<{ user: UserProfile; token: string }> {
  const data = await safeFetchJson<{ user: UserProfile; token: string; recoveryRecord?: string }>(
    '/api/auth/register',
    {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify(payload),
    }
  );
  setAuthToken(data.token);
  saveUserSnapshot(data.user, data.recoveryRecord);
  return data;
}

export async function loginUser(identifier: string, password: string): Promise<{ user: UserProfile; token: string }> {
  try {
    const data = await safeFetchJson<{ user: UserProfile; token: string; recoveryRecord?: string }>(
      '/api/auth/login',
      {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ identifier, password }),
      }
    );
    setAuthToken(data.token);
    saveUserSnapshot(data.user, data.recoveryRecord);
    return data;
  } catch (err: any) {
    // If server disk reset on Render Free Tier, automatically restore from signed recovery record
    if (err?.status === 404 || err?.code === 'USER_NOT_FOUND') {
      const rec = findRecoveryRecord(identifier);
      if (rec) {
        const restored = await safeFetchJson<{ user: UserProfile; token: string; recoveryRecord?: string }>(
          '/api/auth/restore',
          {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
            body: JSON.stringify({ recoveryRecord: rec, identifier, password }),
          }
        );
        setAuthToken(restored.token);
        saveUserSnapshot(restored.user, restored.recoveryRecord);
        return restored;
      }
    }
    throw err;
  }
}

export async function fetchCurrentUser(): Promise<UserProfile | null> {
  const token = getAuthToken();
  if (!token) return null;
  try {
    const data = await safeFetchJson<{ user: UserProfile; token?: string; recoveryRecord?: string }>(
      '/api/auth/me',
      { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } },
      12000
    );
    if (data.token) setAuthToken(data.token);
    saveUserSnapshot(data.user, data.recoveryRecord);
    return data.user;
  } catch (err: any) {
    // If Render restarted and token was legacy or expired, try auto-restoring using signed recoveryRecord
    const rec = findRecoveryRecord();
    if (rec && (err?.status === 401 || err?.status === 404)) {
      try {
        const restored = await safeFetchJson<{ user: UserProfile; token: string; recoveryRecord?: string }>(
          '/api/auth/restore',
          {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
            body: JSON.stringify({ recoveryRecord: rec }),
          },
          12000
        );
        setAuthToken(restored.token);
        saveUserSnapshot(restored.user, restored.recoveryRecord);
        return restored.user;
      } catch {}
    }
    // Keep saved local snapshot intact on offline/transient network errors so user is NEVER kicked out
    return getSavedUserSnapshot();
  }
}

export async function updateUserProfile(payload: Partial<UserProfile>): Promise<UserProfile> {
  const token = getAuthToken();
  const data = await safeFetchJson<{ user: UserProfile; token?: string; recoveryRecord?: string }>(
    '/api/auth/profile',
    {
      method: 'PUT',
      headers: {
        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        ...(token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {}),
      },
      body: JSON.stringify(payload),
    }
  );
  if (data.token) setAuthToken(data.token);
  saveUserSnapshot(data.user, data.recoveryRecord);
  return data.user;
}

export async function logoutUser(): Promise<void> {
  const token = getAuthToken();
  if (token) {
    fetch(apiUrl('/api/auth/logout'), {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },
    }).catch(() => {});
  }
  setAuthToken(null);
  saveUserSnapshot(null);
}

export async function fetchRegisteredUsers(): Promise<UserProfile[]> {
  try {
    const data = await safeFetchJson<{ users: UserProfile[] }>('/api/users', {}, 12000);
    return data.users || [];
  } catch {
    return [];
  }
}

// --- 2. REAL GEMINI AI SPIRITUAL COUNSEL & ORTHODOX SEARCH (STREAMING + FAST CACHE) ---
async function readSseStreamOnce(
  path: string,
  body: object,
  onChunk: (accumulatedText: string) => void,
  signal?: AbortSignal
): Promise<string> {
  const res = await fetch(apiUrl(path), {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify(body),
    signal,
  });

  if (!res.ok) {
    const text = await res.text();
    let msg = `የኤአይ ሰርቨር ስህተት (${res.status})`;
    let retryable = res.status === 502 || res.status === 503 || res.status === 504;
    try {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(text);
      msg = parsed.message || parsed.error || msg;
      if (parsed.retryable !== undefined) retryable = Boolean(parsed.retryable);
    } catch {}
    const err: any = new Error(msg);
    err.status = res.status;
    err.retryable = retryable;
    throw err;
  }

  const contentType = res.headers.get('content-type') || '';
  if (!contentType.includes('text/event-stream') || !res.body) {
    const json = await res.json();
    const full = json.reply || json.result || '';
    onChunk(full);
    return full;
  }

  const reader = res.body.getReader();
  const decoder = new TextDecoder();
  let buffer = '';
  let accumulated = '';

  while (true) {
    const { value, done } = await reader.read();
    if (done) break;
    buffer += decoder.decode(value, { stream: true });
    const events = buffer.split('\n\n');
    buffer = events.pop() || '';

    for (const evt of events) {
      for (const line of evt.split('\n')) {
        if (!line.startsWith('data:')) continue;
        const raw = line.slice(5).trim();
        if (!raw) continue;
        try {
          const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
          if (parsed.error) {
            const err: any = new Error(parsed.error);
            err.retryable = Boolean(parsed.retryable) || String(parsed.error).includes('503') || String(parsed.error).includes('ተጨናንቋል');
            throw err;
          }
          if (parsed.chunk) {
            accumulated += parsed.chunk;
            onChunk(accumulated);
          }
        } catch (err: any) {
          if (err instanceof Error && err.message && !err.message.includes('JSON')) {
            throw err;
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }

  return accumulated;
}

async function readSseStream(
  path: string,
  body: object,
  onChunk: (accumulatedText: string) => void,
  signal?: AbortSignal
): Promise<string> {
  try {
    return await readSseStreamOnce(path, body, onChunk, signal);
  } catch (err: any) {
    if (err?.name === 'AbortError' || signal?.aborted) throw err;
    const isTransient = Boolean(err?.retryable) || err?.status === 502 || err?.status === 503 || err?.status === 504;
    if (!isTransient) throw err;
    // Wait 1.6s and retry automatically once when Gemini server reports transient high demand (503)
    await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
      const t = setTimeout(resolve, 1600);
      signal?.addEventListener(
        'abort',
        () => {
          clearTimeout(t);
          reject(new DOMException('Aborted', 'AbortError'));
        },
        { once: true }
      );
    });
    return readSseStreamOnce(path, body, onChunk, signal);
  }
}

export async function streamSpiritualCounsel(
  prompt: string,
  topic: string,
  baptismalName: string | undefined,
  onChunk: (accumulatedText: string) => void,
  signal?: AbortSignal
): Promise<string> {
  return readSseStream(
    '/api/gemini/spiritual-counsel-stream',
    { prompt, topic, baptismalName },
    onChunk,
    signal
  );
}

export async function streamOrthodoxSearch(
  query: string,
  category: string,
  onChunk: (accumulatedText: string) => void,
  signal?: AbortSignal
): Promise<string> {
  return readSseStream(
    '/api/gemini/scripture-search-stream',
    { query, category },
    onChunk,
    signal
  );
}

    '/api/gemini/spiritual-counsel',
    {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ prompt, topic, baptismalName }),
    },
    35000
  );
  return data.reply;
}

export async function searchScripture(query: string, category?: string): Promise<string> {
  const data = await safeFetchJson<{ result: string }>(
    '/api/gemini/scripture-search',
    {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ query, category }),
    },
    35000
  );
  return data.result;
}

// --- 3. WEBRTC ICE / TURN / LIVEKIT SFU CONFIG ---
export interface ExtendedRTCConfig extends RTCConfiguration {
  sfuConfigured?: boolean;
  livekitUrl?: string | null;
}

export async function fetchWebRTCConfig(): Promise<ExtendedRTCConfig> {
  try {
    const data = await safeFetchJson<{
      iceServers: RTCIceServer[];
      sfuConfigured?: boolean;
      livekitUrl?: string | null;
    }>('/api/webrtc-config', {}, 10000);
    if (Array.isArray(data.iceServers)) {
      return {
        iceServers: data.iceServers,
        sfuConfigured: Boolean(data.sfuConfigured),
        livekitUrl: data.livekitUrl || null,
      };
    }
  } catch {}
  return {
    iceServers: [
      { urls: 'stun:stun.l.google.com:19302' },
      { urls: 'stun:stun1.l.google.com:19302' },
      { urls: 'stun:stun2.l.google.com:19302' },
    ],
    sfuConfigured: false,
    livekitUrl: null,
  };
}

export async function fetchLiveKitToken(
  roomId: string,
  userId: string,
  userName: string,
  canPublish: boolean
): Promise<{ sfuConfigured: boolean; url?: string; token?: string }> {
  return safeFetchJson('/api/livekit/token', {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify({ roomId, userId, userName, canPublish }),
  });
}

// --- 5. SPIRITUAL WALLET & BANKING API ---
export interface WalletDashboardPayload {
  balance: WalletState;
  transactions: WalletTransaction[];
  ministryBanks?: MinistryBankAccounts;
  userBankAccount?: BankAccountInfo | null;
}

function saveWalletLocalBackup(data: Partial<WalletDashboardPayload>) {
  try {
    if (data.balance || data.transactions) {
      const prevRaw = localStorage.getItem(WALLET_BACKUP_KEY);
      const prev = prevRaw ? JSON.parse(prevRaw) : {};
      localStorage.setItem(
        WALLET_BACKUP_KEY,
        JSON.stringify({
          balance: data.balance || prev.balance,
          transactions: data.transactions || prev.transactions || [],
        })
      );
    }
    if (data.ministryBanks || data.userBankAccount) {
      const prevRaw = localStorage.getItem(BANK_BACKUP_KEY);
      const prev = prevRaw ? JSON.parse(prevRaw) : {};
      localStorage.setItem(
        BANK_BACKUP_KEY,
        JSON.stringify({
          ministryBanks: data.ministryBanks || prev.ministryBanks,
          userBankAccount: data.userBankAccount !== undefined ? data.userBankAccount : prev.userBankAccount,
        })
      );
    }
  } catch {}
}

export async function fetchWalletData(): Promise<WalletDashboardPayload> {
  const token = getAuthToken();
  try {
    const data = await safeFetchJson<WalletDashboardPayload>(
      '/api/wallet',
      { headers: token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {} },
      12000
    );
    saveWalletLocalBackup(data);
    return data;
  } catch {
    try {
      const w = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(WALLET_BACKUP_KEY) || '{}');
      const b = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(BANK_BACKUP_KEY) || '{}');
      return {
        balance: w.balance || { totalBalance: 0, tithes: 0, charity: 0, churchSupport: 0, withdrawnTotal: 0 },
        transactions: w.transactions || [],
        ministryBanks: b.ministryBanks,
        userBankAccount: b.userBankAccount || null,
      };
    } catch {
      return {
        balance: { totalBalance: 0, tithes: 0, charity: 0, churchSupport: 0, withdrawnTotal: 0 },
        transactions: [],
      };
    }
  }
}

export async function submitWalletGiving(payload: {
  userId?: string;
  userName: string;
  type: string;
  amount: number;
  paymentMethod: string;
  purposeNote: string;
  bankName?: string;
  accountNumber?: string;
  accountHolderName?: string;
  referenceNumber?: string;
}): Promise<{ transaction: WalletTransaction; newBalance: WalletState }> {
  const token = getAuthToken();
  const res = await safeFetchJson<{ transaction: WalletTransaction; newBalance: WalletState }>(
    '/api/wallet/give',
    {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: {
        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        ...(token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {}),
      },
      body: JSON.stringify(payload),
    }
  );
  saveWalletLocalBackup({ balance: res.newBalance });
  return res;
}

export async function submitWalletWithdrawal(payload: {
  userName: string;
  amount: number;
  bankName: string;
  accountNumber: string;
  accountHolderName: string;
  purposeNote?: string;
  paymentMethod?: string;

================================================================================
FILE: src/hooks/usePWAInstall.ts
================================================================================
import { useEffect, useState, useCallback } from 'react';

export interface BeforeInstallPromptEvent extends Event {
  prompt: () => Promise<void>;
  userChoice: Promise<{ outcome: 'accepted' | 'dismissed'; platform: string }>;
}

declare global {
  interface Window {
    __deferredInstallPrompt?: BeforeInstallPromptEvent | null;
  }
}

export interface PWAEnvironment {
  isInstallable: boolean;
  isInstalled: boolean;
  isIOS: boolean;
  isAndroid: boolean;
  isInAppBrowser: boolean;
  inAppName: string | null;
  isSafariOnIOS: boolean;
  isSamsungInternet: boolean;
  isFirefox: boolean;
  install: () => Promise<'accepted' | 'dismissed' | 'unavailable'>;
}

function detectEnvironment() {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') {
    return {
      isStandalone: false,
      isIOS: false,
      isAndroid: false,
      isInAppBrowser: false,
      inAppName: null as string | null,
      isSafariOnIOS: false,
      isSamsungInternet: false,
      isFirefox: false,
    };
  }

  const ua = window.navigator.userAgent || '';
  const uaLower = ua.toLowerCase();

  const isStandalone =
    window.matchMedia('(display-mode: standalone)').matches ||
    window.matchMedia('(display-mode: fullscreen)').matches ||
    (window.navigator as unknown as { standalone?: boolean }).standalone === true;

  const isIOS =
    /iphone|ipad|ipod/.test(uaLower) ||
    (window.navigator.platform === 'MacIntel' && window.navigator.maxTouchPoints > 1);

  const isAndroid = /android/.test(uaLower);

  let inAppName: string | null = null;
  if (/telegram/i.test(ua)) inAppName = 'Telegram';
  else if (/whatsapp/i.test(ua)) inAppName = 'WhatsApp';
  else if (/instagram/i.test(ua)) inAppName = 'Instagram';
  else if (/fban|fbav|fb_iab/i.test(ua)) inAppName = 'Facebook / Messenger';
  else if (/tiktok|bytedancewebview|musical_ly/i.test(ua)) inAppName = 'TikTok';
  else if (/; wv\)/i.test(ua)) inAppName = 'In-App Browser';

  const isInAppBrowser = Boolean(inAppName);
  const isSamsungInternet = /samsungbrowser/i.test(ua);
  const isFirefox = /firefox|fxios/i.test(ua);
  const isSafariOnIOS =
    isIOS &&
    /safari/i.test(ua) &&
    !/crios|fxios|edgios|opios|duckduckgo/i.test(ua) &&
    !isInAppBrowser;

  return {
    isStandalone,
    isIOS,
    isAndroid,
    isInAppBrowser,
    inAppName,
    isSafariOnIOS,
    isSamsungInternet,
    isFirefox,
  };
}

export function usePWAInstall(): PWAEnvironment {
  const [deferredPrompt, setDeferredPrompt] = useState<BeforeInstallPromptEvent | null>(() =>
    typeof window !== 'undefined' ? window.__deferredInstallPrompt || null : null
  );
  const [env, setEnv] = useState(detectEnvironment);
  const [isInstalled, setIsInstalled] = useState(() => detectEnvironment().isStandalone);

  useEffect(() => {
    setEnv(detectEnvironment());
    if (window.__deferredInstallPrompt) {
      setDeferredPrompt(window.__deferredInstallPrompt);
    }

    const syncPrompt = () => {
      setDeferredPrompt(window.__deferredInstallPrompt || null);
    };

    const handleBeforeInstallPrompt = (e: Event) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      window.__deferredInstallPrompt = e as BeforeInstallPromptEvent;
      setDeferredPrompt(e as BeforeInstallPromptEvent);
    };

    const handleAppInstalled = () => {
      window.__deferredInstallPrompt = null;
      setDeferredPrompt(null);
      setIsInstalled(true);
    };

    const mediaQuery = window.matchMedia('(display-mode: standalone)');
    const handleDisplayModeChange = (e: MediaQueryListEvent) => {
      if (e.matches) setIsInstalled(true);
    };

    window.addEventListener('beforeinstallprompt', handleBeforeInstallPrompt);
    window.addEventListener('pwa-install-available', syncPrompt);
    window.addEventListener('appinstalled', handleAppInstalled);
    window.addEventListener('pwa-app-installed', handleAppInstalled);
    if (typeof mediaQuery.addEventListener === 'function') {
      mediaQuery.addEventListener('change', handleDisplayModeChange);
    }

    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('beforeinstallprompt', handleBeforeInstallPrompt);
      window.removeEventListener('pwa-install-available', syncPrompt);
      window.removeEventListener('appinstalled', handleAppInstalled);
      window.removeEventListener('pwa-app-installed', handleAppInstalled);
      if (typeof mediaQuery.removeEventListener === 'function') {
        mediaQuery.removeEventListener('change', handleDisplayModeChange);
      }
    };
  }, []);

  const install = useCallback(async (): Promise<'accepted' | 'dismissed' | 'unavailable'> => {
    const promptEvent = deferredPrompt || window.__deferredInstallPrompt;
    if (!promptEvent) return 'unavailable';
    await promptEvent.prompt();
    const { outcome } = await promptEvent.userChoice;
    if (outcome === 'accepted') {
      window.__deferredInstallPrompt = null;
      setDeferredPrompt(null);
      setIsInstalled(true);
      return 'accepted';
    }
    return 'dismissed';
  }, [deferredPrompt]);

  return {
    isInstallable: !!deferredPrompt,
    isInstalled,
    isIOS: env.isIOS,
    isAndroid: env.isAndroid,
    isInAppBrowser: env.isInAppBrowser,
    inAppName: env.inAppName,
    isSafariOnIOS: env.isSafariOnIOS,
    isSamsungInternet: env.isSamsungInternet,
    isFirefox: env.isFirefox,
    install,
  };
}

// Web Audio API Sound Generator for Sacred Chimes & Spiritual Hymns

let audioCtx: AudioContext | null = null;
let hymnOscillators: OscillatorNode[] = [];
let hymnGainNode: GainNode | null = null;
let isHymnPlaying = false;

function getAudioContext(): AudioContext {
  if (!audioCtx) {
    const AudioContextClass = window.AudioContext || (window as any).webkitAudioContext;
    audioCtx = new AudioContextClass();
  }
  if (audioCtx.state === 'suspended') {
    audioCtx.resume();
  }
  return audioCtx;
}

// 1. Sacred Church Hand Bell Chime (የመስቀልና የደወል ድምፅ)
export function playChurchBell() {
  try {
    const ctx = getAudioContext();
    const now = ctx.currentTime;

    // Harmonic frequencies for bronze hand cross bell (D5, A5, D6)
    const freqs = [587.33, 880.0, 1174.66, 1760.0];

    freqs.forEach((freq, idx) => {
      const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
      const gain = ctx.createGain();

      osc.type = idx === 0 ? 'sine' : 'triangle';
      osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq, now);

      // Exponential decay envelope
      const initialGain = idx === 0 ? 0.35 : 0.15 / (idx + 1);
      gain.gain.setValueAtTime(initialGain, now);
      gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, now + 3.2);

      osc.connect(gain);
      gain.connect(ctx.destination);

      osc.start(now);
      osc.stop(now + 3.3);
    });
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('Audio bell error:', err);
  }
}

// 2. Ethiopian Sacred Begena / Harp Pentatonic Spiritual Harmony (የበገናና የመዝሙር ዜማ)
export function toggleSpiritualHymn(onStateChange?: (playing: boolean) => void): boolean {
  try {
    const ctx = getAudioContext();

    if (isHymnPlaying) {
      stopSpiritualHymn();
      if (onStateChange) onStateChange(false);
      return false;
    }

    const now = ctx.currentTime;
    isHymnPlaying = true;

    hymnGainNode = ctx.createGain();
    hymnGainNode.gain.setValueAtTime(0.01, now);
    hymnGainNode.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.12, now + 1.5);
    hymnGainNode.connect(ctx.destination);

    // Pentatonic scale frequencies for meditative prayer (D3, F3, G3, A3, C4)
    const chordFreqs = [146.83, 174.61, 196.0, 220.0, 261.63];

    hymnOscillators = chordFreqs.map((freq, idx) => {
      const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
      osc.type = idx % 2 === 0 ? 'sine' : 'triangle';
      osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq, now);

      // Low frequency modulation for acoustic resonance
      const lfo = ctx.createOscillator();
      const lfoGain = ctx.createGain();
      lfo.frequency.setValueAtTime(2.5 + idx * 0.4, now);
      lfoGain.gain.setValueAtTime(1.5, now);
      lfo.connect(lfoGain);
      lfoGain.connect(osc.frequency);
      lfo.start(now);

      osc.connect(hymnGainNode!);
      osc.start(now);
      return osc;
    });

    if (onStateChange) onStateChange(true);
    return true;
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('Hymn player error:', err);
    if (onStateChange) onStateChange(false);
    return false;
  }
}

export function stopSpiritualHymn() {
  if (hymnGainNode && audioCtx) {
    hymnGainNode.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.001, audioCtx.currentTime + 0.8);
    setTimeout(() => {
      hymnOscillators.forEach((osc) => {
        try {
          osc.stop();
          osc.disconnect();
        } catch {}
      });
      hymnOscillators = [];
      isHymnPlaying = false;
    }, 900);
  } else {
    isHymnPlaying = false;
  }
}

// 3. Priest Henok's Voice Blessing via Web Speech API (የቀሲሱ የድምፅ ቡራኬ)
export function speakBlessingPrayer(text: string) {
  if ('speechSynthesis' in window) {
    window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
    const utterance = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(text);
    utterance.lang = 'am-ET';
    utterance.rate = 0.85;
    utterance.pitch = 0.95;
    window.speechSynthesis.speak(utterance);
  }
}

FILE: src/utils/wsMediaRelay.ts

// Universal HTTPS/WSS Port-443 Media Relay Fallback
// Features continuous ring-buffer jitter smoothing, anti-aliased downsampling, soft VAD hangover,
// and strict audio-over-video WebSocket prioritization so voices stay crystal clear on mobile 3G/4G.

function uint8ToBase64(bytes: Uint8Array): string {
  let binary = '';
  const chunkSize = 0x8000;
  for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i += chunkSize) {
    binary += String.fromCharCode.apply(null, Array.from(bytes.subarray(i, i + chunkSize)));
  }
  return btoa(binary);
}

function base64ToUint8(b64: string): Uint8Array {
  const binary = atob(b64);
  const len = binary.length;
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(len);
  for (let i = 0; i < len; i++) {
    bytes[i] = binary.charCodeAt(i);
  }
  return bytes;
}

interface PeerAudioQueue {
  buffer: Float32Array;
  capacity: number;
  readPos: number;
  writePos: number;
  bufferedSamples: number;
  primed: boolean;
  lastChunkAt: number;
  fadeInRemaining: number;
}

export class WsMediaAudioPlayer {
  private ctx: AudioContext | null = null;
  private processor: ScriptProcessorNode | null = null;
  private silentSource: GainNode | null = null;
  private peerQueues = new Map<string, PeerAudioQueue>();
  private onBlockedChange?: (blocked: boolean) => void;
  private globalMuted = false;

  constructor(onBlockedChange?: (blocked: boolean) => void) {
    this.onBlockedChange = onBlockedChange;
  }

  private ensurePipeline(): AudioContext | null {
    if (typeof window === 'undefined') return null;

    if (!this.ctx || this.ctx.state === 'closed') {
      const AudioCtx = window.AudioContext || (window as any).webkitAudioContext;
      if (!AudioCtx) return null;
      this.ctx = new AudioCtx();
    }

    if (!this.processor && this.ctx) {
      try {
        const ctx = this.ctx;
        // Continuous single output stream eliminates per-chunk BufferSource gaps & clicks
        const processor = ctx.createScriptProcessor(2048, 1, 1);
        const silentInput = ctx.createGain();
        silentInput.gain.value = 0;

        const outSampleRate = ctx.sampleRate || 48000;
        const targetJitterSamples = Math.round(outSampleRate * 0.14); // 140ms jitter cushion for 4G/Render RTT
        const maxBufferSamples = Math.round(outSampleRate * 0.45);
        const fadeLen = Math.round(outSampleRate * 0.008); // 8ms smooth fade

        processor.onaudioprocess = (ev) => {
          const out = ev.outputBuffer.getChannelData(0);
          out.fill(0);
          const nowMs = Date.now();

          for (const [peerId, q] of this.peerQueues.entries()) {
            if (nowMs - q.lastChunkAt > 10000 && q.bufferedSamples === 0) {
              this.peerQueues.delete(peerId);
              continue;
            }

            if (this.globalMuted) {
              // Drain quietly when muted so stale audio never accumulates
              const drop = Math.min(q.bufferedSamples, out.length);
              q.readPos = (q.readPos + drop) % q.capacity;
              q.bufferedSamples -= drop;
              if (q.bufferedSamples === 0) q.primed = false;
              continue;
            }

            if (!q.primed) {
              if (q.bufferedSamples >= targetJitterSamples || (q.bufferedSamples > 0 && nowMs - q.lastChunkAt > 160)) {
                q.primed = true;
                q.fadeInRemaining = fadeLen;
              } else {
                continue;
              }
            }

            // Cap excessive latency buildup smoothly
            if (q.bufferedSamples > maxBufferSamples) {
              const trim = q.bufferedSamples - targetJitterSamples;
              q.readPos = (q.readPos + trim) % q.capacity;
              q.bufferedSamples -= trim;
              q.fadeInRemaining = fadeLen;
            }

            const toRead = Math.min(q.bufferedSamples, out.length);
            if (toRead === 0) {
              q.primed = false;
              continue;
            }

            const underrunAfter = q.bufferedSamples - toRead === 0;
            for (let i = 0; i < toRead; i++) {
              let sample = q.buffer[q.readPos];
              q.readPos = (q.readPos + 1) % q.capacity;

              if (q.fadeInRemaining > 0) {
                const factor = (fadeLen - q.fadeInRemaining + 1) / fadeLen;
                sample *= factor;
                q.fadeInRemaining--;
              }

              // Soft fade-out on trailing edge before entering silence to prevent clicks
              if (underrunAfter && i >= toRead - fadeLen) {
                const remainingTail = toRead - 1 - i;
                sample *= remainingTail / fadeLen;
              }

              out[i] += sample;
            }
            q.bufferedSamples -= toRead;

            if (q.bufferedSamples === 0) {
              q.primed = false;
            }
          }

          // Soft limiter so overlapping speakers never clip harshly
          for (let i = 0; i < out.length; i++) {
            const v = out[i];
            if (v > 0.95 || v < -0.95) {
              out[i] = Math.tanh(v);
            }
          }
        };

        silentInput.connect(processor);
        processor.connect(ctx.destination);
        this.silentSource = silentInput;
        this.processor = processor;
      } catch {}
    }

    if (this.ctx.state === 'suspended') {
      this.onBlockedChange?.(true);
    } else {
      this.onBlockedChange?.(false);
    }
    return this.ctx;
  }

  async unlock(): Promise<boolean> {
    const ctx = this.ensurePipeline();
    if (!ctx) return false;
    try {
      if (ctx.state === 'suspended') {
        await ctx.resume();
      }
      const ok = ctx.state === 'running';
      this.onBlockedChange?.(!ok);
      return ok;
    } catch {
      this.onBlockedChange?.(true);
      return false;
    }
  }

  playChunk(peerId: string, pcm16Base64: string, sampleRate = 16000, isMuted = false) {
    this.globalMuted = isMuted;
    if (isMuted || !pcm16Base64) return;

    const ctx = this.ensurePipeline();
    if (!ctx) return;
    if (ctx.state === 'suspended') {
      ctx.resume().then(() => this.onBlockedChange?.(false)).catch(() => this.onBlockedChange?.(true));
      return;
    }

    try {
      const rawBytes = base64ToUint8(pcm16Base64);
      const int16 = new Int16Array(rawBytes.buffer, rawBytes.byteOffset, Math.floor(rawBytes.byteLength / 2));
      if (int16.length === 0) return;

      const outSampleRate = ctx.sampleRate || 48000;
      const ratio = outSampleRate / sampleRate;
      const resampledLen = Math.max(1, Math.round(int16.length * ratio));

      let q = this.peerQueues.get(peerId);
      if (!q) {
        const capacity = outSampleRate * 2; // 2-second ring buffer
        q = {
          buffer: new Float32Array(capacity),
          capacity,
          readPos: 0,
          writePos: 0,
          bufferedSamples: 0,
          primed: false,
          lastChunkAt: Date.now(),
          fadeInRemaining: 0,
        };
        this.peerQueues.set(peerId, q);
      }
      q.lastChunkAt = Date.now();

      // Linear interpolation upsampling (16kHz -> device 48kHz/44.1kHz) for warm, distortion-free voice
      for (let i = 0; i < resampledLen; i++) {
        const srcPos = i / ratio;
        const idx0 = Math.min(int16.length - 1, Math.floor(srcPos));
        const idx1 = Math.min(int16.length - 1, idx0 + 1);
        const frac = srcPos - idx0;
        const s0 = int16[idx0] / 32768;
        const s1 = int16[idx1] / 32768;
        const sample = s0 + (s1 - s0) * frac;

        if (q.bufferedSamples >= q.capacity) {
          q.readPos = (q.readPos + 1) % q.capacity;
          q.bufferedSamples--;
        }
        q.buffer[q.writePos] = sample;
        q.writePos = (q.writePos + 1) % q.capacity;
        q.bufferedSamples++;
      }
    } catch {}
  }

  dispose() {
    this.peerQueues.clear();
    try {
      this.processor?.disconnect();
      this.silentSource?.disconnect();
    } catch {}
    this.processor = null;
    this.silentSource = null;
    if (this.ctx && this.ctx.state !== 'closed') {
      this.ctx.close().catch(() => {});
    }
    this.ctx = null;
  }
}

export interface WsRelaySenderOptions {
  roomId: string;
  getWs: () => WebSocket | null;
  getVideoElement: () => HTMLVideoElement | null;
  getLocalStream: () => MediaStream | null;
  isVideoEnabled: () => boolean;
  isMicEnabled: () => boolean;
  shouldSendVideo?: () => boolean;
  shouldSendAudio?: () => boolean;
}

export class WsMediaSender {
  private opts: WsRelaySenderOptions;
  private videoTimer: number | null = null;
  private lastVideoSentAt = 0;
  private canvas: HTMLCanvasElement | null = null;
  private fallbackVideo: HTMLVideoElement | null = null;
  private audioCtx: AudioContext | null = null;
  private audioSource: MediaStreamAudioSourceNode | null = null;
  private processor: ScriptProcessorNode | null = null;
  private muteGain: GainNode | null = null;
  private attachedStreamId: string | null = null;
  private lastVoiceActivityAt = 0;
  private running = false;

  constructor(opts: WsRelaySenderOptions) {
    this.opts = opts;
  }

  start() {
    if (this.running) return;
    this.running = true;

    this.videoTimer = window.setInterval(() => {
      if (!this.running) return;
      this.maybeSendVideoFrame();
      this.ensureAudioCapture();
    }, 150);

    this.ensureAudioCapture();
  }

  resumeAudio() {
    if (this.audioCtx && this.audioCtx.state === 'suspended') {
      this.audioCtx.resume().catch(() => {});
    }
    this.ensureAudioCapture();
  }

  private resolveReadyVideoElement(): HTMLVideoElement | null {
    const domVideo = this.opts.getVideoElement();
    if (domVideo && domVideo.readyState >= 2 && domVideo.videoWidth > 0) {
      return domVideo;
    }

    const stream = this.opts.getLocalStream();
    if (!stream || stream.getVideoTracks().length === 0 || typeof document === 'undefined') {
      return null;
    }

    if (!this.fallbackVideo) {
      const vid = document.createElement('video');
      vid.muted = true;
      vid.playsInline = true;
      vid.autoplay = true;
      this.fallbackVideo = vid;
    }
    if (this.fallbackVideo.srcObject !== stream) {
      this.fallbackVideo.srcObject = stream;
      this.fallbackVideo.play().catch(() => {});
    }
    if (this.fallbackVideo.readyState >= 2 && this.fallbackVideo.videoWidth > 0) {
      return this.fallbackVideo;
    }
    return null;
  }

  private maybeSendVideoFrame() {
    if (this.opts.shouldSendVideo && !this.opts.shouldSendVideo()) return;
    if (!this.opts.isVideoEnabled()) return;

    const ws = this.opts.getWs();
    if (!ws || ws.readyState !== WebSocket.OPEN) return;

    // Prioritize audio packets: throttle or skip JPEG video frames whenever WS buffer starts backing up
    if (ws.bufferedAmount > 65_000) return;
    const now = performance.now();
    const minInterval = ws.bufferedAmount > 25_000 ? 360 : 180;
    if (now - this.lastVideoSentAt < minInterval) return;

    const videoEl = this.resolveReadyVideoElement();
    if (!videoEl) return;

    try {
      if (!this.canvas) {
        this.canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
      }
      const targetW = 320;
      const ratio = videoEl.videoHeight > 0 ? videoEl.videoHeight / videoEl.videoWidth : 0.75;
      const targetH = Math.max(180, Math.round(targetW * ratio));
      if (this.canvas.width !== targetW || this.canvas.height !== targetH) {
        this.canvas.width = targetW;
        this.canvas.height = targetH;
      }
      const ctx = this.canvas.getContext('2d');
      if (!ctx) return;
      ctx.drawImage(videoEl, 0, 0, targetW, targetH);
      const frame = this.canvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 0.52);
      this.lastVideoSentAt = now;
      ws.send(
        JSON.stringify({
          type: 'ws-video-frame',
          roomId: this.opts.roomId,
          frame,
          isVideoOn: true,
        })
      );
    } catch {}
  }

  ensureAudioCapture() {
    if (!this.running) return;
    if (this.opts.shouldSendAudio && !this.opts.shouldSendAudio()) {
      return;
    }
    const stream = this.opts.getLocalStream();
    if (!stream || stream.getAudioTracks().length === 0) return;

    if (this.attachedStreamId === stream.id && this.audioCtx) {
      if (this.audioCtx.state === 'suspended') {
        this.audioCtx.resume().catch(() => {});
      }
      return;
    }

    this.teardownAudio();
    this.attachedStreamId = stream.id;

    try {
      const AudioCtx = window.AudioContext || (window as any).webkitAudioContext;
      const ctx = new AudioCtx();
      this.audioCtx = ctx;
      if (ctx.state === 'suspended') {
        ctx.resume().catch(() => {});
      }

      const audioOnlyStream = new MediaStream(stream.getAudioTracks());
      const source = ctx.createMediaStreamSource(audioOnlyStream);
      // 4096 samples (~85ms @ 48kHz) halves WS framing overhead and dramatically improves jitter stability
      const processor = ctx.createScriptProcessor(4096, 1, 1);
      const muteGain = ctx.createGain();
      muteGain.gain.value = 0;

      const targetRate = 16000;
      const srcRate = ctx.sampleRate || 48000;
      const ratio = srcRate / targetRate;

      processor.onaudioprocess = (ev) => {
        if (!this.running || !this.opts.isMicEnabled()) return;
        if (this.opts.shouldSendAudio && !this.opts.shouldSendAudio()) return;
        const ws = this.opts.getWs();
        if (!ws || ws.readyState !== WebSocket.OPEN || ws.bufferedAmount > 280_000) return;

        const input = ev.inputBuffer.getChannelData(0);
        const outLen = Math.floor(input.length / ratio);
        if (outLen <= 0) return;

        const pcm16 = new Int16Array(outLen);
        let energySum = 0;
        let peak = 0;

        // Box-averaging anti-aliased downsample preserves natural speech timbre
        for (let i = 0; i < outLen; i++) {
          const startIdx = Math.floor(i * ratio);
          const endIdx = Math.min(input.length, Math.max(startIdx + 1, Math.floor((i + 1) * ratio)));
          let acc = 0;
          for (let j = startIdx; j < endIdx; j++) {
            acc += input[j];
          }
          const avgSample = Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, acc / (endIdx - startIdx)));
          const absVal = Math.abs(avgSample);
          energySum += absVal;
          if (absVal > peak) peak = absVal;
          pcm16[i] = avgSample < 0 ? avgSample * 0x8000 : avgSample * 0x7fff;
        }

        const nowMs = performance.now();
        const avgEnergy = energySum / outLen;
        if (avgEnergy >= 0.00025 || peak >= 0.002) {
          this.lastVoiceActivityAt = nowMs;
        }
        // Keep streaming 900ms after last speech syllable so word endings/pauses are NEVER clipped
        if (nowMs - this.lastVoiceActivityAt > 900) return;

        const b64 = uint8ToBase64(new Uint8Array(pcm16.buffer));
        ws.send(
          JSON.stringify({
            type: 'ws-audio-chunk',
            roomId: this.opts.roomId,
            pcm16: b64,
            sr: targetRate,
          })
        );
      };

      source.connect(processor);
      processor.connect(muteGain);
      muteGain.connect(ctx.destination);

      this.audioSource = source;
      this.processor = processor;
      this.muteGain = muteGain;
    } catch {}
  }

  private teardownAudio() {
    try {
      this.processor?.disconnect();
      this.audioSource?.disconnect();
      this.muteGain?.disconnect();
      if (this.audioCtx && this.audioCtx.state !== 'closed') {
        this.audioCtx.close().catch(() => {});
      }
    } catch {}
    this.processor = null;
    this.audioSource = null;
    this.muteGain = null;
    this.audioCtx = null;
    this.attachedStreamId = null;
  }

  stop() {
    this.running = false;
    if (this.videoTimer !== null) {
      clearInterval(this.videoTimer);
      this.videoTimer = null;
    }
    if (this.fallbackVideo) {
      this.fallbackVideo.srcObject = null;
      this.fallbackVideo = null;
    }
    this.teardownAudio();
  }
}
