              </div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400 shrink-0" />
                <span>ቀጥታ የHD ድምፅና ቪዲዮ</span>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Column 2: Priest Blessing Portrait */}
          <div className="lg:col-span-5 relative">
            <div className="relative rounded-2xl overflow-hidden border-2 border-amber-600/40 shadow-2xl bg-stone-900 group">
              <img
                src={priestHeroImg}
                alt="ቀሲስ ሄኖክ በመስቀል ሲባርኩ"
                decoding="async"
                className="w-full h-[380px] sm:h-[460px] object-cover object-top filter brightness-95 group-hover:scale-102 transition-transform duration-700"
              />
              <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-t from-stone-950 via-stone-950/20 to-transparent" />

              {/* Bottom Portrait Bar */}
              <div className="absolute bottom-4 left-4 right-4 p-3.5 rounded-xl bg-stone-950/85 backdrop-blur-md border border-amber-700/40">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                  <div>
                    <h4 className="text-amber-200 font-heading font-bold text-base">ቀሲስ ሄኖክ (የንስሐ አባት)</h4>
                  </div>
                  <div className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-md bg-red-950/80 border border-red-700/60 text-red-200 text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5">
                    <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-red-500 animate-pulse" />
                    <span>አገልግሎት ላይ</span>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* 4 Status & Metric Strips below hero */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-4 mt-8 pt-6 border-t border-amber-900/20">
          <div className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-stone-900/50 border border-stone-800 flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="p-2.5 rounded-lg bg-amber-950/60 border border-amber-700/40 text-amber-400">
              <Users className="w-5 h-5" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <p className="text-lg font-extrabold font-heading text-amber-200 tabular-nums">1,000+ ዝግጁ</p>
              <p className="text-xs text-stone-200 font-bold">በቀጥታ ጉባኤ ተሳታፊዎች</p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-stone-900/50 border border-stone-800 flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="p-2.5 rounded-lg bg-red-950/60 border border-red-700/40 text-red-400">
              <Shield className="w-5 h-5" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <p className="text-lg font-extrabold font-heading text-amber-200 tabular-nums">100% የተጠበቀ</p>
              <p className="text-xs text-stone-200 font-bold">የንስሐ ሚስጥር ቤት</p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-stone-900/50 border border-stone-800 flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="p-2.5 rounded-lg bg-amber-950/60 border border-amber-700/40 text-amber-400">
              <BookOpen className="w-5 h-5" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <p className="text-lg font-extrabold font-heading text-amber-200 tabular-nums">ኦፍላይን ሙሉ</p>
              <p className="text-xs text-stone-200 font-bold">የጸሎትና የቀኖና መጻሕፍት</p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-stone-900/50 border border-stone-800 flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="p-2.5 rounded-lg bg-emerald-950/60 border border-emerald-700/40 text-emerald-400">
              <HeartHandshake className="w-5 h-5" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <p className="text-lg font-extrabold font-heading text-amber-200 tabular-nums">ዘመናዊ ዋሌት</p>
              <p className="text-xs text-stone-200 font-bold">አስራት፣ በኩራትና ምጽዋት</p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
};


================================================================================
FILE: src/components/AuthProfileModal.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { X, LogIn, LogOut, Flame, Shield, AlertCircle, CheckCircle2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { UserProfile, UserRole } from '../types';
import { registerUser, loginUser, updateUserProfile, logoutUser } from '../services/api';

interface AuthProfileModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  currentUser: UserProfile;
  onUpdateUser: (updatedUser: UserProfile) => void;
}

export const AuthProfileModal: React.FC<AuthProfileModalProps> = ({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  currentUser,
  onUpdateUser,
}) => {
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<'profile' | 'signin' | 'signup'>(
    currentUser.isAuthenticated ? 'profile' : 'signin'
  );
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [errorMsg, setErrorMsg] = useState('');
  const [successMsg, setSuccessMsg] = useState('');

  const [loginIdentifier, setLoginIdentifier] = useState('');
  const [loginPassword, setLoginPassword] = useState('');

  const [formData, setFormData] = useState({
    fullName: currentUser.fullName || '',
    baptismalName: currentUser.baptismalName || '',
    phone: currentUser.phone || '',
    email: currentUser.email || '',
    password: '',
    confessorPriest: currentUser.confessorPriest || 'ቀሲስ ሄኖክ',
    confessionStage: currentUser.confessionStage || 'ጀማሪ',
    role: (currentUser.role || 'child') as UserRole,
    priestCode: '',
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isOpen) {
      setErrorMsg('');
      setSuccessMsg('');
      const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
      const inviteName = params.get('inviteName')?.trim() || '';
      const invitePhone = params.get('invitePhone')?.trim() || '';

      if (!currentUser.isAuthenticated && (inviteName || invitePhone)) {
        setMode('signup');
        setFormData((prev) => ({
          ...prev,
          fullName: inviteName || prev.fullName,
          baptismalName: inviteName || prev.baptismalName,
          phone: invitePhone || prev.phone,
        }));
        return;
      }

      setMode(currentUser.isAuthenticated ? 'profile' : 'signin');
      if (currentUser.isAuthenticated) {
        setFormData((prev) => ({
          ...prev,
          fullName: currentUser.fullName || '',
          baptismalName: currentUser.baptismalName || '',
          phone: currentUser.phone || '',
          email: currentUser.email || '',
          confessorPriest: currentUser.confessorPriest || 'ቀሲስ ሄኖክ',
          confessionStage: currentUser.confessionStage || 'ጀማሪ',
          role: currentUser.role || 'child',
        }));
      }
    }
  }, [isOpen, currentUser]);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const handleSignIn = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setErrorMsg('');
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const { user } = await loginUser(loginIdentifier, loginPassword);
      onUpdateUser(user);
      setMode('profile');
      onClose();
    } catch (err: any) {
      setErrorMsg(err.message || 'መግባት አልተቻለም። ስልክ ቁጥርዎንና የይለፍ ቃልዎን ያረጋግጡ።');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleSignUp = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setErrorMsg('');
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const { user } = await registerUser({
        fullName: formData.fullName,
        baptismalName: formData.baptismalName,
        phone: formData.phone,
        email: '',
        password: formData.password,
        role: formData.role === 'priest' ? 'priest' : formData.role === 'deacon' ? 'deacon' : 'child',
        confessorPriest: formData.confessorPriest,
        confessionStage: formData.confessionStage,
        priestCode: formData.priestCode,
      });
      onUpdateUser(user);
      setMode('profile');
      onClose();
    } catch (err: any) {
      setErrorMsg(err.message || 'ምዝገባው አልተሳካም።');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleSignOut = async () => {
    await logoutUser();
    onUpdateUser({
      id: '',
      fullName: '',
      baptismalName: '',
      phone: '',
      email: '',
      role: 'child',
      confessorPriest: 'ቀሲስ ሄኖክ',
      confessionStage: 'ጀማሪ',
      avatarSeed: '',
      isAuthenticated: false,
    });
    setLoginIdentifier('');
    setLoginPassword('');
    setMode('signin');
  };

  const handleSaveProfile = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setErrorMsg('');
    setSuccessMsg('');
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const updated = await updateUserProfile({
        fullName: formData.fullName,
        baptismalName: formData.baptismalName,
        phone: formData.phone,
        email: formData.email,
        confessorPriest: formData.confessorPriest,
        confessionStage: formData.confessionStage as any,
      });
      onUpdateUser(updated);
      setSuccessMsg('መረጃዎ በሰርቨሩ ላይ በትክክል ተቀምጧል!');
      setTimeout(() => {
        setSuccessMsg('');
        onClose();
      }, 1200);
    } catch (err: any) {
      setErrorMsg(err.message || 'መረጃውን ማደስ አልተቻለም።');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div
      onClick={(e) => {
        if (e.target === e.currentTarget) onClose();
      }}
      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 overflow-y-auto bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center p-4"
    >
      <div className="w-full max-w-lg rounded-3xl bg-stone-950 border-2 border-amber-700/50 shadow-2xl p-6 sm:p-8 space-y-5 animate-scale-up relative">
        
        <button
          onClick={onClose}
          className="absolute top-5 right-5 p-2 rounded-lg bg-stone-900 text-stone-400 hover:text-stone-100 transition"
        >
          <X className="w-5 h-5" />
        </button>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 pr-10">
          <div className="w-11 h-11 shrink-0 rounded-2xl bg-amber-950/80 border border-amber-500/50 flex items-center justify-center text-amber-300 font-bold">
            <Flame className="w-5 h-5 animate-candle text-amber-400" />
          </div>
          <div className="min-w-0">
            <h2 className="text-lg sm:text-xl font-heading font-bold text-amber-100 leading-snug">
              {mode === 'profile'
                ? 'የተጠቃሚ መንፈሳዊ ፕሮፋይል'
                : mode === 'signin'
                ? 'ወደ አካውንትዎ ይግቡ'
                : 'አዲስ አካውንት ይፍጠሩ'}
            </h2>
            <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 truncate">ቀሲስ ሄኖክ - የንስሐና የጉባኤ አገልግሎት</p>
          </div>
        </div>

        {errorMsg && (
          <div className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-red-950/70 border border-red-700/70 text-xs text-red-200 flex items-start gap-2">
            <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 text-red-400 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
            <span>{errorMsg}</span>
          </div>
        )}

        {successMsg && (
          <div className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-emerald-950/70 border border-emerald-700/70 text-xs text-emerald-200 flex items-center gap-2">
            <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-400 shrink-0" />
            <span>{successMsg}</span>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* PROFILE VIEW */}
        {mode === 'profile' && (
          <form onSubmit={handleSaveProfile} className="space-y-4 text-xs">
            <div className="p-3.5 rounded-xl bg-stone-900/60 border border-stone-800 flex items-center justify-between">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-full bg-amber-900/60 border border-amber-500/40 flex items-center justify-center text-amber-200 font-bold text-base">
                  {(currentUser.baptismalName || currentUser.fullName || 'ተ').charAt(0)}
                </div>
                <div>
                  <p className="text-sm font-bold text-amber-200">{currentUser.baptismalName}</p>
                  <p className="text-stone-400">
                    {currentUser.fullName} · {currentUser.phone}
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>
              <span className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-md bg-emerald-950 border border-emerald-700 text-emerald-300 font-medium">
                {currentUser.role === 'priest' ? 'የንስሐ አባት' : 'የንስሐ ልጅ'}
              </span>
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-stone-300 font-semibold mb-1">መጠሪያ ሙሉ ስም</label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  required
                  value={formData.fullName}
                  onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, fullName: e.target.value })}
                  className="w-full bg-stone-900 border border-stone-700 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-stone-100"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-stone-300 font-semibold mb-1">የክርስትና ስም</label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  required
                  value={formData.baptismalName}
                  onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, baptismalName: e.target.value })}
                  className="w-full bg-stone-900 border border-stone-700 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-stone-100"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-stone-300 font-semibold mb-1">ስልክ ቁጥር</label>
                <input
                  type="tel"
                  required
                  value={formData.phone}
                  onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, phone: e.target.value })}
                  className="w-full bg-stone-900 border border-stone-700 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-stone-100"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-stone-300 font-semibold mb-1">የንስሐ አባት ስም</label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={formData.confessorPriest}
                  onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, confessorPriest: e.target.value })}
                  className="w-full bg-stone-900 border border-stone-700 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-stone-100"
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-stone-300 font-semibold mb-1">የንስሐ ደረጃ</label>
              <select
                value={formData.confessionStage}
                onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, confessionStage: e.target.value as any })}
                className="w-full bg-stone-900 border border-stone-700 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-stone-100"
              >
                <option value="ጀማሪ">ጀማሪ (በንስሐ መንገድ የጀመረ)</option>
                <option value="መካከለኛ">መካከለኛ (ዘወትር የሚናዘዝ)</option>
                <option value="የበረታ/የቆረበ">የበረታ / የቆረበ (ቅዱስ ቁርባን የሚቀበል)</option>
              </select>
            </div>

            <div className="flex gap-2.5 pt-2">
              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={loading}
                className="flex-1 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-amber-900/80 hover:bg-amber-900 border border-amber-600/50 text-amber-200 font-bold transition disabled:opacity-50"
              >
                {loading ? 'በማስቀመጥ ላይ...' : 'መረጃውን አድስ (Save)'}
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleSignOut}
                className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-stone-900 hover:bg-red-950 border border-stone-700 hover:border-red-800 text-stone-300 hover:text-red-300 transition"
              >
                <LogOut className="w-4 h-4" />
                <span>ሳይን አውት</span>
              </button>
            </div>
          </form>
        )}

        {/* SIGN IN VIEW */}
        {mode === 'signin' && (
          <form onSubmit={handleSignIn} className="space-y-4 text-xs">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-stone-300 font-semibold mb-1">ስልክ ቁጥር</label>
              <input
                type="text"
                required
                value={loginIdentifier}
                onChange={(e) => setLoginIdentifier(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="0911..."
                className="w-full bg-stone-900 border border-stone-700 rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 text-stone-100 focus:outline-none focus:border-amber-500"
              />
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-stone-300 font-semibold mb-1">የይለፍ ቃል (Password)</label>
              <input
                type="password"
                required
                value={loginPassword}
                onChange={(e) => setLoginPassword(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="የይለፍ ቃልዎን ያስገቡ"
                className="w-full bg-stone-900 border border-stone-700 rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 text-stone-100 focus:outline-none focus:border-amber-500"
              />
            </div>

            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={loading}
              className="w-full py-3 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-r from-red-950 via-amber-900 to-red-950 border border-amber-600 text-amber-200 font-bold transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2 disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              <LogIn className="w-4 h-4" />
              <span>{loading ? 'በመግባት ላይ...' : 'ወደ አካውንት ግባ (Sign In)'}</span>
            </button>

            <div className="text-center pt-2">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  setErrorMsg('');
                  setMode('signup');
                }}
                className="text-amber-400 hover:underline font-medium"
              >
                አካውንት የለዎትም? አዲስ ይመዝገቡ (Sign Up)
              </button>
            </div>
          </form>
        )}

        {/* SIGN UP VIEW */}
        {mode === 'signup' && (
          <form onSubmit={handleSignUp} className="space-y-3 text-xs">
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-stone-300 font-semibold mb-1">መጠሪያ ሙሉ ስም *</label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  required
                  value={formData.fullName}
                  onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, fullName: e.target.value })}
                  placeholder="ሙሉ ስም"
                  className="w-full bg-stone-900 border border-stone-700 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-stone-100"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-stone-300 font-semibold mb-1">የክርስትና ስም *</label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  required
                  value={formData.baptismalName}
                  onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, baptismalName: e.target.value })}
                  placeholder="ለምሳሌ፡ ወልደ ዮሐንስ"
                  className="w-full bg-stone-900 border border-stone-700 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-stone-100"
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-stone-300 font-semibold mb-1">ስልክ ቁጥር *</label>
              <input
                type="tel"
                required
                value={formData.phone}
                onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, phone: e.target.value })}
                placeholder="09..."
                className="w-full bg-stone-900 border border-stone-700 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-stone-100"
              />
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-stone-300 font-semibold mb-1">የይለፍ ቃል (Password) *</label>
                <input
                  type="password"
                  required
                  minLength={4}
                  value={formData.password}
                  onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, password: e.target.value })}
                  placeholder="ቢያንስ 4 ቁጥር/ፊደል"
                  className="w-full bg-stone-900 border border-stone-700 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-stone-100"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-stone-300 font-semibold mb-1">የአገልግሎት ድርሻ</label>
                <select
                  value={formData.role}
                  onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, role: e.target.value as UserRole })}
                  className="w-full bg-stone-900 border border-stone-700 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-stone-100"
                >
                  <option value="child">የንስሐ ልጅ (ምዕመን)</option>
                  <option value="deacon">ዲያቆን / አገልጋይ</option>
                  <option value="priest">ቀሲስ (የንስሐ አባት)</option>
                </select>
              </div>
            </div>

            {formData.role === 'priest' && (
              <div className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-amber-950/30 border border-amber-700/50 space-y-1.5">
                <label className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-amber-300 font-semibold">
                  <Shield className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  <span>የቀሲስ ማረጋገጫ ሚስጥር ኮድ</span>
                </label>
                <input
                  type="password"
                  required
                  value={formData.priestCode}
                  onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, priestCode: e.target.value })}
                  placeholder="የቀሲስ ማረጋገጫ ኮድ ያስገቡ"
                  className="w-full bg-stone-900 border border-amber-700/60 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-stone-100"
                />
              </div>
            )}

            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={loading}
              className="w-full py-3 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-r from-red-950 via-amber-900 to-red-950 border border-amber-600 text-amber-200 font-bold transition disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              {loading ? 'በመመዝገብ ላይ...' : 'አካውንት ፍጠርና ተመዝገብ (Register)'}
            </button>

            <div className="text-center pt-1">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  setErrorMsg('');
                  setMode('signin');
                }}
                className="text-stone-400 hover:text-amber-300 hover:underline"
              >
                ቀደም ሲል ተመዝግበዋል? ወደ አካውንት ይግቡ
              </button>
            </div>
          </form>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef, useCallback } from 'react';
import {
  Video,
  VideoOff,
  Mic,
  MicOff,
  Hand,
  Volume2,
  VolumeX,
  Maximize2,
  Minimize2,
  Users,
  Send,
  MessageSquare,
  Share2,
  Smile,
  Shield,
  PhoneOff,

  Square,
  Pin,
  RefreshCw,
  Copy,
  Check,
  Wifi,
  AlertTriangle,
  Sparkles,
  ArrowUpCircle,
  ArrowDownCircle,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { UserProfile } from '../types';
import { fetchLiveKitToken, fetchWebRTCConfig, getRealtimeWsUrl } from '../services/api';
import { toggleSpiritualHymn, stopSpiritualHymn, playChurchBell } from '../utils/audio';
import { WsMediaAudioPlayer, WsMediaSender } from '../utils/wsMediaRelay';

interface AssemblyHallProps {
  user: UserProfile;
  onLeaveMeeting?: () => void;
}

type VideoQualityPreset = '1080p' | '720p' | '360p';

const QUALITY_MAP: Record<VideoQualityPreset, { width: number; height: number; maxBitrate: number; label: string }> = {
  '1080p': { width: 1920, height: 1080, maxBitrate: 1_800_000, label: 'Full HD 1080p' },
  '720p': { width: 1280, height: 720, maxBitrate: 750_000, label: 'HD 720p' },
  '360p': { width: 640, height: 360, maxBitrate: 350_000, label: 'ፈጣን 360p' },
};

function preferRobustOpusSdp(sdp?: string): string | undefined {
  if (!sdp) return sdp;
  const lines = sdp.split('\r\n');
  const opusPt = lines
    .map((line) => line.match(/^a=rtpmap:(\d+)\s+opus\/48000/i)?.[1])
    .find(Boolean);
  if (!opusPt) return sdp;

  const fmtpPrefix = `a=fmtp:${opusPt} `;
  let replaced = false;
  const nextLines = lines.map((line) => {
    if (line.startsWith(fmtpPrefix)) {
      replaced = true;
      let next = line;
      if (!next.includes('useinbandfec=')) next += ';useinbandfec=1';
      if (!next.includes('usedtx=')) next += ';usedtx=0';
      if (!next.includes('maxaveragebitrate=')) next += ';maxaveragebitrate=48000';
      return next;
    }
    return line;
  });
  if (!replaced) {
    const idx = nextLines.findIndex((l) => l.startsWith(`a=rtpmap:${opusPt} `));
    if (idx !== -1) {
      nextLines.splice(idx + 1, 0, `${fmtpPrefix}minptime=10;useinbandfec=1;usedtx=0;maxaveragebitrate=48000`);
    }
  }
  return nextLines.join('\r\n');
}

interface RemotePeer {
  peerId: string;
  userName: string;
  baptismalName: string;
  role: string;
  stream: MediaStream | null;
  relayFrame?: string | null;
  connectionState: string;
  isMicOn: boolean;
  isVideoOn: boolean;
  isHandRaised: boolean;
  isScreenSharing: boolean;
  onStage?: boolean;
}

interface ChatMessage {
  id: string;
  sender: string;
  text: string;
  time: string;
  isPriest?: boolean;
}

interface FloatingReaction {
  id: number;
  emoji: string;
  sender: string;
  x: number;
}

async function applyStudioEncoderTuning(pc: RTCPeerConnection, quality: VideoQualityPreset) {
  const targetBitrate = QUALITY_MAP[quality].maxBitrate;
  for (const sender of pc.getSenders()) {
    if (!sender.track) continue;
    try {
      const params = sender.getParameters();
      if (!params.encodings || params.encodings.length === 0) {
        params.encodings = [{}];
      }
      if (sender.track.kind === 'video') {
        params.encodings[0].maxBitrate = targetBitrate;
        params.encodings[0].maxFramerate = quality === '360p' ? 20 : 24;
        (params.encodings[0] as any).priority = 'low';
        (params.encodings[0] as any).networkPriority = 'low';
        (params as any).degradationPreference = 'balanced';
      } else if (sender.track.kind === 'audio') {
        params.encodings[0].maxBitrate = 48_000;
        (params.encodings[0] as any).priority = 'high';
        (params.encodings[0] as any).networkPriority = 'high';
      }
      await sender.setParameters(params);
    } catch {
      // Browser may skip unsupported RTCRtpEncodingParameters gracefully
    }
  }
}

interface PipCornerData {
  label: string;
  stream: MediaStream | null;
  relayFrame?: string | null;
  isVideoOn?: boolean;
  isMicOn?: boolean;
  mirror?: boolean;
  onClick?: () => void;
}

const PipCornerTile: React.FC<PipCornerData> = ({
  label,
  stream,
  relayFrame,
  isVideoOn = true,
  isMicOn = true,
  mirror = false,
  onClick,
}) => {
  const pipVideoRef = useRef<HTMLVideoElement | null>(null);
  const [hasFrames, setHasFrames] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const el = pipVideoRef.current;
    if (!el || !stream) {
      setHasFrames(false);
      return;
    }
    if (el.srcObject !== stream) {
      el.srcObject = stream;
    }
    el.defaultMuted = true;
    el.muted = true;
    el.play().catch(() => {});
    const check = () => setHasFrames(Boolean(el.videoWidth > 0 && el.videoHeight > 0));
    check();
    const timer = window.setInterval(check, 500);
    return () => clearInterval(timer);
  }, [stream]);

  const showStream = Boolean(
    stream &&
      (hasFrames ||
        (isVideoOn !== false && stream.getVideoTracks().some((t) => t.readyState === 'live' && !t.muted)))
  );
  const showRelay = Boolean(!showStream && relayFrame);

  return (
    <div
      onClick={(e) => {
        if (onClick) {
          e.stopPropagation();
          onClick();
        }
      }}
      className={`absolute bottom-14 right-3 z-30 w-28 sm:w-40 h-36 sm:h-48 rounded-2xl border-2 border-amber-400/90 bg-stone-950 shadow-2xl overflow-hidden flex flex-col justify-end ${
        onClick ? 'cursor-pointer hover:border-amber-300 transition' : 'pointer-events-none'
      }`}
    >
      <video
        ref={pipVideoRef}
        autoPlay
        playsInline
        muted
        onLoadedMetadata={() => {
          pipVideoRef.current?.play().catch(() => {});
          setHasFrames(Boolean(pipVideoRef.current && pipVideoRef.current.videoWidth > 0));
        }}
        onPlaying={() => setHasFrames(Boolean(pipVideoRef.current && pipVideoRef.current.videoWidth > 0))}
        className={`absolute inset-0 w-full h-full object-cover contrast-[1.03] saturate-[1.05] ${
          mirror ? 'mirror' : ''
        } ${showStream ? 'opacity-100' : 'opacity-0 pointer-events-none'}`}
      />
      {showRelay && relayFrame && (
        <img
          src={relayFrame}
          alt={label}
          className="absolute inset-0 w-full h-full object-cover contrast-[1.03] saturate-[1.05]"
        />
      )}
      {!showStream && !showRelay && (
        <div className="absolute inset-0 flex flex-col items-center justify-center bg-stone-900 text-amber-200 p-2">
          <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full bg-amber-950 border border-amber-500/60 flex items-center justify-center text-sm font-extrabold">
            {(label || 'ተ').charAt(0)}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
      <div className="relative z-10 px-2 py-1 bg-gradient-to-t from-black/95 via-black/75 to-transparent flex items-center gap-1.5">
        <span className={`w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full shrink-0 ${isMicOn ? 'bg-emerald-400' : 'bg-red-400'}`} />
        <span className="text-[10px] font-extrabold text-amber-100 truncate">{label}</span>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

// Sub-component for reliable high-res remote video/audio attachment + per-video fullscreen
const RemoteVideoTile: React.FC<{
  peer: RemotePeer;
  isGlobalAudioMuted: boolean;
  isPinned: boolean;
  isExpanded: boolean;
  onPin: (peerId: string) => void;
  onToggleExpand: (peerId: string) => void;
  onUnlockAudio?: () => void;
  onVideoHealthChange?: (peerId: string, hasDecodedFrames: boolean) => void;
  tallStage?: boolean;
  pipCorner?: PipCornerData | null;
}> = ({
  peer,
  isGlobalAudioMuted,
  isPinned,
  isExpanded,
  onPin,
  onToggleExpand,
  onUnlockAudio,
  onVideoHealthChange,
  tallStage,
  pipCorner,
}) => {
  const tileRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);
  const videoRef = useRef<HTMLVideoElement | null>(null);
  const audioRef = useRef<HTMLAudioElement | null>(null);
  const [needsAudioTap, setNeedsAudioTap] = useState(false);
  const [hasDecodedVideo, setHasDecodedVideo] = useState(false);
  const [hasLiveVideoTrack, setHasLiveVideoTrack] = useState(false);
  const [hasLiveAudioTrack, setHasLiveAudioTrack] = useState(false);

  const checkVideoFrames = useCallback(() => {
    const vid = videoRef.current;
    const ready = Boolean(vid && vid.videoWidth > 0 && vid.videoHeight > 0);
    setHasDecodedVideo((prev) => {
      if (prev !== ready) {
        onVideoHealthChange?.(peer.peerId, ready);
      }
      return ready;
    });
  }, [onVideoHealthChange, peer.peerId]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const stream = peer.stream;
    const videoEl = videoRef.current;
    const audioEl = audioRef.current;

    if (!stream) {
      setHasDecodedVideo(false);
      setHasLiveVideoTrack(false);
      setHasLiveAudioTrack(false);
      onVideoHealthChange?.(peer.peerId, false);
      return;
    }

    const updateTrackStatus = () => {
      setHasLiveVideoTrack(stream.getVideoTracks().some((t) => t.readyState === 'live' && !t.muted));
      setHasLiveAudioTrack(stream.getAudioTracks().some((t) => t.readyState === 'live' && !t.muted));
    };
    updateTrackStatus();

    const tracks = stream.getTracks();
    tracks.forEach((t) => {
      t.addEventListener('unmute', updateTrackStatus);
      t.addEventListener('mute', updateTrackStatus);
      t.addEventListener('ended', updateTrackStatus);
    });

    if (videoEl) {
      if (videoEl.srcObject !== stream) {
        videoEl.srcObject = stream;
      }
      videoEl.defaultMuted = true;
      videoEl.muted = true;
      videoEl.play().catch(() => {});
    }
    if (audioEl) {
      if (audioEl.srcObject !== stream) {
        audioEl.srcObject = stream;
      }
      audioEl.muted = isGlobalAudioMuted;
      if (!isGlobalAudioMuted && stream.getAudioTracks().length > 0) {
        audioEl
          .play()
          .then(() => setNeedsAudioTap(false))
          .catch(() => setNeedsAudioTap(true));
      } else {
        setNeedsAudioTap(false);
      }
    }

    checkVideoFrames();
    const healthPoll = window.setInterval(() => {
      updateTrackStatus();
      checkVideoFrames();
      if (videoEl && videoEl.paused && stream.getVideoTracks().length > 0) {
        videoEl.play().catch(() => {});
      }
      if (audioEl) {
        audioEl.muted = isGlobalAudioMuted;
        if (!isGlobalAudioMuted && audioEl.paused && stream.getAudioTracks().length > 0) {
          audioEl.play().then(() => setNeedsAudioTap(false)).catch(() => {});
        }
      }
    }, 600);

    return () => {
      clearInterval(healthPoll);
      tracks.forEach((t) => {
        t.removeEventListener('unmute', updateTrackStatus);
        t.removeEventListener('mute', updateTrackStatus);
        t.removeEventListener('ended', updateTrackStatus);
      });
    };
  }, [peer.stream, peer.peerId, isGlobalAudioMuted, checkVideoFrames, onVideoHealthChange]);

  const isWebRtcConnected = peer.connectionState === 'connected' || peer.connectionState === 'completed';
  // Never hide actual decoded WebRTC video pixels behind a stale boolean flag!
  const hasWebRtcVideo = Boolean(
    peer.stream && (hasDecodedVideo || (hasLiveVideoTrack && peer.isVideoOn !== false))
  );
  const hasRelayVideo = Boolean(!hasWebRtcVideo && peer.relayFrame);
  const showVisualFeed = hasWebRtcVideo || hasRelayVideo;
  const effectiveMicOn = peer.isMicOn || hasLiveAudioTrack;

  const handleTileFullscreen = async () => {
    if (document.fullscreenElement) {
      await document.exitFullscreen().catch(() => {});
      if (isExpanded) onToggleExpand(peer.peerId);
      return;
    }
    onToggleExpand(peer.peerId);
    const el = tileRef.current;
    const vid = videoRef.current as (HTMLVideoElement & { webkitEnterFullscreen?: () => void }) | null;
    if (el?.requestFullscreen) {
      el.requestFullscreen().catch(() => {});
    } else if (vid?.webkitEnterFullscreen) {
      try {
        vid.webkitEnterFullscreen();
      } catch {}
    }
  };

  const isConnected = isWebRtcConnected || Boolean(peer.relayFrame);

  return (
    <div
      ref={tileRef}
      className={`overflow-hidden bg-stone-950 border-2 transition-all shadow-2xl flex flex-col justify-between ${
        isExpanded
          ? 'fixed inset-0 z-50 rounded-none border-amber-500 h-screen w-screen'
          : tallStage
          ? 'relative rounded-2xl min-h-[420px] sm:min-h-[540px] lg:min-h-[620px]'
          : 'relative rounded-2xl min-h-[320px] sm:min-h-[400px]'
      } ${
        effectiveMicOn && isConnected
          ? 'border-emerald-500 ring-2 ring-emerald-500/40'
          : peer.isHandRaised
          ? 'border-amber-400 ring-2 ring-amber-400/40'
          : 'border-stone-800'
      }`}
    >
      {/* Dedicated audio element so audio plays even when camera is off or hidden on mobile browsers */}
      <audio ref={audioRef} autoPlay playsInline muted={isGlobalAudioMuted} />

      {/* Live WebRTC Video (never display:none, uses opacity-0 so stream never freezes) */}
      <video
        ref={videoRef}
        autoPlay
        playsInline
        muted
        onLoadedMetadata={() => {
          videoRef.current?.play().catch(() => {});
          checkVideoFrames();
        }}
        onLoadedData={checkVideoFrames}
        onPlaying={checkVideoFrames}
        onResize={checkVideoFrames}
        className={`absolute inset-0 w-full h-full object-cover contrast-[1.03] saturate-[1.06] transition-opacity ${
          hasWebRtcVideo ? 'opacity-100' : 'opacity-0 pointer-events-none'
        }`}
      />

      {/* Automatic 4G/CGNAT WebSocket Relay Video Fallback */}
      {hasRelayVideo && peer.relayFrame && (
        <img
          src={peer.relayFrame}
          alt={peer.baptismalName || peer.userName}
          className="absolute inset-0 w-full h-full object-cover contrast-[1.03] saturate-[1.06]"
        />
      )}

      {!showVisualFeed && (
        <div className="absolute inset-0 flex flex-col items-center justify-center bg-stone-900/95 text-stone-300 p-4 text-center">
          <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-full bg-amber-950 border-2 border-amber-500/60 flex items-center justify-center text-xl font-extrabold text-amber-200 mb-2">
            {(peer.baptismalName || peer.userName || 'ተ').charAt(0)}
          </div>
          <p className="text-sm font-extrabold text-amber-200">{peer.baptismalName || peer.userName}</p>
          <p className="text-xs text-stone-300 mt-1 max-w-xs leading-relaxed">
            {!peer.stream && !peer.relayFrame
              ? 'ቪዲዮና ድምፅ በመገናኘት ላይ...'
              : peer.isVideoOn === false
              ? effectiveMicOn
                ? 'ካሜራ ዘግተዋል (ድምፅ ክፍት ነው) — ምስል እንዲታይ በእሳቸው ስልክ የካሜራ ቁልፍ (📹) መከፈት አለበት'
                : 'በእሳቸው ስልክ ካሜራና ማይክ ተዘግቷል — እሳቸው ጋር «ካሜራና ማይክ ፍቀድ / ክፈት» መነካት አለበት'
              : 'የቪዲዮ ምስል ከሌላኛው ስልክ እየተቀበለ ነው...'}
          </p>
        </div>
      )}

      {needsAudioTap && peer.stream && !isGlobalAudioMuted && (
        <button
          onClick={() => {
            onUnlockAudio?.();
            videoRef.current?.play().catch(() => {});
            audioRef.current?.play().then(() => setNeedsAudioTap(false)).catch(() => {});
          }}
          className="absolute top-14 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 z-20 px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 border border-emerald-300 shadow-2xl flex items-center text-white text-xs font-extrabold gap-2 animate-pulse"
        >
          <Volume2 className="w-4 h-4" />
          <span>ድምፅ ለመስማት ይንኩ (Unmute)</span>
        </button>
      )}

      {/* Top Info */}
      <div className="relative z-10 p-3 flex items-center justify-between gap-2 bg-gradient-to-b from-black/75 via-black/30 to-transparent">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 bg-stone-950/90 backdrop-blur-md px-3 py-1 rounded-lg border border-stone-700 text-xs text-amber-200 truncate">
          <span
            className={`w-2 h-2 rounded-full shrink-0 ${
              isConnected ? 'bg-emerald-400' : 'bg-amber-400 animate-pulse'
            }`}
          />
          <span className="font-extrabold truncate">
            {peer.baptismalName || peer.userName}
            {peer.role === 'priest' ? ' (ቀሲስ)' : ''}
          </span>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0">
          {peer.isHandRaised && (
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1 bg-amber-500 text-stone-950 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-lg text-[11px] font-extrabold animate-bounce shadow">
              <Hand className="w-3 h-3" />
              <span>ሀሳብ አለኝ</span>
            </div>
          )}
          <button
            onClick={() => onPin(peer.peerId)}
            className={`p-1.5 rounded-lg border transition ${
              isPinned
                ? 'bg-amber-600 text-stone-950 border-amber-400'
                : 'bg-stone-900/90 text-stone-200 border-stone-700 hover:text-amber-200'
            }`}
            title="ዋና ስክሪን አድርግ"
          >
            <Pin className="w-4 h-4" />
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={handleTileFullscreen}
            className="p-1.5 rounded-lg bg-stone-900/90 text-amber-200 border border-amber-600/60 hover:bg-amber-700 hover:text-stone-950 transition"
            title={isExpanded ? 'ሙሉ ስክሪን ዝጋ' : 'ሙሉ ስክሪን ክፈት'}
          >
            {isExpanded ? <Minimize2 className="w-4 h-4" /> : <Maximize2 className="w-4 h-4" />}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Bottom Info */}
      <div className="relative z-10 p-3 flex items-center justify-between bg-gradient-to-t from-black/90 via-black/45 to-transparent text-xs">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <span
            className={`p-1.5 rounded-md border ${
              effectiveMicOn
                ? 'bg-emerald-950/90 border-emerald-500 text-emerald-300'
                : 'bg-red-950/90 border-red-700 text-red-300'
            }`}
          >
            {effectiveMicOn ? <Mic className="w-3.5 h-3.5 animate-pulse" /> : <MicOff className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />}
          </span>
          <span className="text-stone-200 font-bold">
            {peer.isScreenSharing ? 'ስክሪን እያጋሩ ነው' : effectiveMicOn ? 'እየተናገሩ ነው' : 'ማይክ ዝግ ነው'}
          </span>
        </div>

        <span
          className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded font-mono text-[11px] font-bold ${
            isConnected
              ? 'bg-emerald-950/90 text-emerald-300 border border-emerald-700/60'
              : 'bg-amber-950/90 text-amber-300 border border-amber-700/60'
          }`}
        >
          {hasWebRtcVideo ? 'ቀጥታ HD' : peer.relayFrame ? 'ቀጥታ 4G Relay' : isWebRtcConnected ? 'ቀጥታ ድምፅ' : peer.connectionState}
        </span>
      </div>

      {/* Picture-in-Picture corner person when zoomed/expanded or in speaker stage */}
      {pipCorner && <PipCornerTile {...pipCorner} />}
    </div>
  );
};

export const AssemblyHall: React.FC<AssemblyHallProps> = ({ user, onLeaveMeeting }) => {
  const ROOM_ID = 'assembly-zoom-main';

  const [viewMode, setViewMode] = useState<'gallery' | 'speaker'>('gallery');
