          >
            {isVideoOn ? <Video className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-400" /> : <VideoOff className="w-4 h-4 text-red-400" />}
            <span className="hidden sm:inline">{isVideoOn ? 'ካሜራ በርቷል' : 'ካሜራ ዝግ ነው'}</span>
          </button>

          <button
            onClick={() => setIsGlobalAudioMuted(!isGlobalAudioMuted)}
            className={`p-2.5 rounded-xl border transition ${
              isGlobalAudioMuted
                ? 'bg-red-950 border-red-700 text-red-300'
                : 'bg-stone-900 border-stone-700 text-stone-200 hover:text-amber-200'
            }`}
            title={isGlobalAudioMuted ? 'የተሳታፊዎችን ድምፅ ክፈት' : 'የተሳታፊዎችን ድምፅ ዝጋ'}
          >
            {isGlobalAudioMuted ? <VolumeX className="w-4 h-4" /> : <Volume2 className="w-4 h-4" />}
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <button
            onClick={handleToggleHand}
            className={`flex items-center gap-2 px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-extrabold border transition shadow-lg ${
              isHandRaised
                ? 'bg-amber-500 hover:bg-amber-400 text-stone-950 border-amber-300 animate-bounce'
                : 'bg-stone-900 hover:bg-stone-800 text-amber-200 border-amber-700/60'
            }`}
          >
            <Hand className="w-4 h-4" />
            <span>{isHandRaised ? 'እጅ ወጥቷል' : 'እጅ አውጣ'}</span>
          </button>

          <button
            onClick={handleToggleScreenShare}
            className={`flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold border transition hidden sm:flex ${
              isScreenSharing
                ? 'bg-emerald-950 text-emerald-200 border-emerald-500'
                : 'bg-stone-900 hover:bg-stone-800 text-stone-200 border-stone-700'
            }`}
          >
            <Share2 className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400" />
            <span className="hidden md:inline">{isScreenSharing ? 'ማጋራት አቁም' : 'ስክሪን አጋራ'}</span>
          </button>

          <div className="relative">
            <button
              onClick={() => setShowReactionsMenu(!showReactionsMenu)}
              className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-stone-900 hover:bg-stone-800 text-amber-200 border border-stone-700 text-xs font-bold transition"
            >
              <Smile className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400" />
              <span className="hidden md:inline">ምላሾች</span>
            </button>

            {showReactionsMenu && (
              <div className="absolute bottom-12 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 p-2 bg-stone-950 border border-amber-700/60 rounded-2xl shadow-2xl flex items-center gap-2 z-50">
                {['🙏', '✝️', '❤️', '👏', '💡'].map((emoji) => (
                  <button
                    key={emoji}
                    onClick={() => handleSendReaction(emoji)}
                    className="p-2 hover:bg-stone-900 rounded-xl transition text-2xl"
                  >
                    {emoji}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          <button
            onClick={() => toggleSpiritualHymn((playing) => setAudioHymnPlaying(playing))}
            className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2.5 rounded-xl border text-xs font-bold transition hidden lg:flex ${
              audioHymnPlaying
                ? 'bg-amber-950 border-amber-500 text-amber-200 animate-pulse'
                : 'bg-stone-900 border-stone-800 text-stone-300 hover:text-amber-200'
            }`}
          >
            <Volume2 className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400" />
            <span>{audioHymnPlaying ? 'የበገና ዜማ በርቷል' : 'የበገና ዜማ'}</span>
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <button
            onClick={() => setActiveTabPanel((prev) => (prev === 'chat' ? 'none' : 'chat'))}
            className={`p-2.5 rounded-xl border transition ${
              activeTabPanel === 'chat'
                ? 'bg-amber-900/80 text-amber-200 border-amber-500 font-bold'
                : 'bg-stone-900 text-stone-300 border-stone-700'
            }`}
            title="የቀጥታ ቻት"
          >
            <MessageSquare className="w-4 h-4" />
          </button>

          <button
            onClick={() => setActiveTabPanel((prev) => (prev === 'participants' ? 'none' : 'participants'))}
            className={`p-2.5 rounded-xl border transition ${
              activeTabPanel === 'participants'
                ? 'bg-amber-900/80 text-amber-200 border-amber-500 font-bold'
                : 'bg-stone-900 text-stone-300 border-stone-700'
            }`}
            title="ተሳታፊዎች"
          >
            <Users className="w-4 h-4" />
          </button>

          <button
            onClick={() => {
              if (onLeaveMeeting) onLeaveMeeting();
              else window.location.reload();
            }}
            className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-red-950/80 hover:bg-red-900 border border-red-700/70 text-red-200 text-xs font-extrabold transition shadow"
          >
            <PhoneOff className="w-4 h-4 text-red-300" />
            <span className="hidden sm:inline">ውጣ</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

import React, { useState, useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Shield, Lock, Video, VideoOff, Mic, MicOff, PhoneOff, Check, Sparkles, KeyRound, Flame, Volume2, RefreshCw, Maximize2, Minimize2 } from 'lucide-react';

import { playChurchBell, speakBlessingPrayer } from '../utils/audio';

import sacredSanctuaryImg from '../assets/images/sacred_confession_sanctuary_1794468348510.jpg';

function preferRobustOpusSdp(sdp?: string): string | undefined {
  if (!sdp) return sdp;
  const lines = sdp.split('\r\n');
  const opusPt = lines
    .map((line) => line.match(/^a=rtpmap:(\d+)\s+opus\/48000/i)?.[1])
    .find(Boolean);
  if (!opusPt) return sdp;

  const fmtpPrefix = `a=fmtp:${opusPt} `;
  let replaced = false;
  const nextLines = lines.map((line) => {
    if (line.startsWith(fmtpPrefix)) {
      replaced = true;
      let next = line;
      if (!next.includes('useinbandfec=')) next += ';useinbandfec=1';
      if (!next.includes('usedtx=')) next += ';usedtx=0';
      if (!next.includes('maxaveragebitrate=')) next += ';maxaveragebitrate=48000';
      return next;
    }
    return line;
  });
  if (!replaced) {
    const idx = nextLines.findIndex((l) => l.startsWith(`a=rtpmap:${opusPt} `));
    if (idx !== -1) {
      nextLines.splice(idx + 1, 0, `${fmtpPrefix}minptime=10;useinbandfec=1;usedtx=0;maxaveragebitrate=48000`);
    }
  }
  return nextLines.join('\r\n');
}

interface SecretConfessionRoomProps {

  bookings: ConfessionBooking[];
  onCompleteConfession: (bookingId: string) => void;
}

export const SecretConfessionRoom: React.FC<SecretConfessionRoomProps> = ({

  const [wsConnected, setWsConnected] = useState(false);
  const [audioBlocked, setAudioBlocked] = useState(false);

  const [activeStep, setActiveStep] = useState<1 | 2 | 3 | 4>(1);

    `secret-${user.id || 'u'}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 7)}`
  );

  const remoteVideoRef = useRef<HTMLVideoElement | null>(null);
  const remoteAudioRef = useRef<HTMLAudioElement | null>(null);
  const videoBoxRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);
  const streamRef = useRef<MediaStream | null>(null);
  const remoteStreamRef = useRef<MediaStream | null>(null);
  const pcRef = useRef<RTCPeerConnection | null>(null);
  const wsRef = useRef<WebSocket | null>(null);
  const wsAudioPlayerRef = useRef<WsMediaAudioPlayer | null>(null);
  const wsMediaSenderRef = useRef<WsMediaSender | null>(null);
  const audioContextRef = useRef<AudioContext | null>(null);
  const analyserRef = useRef<AnalyserNode | null>(null);
  const animationFrameRef = useRef<number | null>(null);
  const iceCandidatesQueue = useRef<RTCIceCandidateInit[]>([]);
  const remotePeerIdRef = useRef<string | null>(null);

  const iceStateRef = useRef<string>(iceState);
  iceStateRef.current = iceState;
  const isMicOnRef = useRef(isMicOn);
  isMicOnRef.current = isMicOn;
  const isVideoOnRef = useRef(isVideoOn);
  isVideoOnRef.current = isVideoOn;
  const wsRelayActiveRef = useRef(wsRelayActive);
  wsRelayActiveRef.current = wsRelayActive;

  videoRelayForcedRef.current = videoRelayForced;
  const audioRelayForcedRef = useRef(audioRelayForced);

  remoteVideoHasFramesRef.current = remoteVideoHasFrames;
  const remoteIsVideoOnRef = useRef(remoteIsVideoOn);
  remoteIsVideoOnRef.current = remoteIsVideoOn;

  const unlockSecretAudio = () => {
    wsAudioPlayerRef.current?.unlock().then((ok) => {
      if (ok) setAudioBlocked(false);
    });
    wsMediaSenderRef.current?.resumeAudio();
    if (audioContextRef.current && audioContextRef.current.state === 'suspended') {
      audioContextRef.current.resume().catch(() => {});
    }
    if (remoteAudioRef.current && remoteStreamRef.current) {
      remoteAudioRef.current.muted = false;
      remoteAudioRef.current.play().then(() => setAudioBlocked(false)).catch(() => {});
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    const onGesture = () => unlockSecretAudio();
    window.addEventListener('pointerdown', onGesture, { passive: true, capture: true });
    window.addEventListener('touchstart', onGesture, { passive: true, capture: true });
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('pointerdown', onGesture, true);
      window.removeEventListener('touchstart', onGesture, true);
    };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    const onFsChange = () => {
      if (!document.fullscreenElement) {
        setIsVideoFullscreen(false);
      }
    };
    document.addEventListener('fullscreenchange', onFsChange);
    return () => document.removeEventListener('fullscreenchange', onFsChange);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (remoteVideoRef.current && remoteStreamRef.current) {
      if (remoteVideoRef.current.srcObject !== remoteStreamRef.current) {
        remoteVideoRef.current.srcObject = remoteStreamRef.current;
      }
      remoteVideoRef.current.muted = true;
      remoteVideoRef.current.play().catch(() => {});
    }
    if (remoteAudioRef.current && remoteStreamRef.current) {
      if (remoteAudioRef.current.srcObject !== remoteStreamRef.current) {
        remoteAudioRef.current.srcObject = remoteStreamRef.current;
      }
      if (remoteStreamRef.current.getAudioTracks().length > 0) {
        remoteAudioRef.current
          .play()
          .then(() => setAudioBlocked(false))
          .catch(() => setAudioBlocked(true));
      }
    }
    if (localVideoRef.current && streamRef.current) {
      if (localVideoRef.current.srcObject !== streamRef.current) {
        localVideoRef.current.srcObject = streamRef.current;
      }
      localVideoRef.current.play().catch(() => {});
    }
  }, [remoteUserConnected, swapVideoFeed, isVideoOn]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isAuthenticated || !roomId) return;
    let isCancelled = false;

    const createPC = (rtcConfig: RTCConfiguration, ws: WebSocket, targetPeerId: string | null) => {
      if (pcRef.current) {
        pcRef.current.close();
      }
      const pc = new RTCPeerConnection({
        iceServers: rtcConfig.iceServers,
        iceCandidatePoolSize: 4,
      });
      pcRef.current = pc;

      const applyEncoderParams = (conn: RTCPeerConnection) => {
        try {
          conn.getSenders().forEach((sender) => {
            if (!sender.track) return;
            const params = sender.getParameters();
            if (!params.encodings || params.encodings.length === 0) {
              params.encodings = [{}];
            }
            if (sender.track.kind === 'video') {
              params.encodings[0].maxBitrate = 750_000;
              params.encodings[0].maxFramerate = 24;
              (params.encodings[0] as any).priority = 'low';
              (params.encodings[0] as any).networkPriority = 'low';
              (params as any).degradationPreference = 'balanced';
            } else if (sender.track.kind === 'audio') {
              params.encodings[0].maxBitrate = 48_000;
              (params.encodings[0] as any).priority = 'high';
              (params.encodings[0] as any).networkPriority = 'high';
            }
            sender.setParameters(params).catch(() => {});
          });
        } catch {}
      };

      const stream = streamRef.current;
      if (stream && stream.getTracks().length > 0) {
        stream.getTracks().forEach((track) => pc.addTrack(track, stream));
        if (stream.getVideoTracks().length === 0) {
          pc.addTransceiver('video', { direction: 'recvonly' });
        }
        if (stream.getAudioTracks().length === 0) {
          pc.addTransceiver('audio', { direction: 'recvonly' });
        }
      } else {
        pc.addTransceiver('video', { direction: 'recvonly' });
        pc.addTransceiver('audio', { direction: 'recvonly' });
      }

      const syncSecretRemoteTrack = (track: MediaStreamTrack) => {
        if (!remoteStreamRef.current) {
          remoteStreamRef.current = new MediaStream();
        }
        remoteStreamRef.current.getTracks().forEach((t) => {
          if (t.kind === track.kind && t.id !== track.id) {
            remoteStreamRef.current!.removeTrack(t);
          }
        });
        if (!remoteStreamRef.current.getTracks().some((t) => t.id === track.id)) {
          remoteStreamRef.current.addTrack(track);
        }
        const wrapped = new MediaStream(remoteStreamRef.current.getTracks());
        remoteStreamRef.current = wrapped;
        if (remoteVideoRef.current) {
          remoteVideoRef.current.srcObject = wrapped;
          remoteVideoRef.current.muted = true;
          remoteVideoRef.current.play().catch(() => {});
        }
        if (remoteAudioRef.current) {
          remoteAudioRef.current.srcObject = wrapped;
          if (wrapped.getAudioTracks().length > 0) {
            remoteAudioRef.current
              .play()
              .then(() => setAudioBlocked(false))
              .catch(() => setAudioBlocked(true));
          }
        }
        setRemoteUserConnected(true);
      };

      pc.ontrack = (event) => {
        syncSecretRemoteTrack(event.track);
        event.track.onunmute = () => syncSecretRemoteTrack(event.track);
      };

      pc.onicecandidate = (event) => {
        if (event.candidate && ws.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN) {
          ws.send(
            JSON.stringify({
              type: 'webrtc-signal',
              roomId,
              targetPeerId: remotePeerIdRef.current || targetPeerId,
              signal: { candidate: event.candidate },
            })
          );
        }
      };

      const handleConnState = () => {
        const state =
          pc.iceConnectionState === 'connected' || pc.iceConnectionState === 'completed'
            ? pc.iceConnectionState
            : pc.connectionState || pc.iceConnectionState;
        setIceState(state);
        if (state === 'connected' || state === 'completed') {
          applyEncoderParams(pc);
        } else if (pc.iceConnectionState === 'failed' || pc.connectionState === 'failed') {
          try {
            pc.restartIce();
          } catch {}
          setWsRelayActive(true);
        }
      };

      pc.oniceconnectionstatechange = handleConnState;
      pc.onconnectionstatechange = handleConnState;

      return pc;
    };

    const flushIce = async (pc: RTCPeerConnection) => {
      const q = iceCandidatesQueue.current;
      iceCandidatesQueue.current = [];
      for (const c of q) {
        try {
          await pc.addIceCandidate(new RTCIceCandidate(c));
        } catch {}
      }
    };

    const bootSecretCall = async () => {
      const rtcConfig = await fetchWebRTCConfig();

      let stream: MediaStream | null = null;
      if (navigator.mediaDevices?.getUserMedia) {
        const hdAudio: MediaTrackConstraints = {
          echoCancellation: true,
          noiseSuppression: true,
          autoGainControl: true,
          sampleRate: 48000,
          channelCount: 1,
        };
        const candidates: MediaStreamConstraints[] = [
          {
            video: { facingMode: 'user', width: { ideal: 1280, min: 320 }, height: { ideal: 720, min: 240 }, frameRate: { ideal: 24, max: 30 } },
            audio: hdAudio,
          },
          { video: { facingMode: 'user' }, audio: true },
          { video: true, audio: true },
          { video: true },
          { audio: true },
        ];
        for (const c of candidates) {
          try {
            stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia(c);
            break;
          } catch {}
        }
      }

      if (isCancelled) {
        stream?.getTracks().forEach((t) => t.stop());
        return;
      }

      if (stream) {
        streamRef.current = stream;
        const hasVideo = stream.getVideoTracks().length > 0;
        const hasAudio = stream.getAudioTracks().length > 0;
        isVideoOnRef.current = hasVideo;
        isMicOnRef.current = hasAudio;
        setIsVideoOn(hasVideo);
        setIsMicOn(hasAudio);
        setMediaPermissionMsg(hasVideo ? null : 'ካሜራው አልተፈቀደም፤ ከታች «ካሜራ ፍቀድ» የሚለውን ይንኩ።');
        if (localVideoRef.current) {
          localVideoRef.current.srcObject = stream;
          localVideoRef.current.play().catch(() => {});
        }

        if (hasAudio) {
          try {
            const AudioCtx = window.AudioContext || (window as any).webkitAudioContext;
            const ctx = new AudioCtx();
            audioContextRef.current = ctx;
            const source = ctx.createMediaStreamSource(stream);
            const analyser = ctx.createAnalyser();
            analyser.fftSize = 64;
            analyserRef.current = analyser;
            source.connect(analyser);

            const dataArray = new Uint8Array(analyser.frequencyBinCount);
            const updateVolume = () => {
              if (isCancelled) return;
              if (analyserRef.current && isMicOnRef.current) {
                analyserRef.current.getByteFrequencyData(dataArray);
                let sum = 0;
                for (let i = 0; i < dataArray.length; i++) sum += dataArray[i];
                setMicVolumeLevel(Math.min(100, Math.round((sum / dataArray.length / 128) * 100)));
              } else {
                setMicVolumeLevel(0);
              }
              animationFrameRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(updateVolume);
            };
            updateVolume();
          } catch {}
        }
      } else {
        isVideoOnRef.current = false;
        isMicOnRef.current = false;
        setIsVideoOn(false);
        setIsMicOn(false);
        setMediaPermissionMsg('የቪዲዮ ካሜራ ፍቃድ አልተሰጠም፤ «ካሜራና ማይክ ፍቀድ» የሚለውን ይጫኑ።');
      }

      if (!wsAudioPlayerRef.current) {
        wsAudioPlayerRef.current = new WsMediaAudioPlayer((blocked) => setAudioBlocked(blocked));
      }

      let reconnectTimer: number | null = null;
      let keepaliveTimer: number | null = null;

      const connectSecretWs = () => {
        if (isCancelled) return;
        const ws = new WebSocket(getRealtimeWsUrl());
        wsRef.current = ws;

        ws.onopen = () => {
          if (isCancelled) {
            ws.close();
            return;
          }
          setWsConnected(true);
          ws.send(
            JSON.stringify({
              type: 'join-room',
              roomId,
              userId: selfPeerIdRef.current,
              userName: user.fullName,
              baptismalName: user.baptismalName || user.fullName,
              role: user.role === 'priest' ? 'priest' : 'child',
              isMicOn: isMicOnRef.current && Boolean(streamRef.current?.getAudioTracks().length),
              isVideoOn: Boolean(streamRef.current?.getVideoTracks().length),
            })
          );
          if (keepaliveTimer) clearInterval(keepaliveTimer);
          keepaliveTimer = window.setInterval(() => {
            if (ws.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN) {
              ws.send(JSON.stringify({ type: 'ws-ping' }));
            }
          }, 15000);
        };

        ws.onclose = () => {
          setWsConnected(false);
          if (keepaliveTimer) clearInterval(keepaliveTimer);
          if (!isCancelled) {
            reconnectTimer = window.setTimeout(connectSecretWs, 2500);
          }
        };

        ws.onmessage = async (event) => {
          try {
            const data = JSON.parse(event.data);

            if (data.type === 'ws-pong') {
              return;
            } else if (data.type === 'request-video-relay') {
              if (!data.senderPeerId || data.senderPeerId === selfPeerIdRef.current) return;
              setVideoRelayForced(true);
              return;
            } else if (data.type === 'request-audio-relay') {
              if (!data.senderPeerId || data.senderPeerId === selfPeerIdRef.current) return;
              setAudioRelayForced(true);
              return;
            } else if (data.type === 'ws-video-frame') {
              if (!data.senderPeerId || data.senderPeerId === selfPeerIdRef.current) return;
              setRemoteRelayFrame(data.frame);
              setRemoteIsVideoOn(data.isVideoOn !== false);
              if (data.senderName) setRemoteUserName(data.senderName);
              setRemoteUserConnected(true);
              return;
            } else if (data.type === 'ws-audio-chunk') {
              if (!data.senderPeerId || data.senderPeerId === selfPeerIdRef.current) return;
              const hasVerifiedWebRtcAudio =
                (iceStateRef.current === 'connected' || iceStateRef.current === 'completed') &&
                webrtcAudioFlowingRef.current &&
                Boolean(remoteStreamRef.current?.getAudioTracks().some((t) => t.readyState === 'live' && !t.muted));
              if (!hasVerifiedWebRtcAudio) {
                wsAudioPlayerRef.current?.playChunk(data.senderPeerId, data.pcm16, data.sr || 16000, false);
              }
              return;
            }

            if (data.type === 'room-state' && Array.isArray(data.existingPeers) && data.existingPeers.length > 0) {
              const target = data.existingPeers[0];
              if (target.peerId === selfPeerIdRef.current) return;
              remotePeerIdRef.current = target.peerId;
              webrtcAudioFlowingRef.current = false;
              inboundAudioStatsRef.current = { bytes: 0, stalledChecks: 0 };
              setRemoteUserName(target.baptismalName || target.userName);
              setRemoteIsVideoOn(target.isVideoOn !== false);
              const pc = createPC(rtcConfig, ws, target.peerId);
              const offer = await pc.createOffer();
              const tunedSdp = preferRobustOpusSdp(offer.sdp);
              const tunedOffer = tunedSdp ? { type: offer.type, sdp: tunedSdp } : offer;
              await pc.setLocalDescription(tunedOffer);
              ws.send(
                JSON.stringify({
                  type: 'webrtc-signal',
                  roomId,
                  targetPeerId: target.peerId,
                  signal: { sdp: tunedOffer },
                })
              );
            } else if (data.type === 'peer-joined' && data.peer) {
              if (data.peer.peerId === selfPeerIdRef.current) return;
              remotePeerIdRef.current = data.peer.peerId;
              webrtcAudioFlowingRef.current = false;
              inboundAudioStatsRef.current = { bytes: 0, stalledChecks: 0 };
              setRemoteUserName(data.peer.baptismalName || data.peer.userName);
              setRemoteIsVideoOn(data.peer.isVideoOn !== false);
            } else if (data.type === 'peer-media-state' && data.peerId !== selfPeerIdRef.current) {
              setRemoteIsVideoOn(data.isVideoOn !== false);
              if (data.isVideoOn === false) {
                setRemoteRelayFrame(null);
              }
            } else if (data.type === 'webrtc-signal' && data.signal) {
              if (data.senderPeerId === selfPeerIdRef.current) return;
              if (data.senderPeerId) {
                remotePeerIdRef.current = data.senderPeerId;
              }
              if (data.senderName) {
                setRemoteUserName(data.senderName);
              }

              let pc = pcRef.current;
              if (!pc || pc.signalingState === 'closed') {
                pc = createPC(rtcConfig, ws, data.senderPeerId || null);
              }

              const { signal } = data;
              if (signal.sdp) {
                const isOffer = signal.sdp.type === 'offer';
                const polite = selfPeerIdRef.current < (data.senderPeerId || '');
                const tunedRemoteSdp = preferRobustOpusSdp(signal.sdp.sdp);
                const remoteDesc = tunedRemoteSdp ? { type: signal.sdp.type, sdp: tunedRemoteSdp } : signal.sdp;
                if (isOffer && pc.signalingState !== 'stable') {
                  if (!polite) return;
                  await Promise.all([
                    pc.setLocalDescription({ type: 'rollback' }),
                    pc.setRemoteDescription(new RTCSessionDescription(remoteDesc)),
                  ]);
                } else {
                  await pc.setRemoteDescription(new RTCSessionDescription(remoteDesc));
                }
                await flushIce(pc);
                if (isOffer) {
                  const answer = await pc.createAnswer();
                  const tunedAnswerSdp = preferRobustOpusSdp(answer.sdp);
                  const tunedAnswer = tunedAnswerSdp ? { type: answer.type, sdp: tunedAnswerSdp } : answer;
                  await pc.setLocalDescription(tunedAnswer);
                  ws.send(
                    JSON.stringify({
                      type: 'webrtc-signal',
                      roomId,
                      targetPeerId: data.senderPeerId,
                      signal: { sdp: tunedAnswer },
                    })
                  );
                }
              } else if (signal.candidate) {
                if (pc.remoteDescription && pc.remoteDescription.type) {
                  await pc.addIceCandidate(new RTCIceCandidate(signal.candidate));
                } else {
                  iceCandidatesQueue.current.push(signal.candidate);
                }
              }
            } else if (data.type === 'peer-left') {
              setRemoteUserConnected(false);
              setRemoteUserName('');
              setRemoteRelayFrame(null);
              setRemoteVideoHasFrames(false);
              setRemoteIsVideoOn(true);
              setVideoRelayForced(false);
              setAudioRelayForced(false);
              webrtcAudioFlowingRef.current = false;
              inboundAudioStatsRef.current = { bytes: 0, stalledChecks: 0 };
              setIceState('waiting');
              remotePeerIdRef.current = null;
              remoteStreamRef.current = null;
              if (pcRef.current) {
                pcRef.current.close();
                pcRef.current = null;
              }
            }
          } catch (err) {
            console.warn('Secret room signaling error:', err);
          }
        };
      };

      connectSecretWs();

      return () => {
        if (reconnectTimer) clearTimeout(reconnectTimer);
        if (keepaliveTimer) clearInterval(keepaliveTimer);
      };
    };

    let cleanupWsTimers: (() => void) | undefined;
    bootSecretCall().then((fn) => {
      if (typeof fn === 'function') cleanupWsTimers = fn;
    });

    return () => {
      isCancelled = true;
      cleanupWsTimers?.();
      wsMediaSenderRef.current?.stop();
      wsMediaSenderRef.current = null;
      wsAudioPlayerRef.current?.dispose();
      wsAudioPlayerRef.current = null;
      if (wsRef.current && wsRef.current.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN) {
        wsRef.current.send(JSON.stringify({ type: 'leave-room' }));
        wsRef.current.close();
      }
      if (pcRef.current) {
        pcRef.current.close();
        pcRef.current = null;
      }
      if (streamRef.current) {
        streamRef.current.getTracks().forEach((t) => t.stop());
      }
      if (audioContextRef.current && audioContextRef.current.state !== 'closed') {
        audioContextRef.current.close().catch(() => {});
      }
      if (animationFrameRef.current) {
        cancelAnimationFrame(animationFrameRef.current);
      }
    };
  }, [isAuthenticated, roomId, user.id, user.fullName, user.baptismalName, user.role]);

  // Automatic 4.5s WebSocket Relay Fallback in 1-on-1 Confession Room if 4G CGNAT blocks WebRTC ICE
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isAuthenticated || !wsConnected) {
      setWsRelayActive(false);
      setVideoRelayForced(false);
      setAudioRelayForced(false);
      return;
    }
    if (iceState === 'connected' || iceState === 'completed') {
      setWsRelayActive(false);
      return;
    }
    if (iceState === 'failed') {
      setWsRelayActive(true);
      return;
    }
    const timer = window.setTimeout(() => {
      setWsRelayActive(true);
    }, 4500);
    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [isAuthenticated, wsConnected, iceState]);

  // Monitor decoded WebRTC video frames AND real audio RTP byte counters in Secret Room
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isAuthenticated || !wsConnected || !remoteUserConnected) return;

    const healthCheck = window.setInterval(() => {
      const vid = remoteVideoRef.current;
      const ready = Boolean(vid && vid.videoWidth > 0 && vid.videoHeight > 0);
      setRemoteVideoHasFrames(ready);
      if (vid && vid.paused && remoteStreamRef.current?.getVideoTracks().length) {
        vid.play().catch(() => {});
      }
      if (remoteAudioRef.current && remoteAudioRef.current.paused && remoteStreamRef.current?.getAudioTracks().length) {
        remoteAudioRef.current.play().then(() => setAudioBlocked(false)).catch(() => {});
      }
    }, 600);

    const checkMediaStats = async () => {
      const isConn = iceStateRef.current === 'connected' || iceStateRef.current === 'completed';
      if (!isConn) return;

      if (
        !remoteVideoHasFramesRef.current &&
        remoteIsVideoOnRef.current &&
        wsRef.current?.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN &&
        remotePeerIdRef.current
      ) {
        wsRef.current.send(
          JSON.stringify({
            type: 'request-video-relay',
            roomId,
            targetPeerId: remotePeerIdRef.current,
          })
        );
      }

      if (pcRef.current) {
        try {
          const stats = await pcRef.current.getStats();
          let inboundAudioBytes = 0;
          stats.forEach((report) => {
            if (report.type === 'inbound-rtp' && report.kind === 'audio') {
              inboundAudioBytes += Number(report.bytesReceived || 0);
            }
          });
          const prev = inboundAudioStatsRef.current;
          if (inboundAudioBytes > prev.bytes) {
            webrtcAudioFlowingRef.current = true;
            inboundAudioStatsRef.current = { bytes: inboundAudioBytes, stalledChecks: 0 };
          } else {
            const nextStalls = prev.stalledChecks + 1;
            inboundAudioStatsRef.current = { bytes: inboundAudioBytes, stalledChecks: nextStalls };
            if ((inboundAudioBytes === 0 || nextStalls >= 2) && wsRef.current?.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN && remotePeerIdRef.current) {
              webrtcAudioFlowingRef.current = false;
              wsRef.current.send(
                JSON.stringify({
                  type: 'request-audio-relay',
                  roomId,
                  targetPeerId: remotePeerIdRef.current,
                })
              );
            }
          }

          if (isMicOnRef.current) {
            let audioFound = false;
            let packetsSent = 0;
            stats.forEach((report) => {
              if (report.type === 'outbound-rtp' && report.kind === 'audio') {
                audioFound = true;
                packetsSent += Number(report.packetsSent || 0);
              }
            });
            if (!audioFound || packetsSent === 0) {
              setAudioRelayForced(true);
            }
          }

          if (isVideoOnRef.current) {
            let found = false;
            let framesSent = 0;
            stats.forEach((report) => {
              if (report.type === 'outbound-rtp' && report.kind === 'video') {
                found = true;
                framesSent += Number(report.framesSent || 0);
              }
            });
            if (!found || framesSent === 0) {
              setVideoRelayForced(true);
            }
          }
        } catch {}
      }
    };

    const initialStatsTimer = window.setTimeout(checkMediaStats, 2500);
    const intervalStatsTimer = window.setInterval(checkMediaStats, 3500);

    return () => {
      clearInterval(healthCheck);
      clearTimeout(initialStatsTimer);
      clearInterval(intervalStatsTimer);
    };
  }, [isAuthenticated, wsConnected, remoteUserConnected, roomId]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const needsSender = (wsRelayActive || videoRelayForced || audioRelayForced) && wsConnected && Boolean(roomId);
    if (!needsSender) {
      wsMediaSenderRef.current?.stop();
      wsMediaSenderRef.current = null;
      return;
    }
    const sender = new WsMediaSender({
      roomId,
      getWs: () => wsRef.current,
      getVideoElement: () => localVideoRef.current,
      getLocalStream: () => streamRef.current,
      isVideoEnabled: () => isVideoOnRef.current,
      isMicEnabled: () => isMicOnRef.current,
      shouldSendVideo: () => (wsRelayActiveRef.current || videoRelayForcedRef.current) && isVideoOnRef.current,
      shouldSendAudio: () => (wsRelayActiveRef.current || audioRelayForcedRef.current) && isMicOnRef.current,
    });
    wsMediaSenderRef.current = sender;
    sender.start();
    return () => {
      sender.stop();
      if (wsMediaSenderRef.current === sender) {
        wsMediaSenderRef.current = null;
      }
    };
  }, [wsRelayActive, videoRelayForced, audioRelayForced, wsConnected, roomId]);

  const handleRequestSecretMedia = async () => {
    if (!navigator.mediaDevices?.getUserMedia) {
      setMediaPermissionMsg('ካሜራ ለመክፈት HTTPS ደህንነቱ የተጠበቀ ግንኙነት ያስፈልጋል።');
      return;
    }
    setRequestingMedia(true);
    try {
      const candidates: MediaStreamConstraints[] = [
        { video: { facingMode, width: { ideal: 1280 }, height: { ideal: 720 } }, audio: true },
        { video: { facingMode }, audio: true },
        { video: true, audio: true },
        { video: true },
      ];
      let stream: MediaStream | null = null;
      let lastErr: any = null;
      for (const c of candidates) {
        try {
          stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia(c);
          break;
        } catch (err) {
          lastErr = err;
        }
      }

      if (!stream) {
        if (lastErr?.name === 'NotAllowedError' || lastErr?.name === 'PermissionDeniedError') {
          setMediaPermissionMsg('የብራውዘር ካሜራ ፍቃድ ተከልክሏል። ከላይ 🔒 (Settings) ላይ Camera → Allow ያድርጉ።');
        } else {
          setMediaPermissionMsg('ካሜራ መክፈት አልተቻለም፤ ካሜራው በሌላ መተግበሪያ ክፍት አለመሆኑን ያረጋግጡ።');
        }
        return;
      }

      streamRef.current?.getTracks().forEach((t) => t.stop());
      streamRef.current = stream;
      if (localVideoRef.current) {
        localVideoRef.current.srcObject = stream;
        localVideoRef.current.play().catch(() => {});
      }
      const hasVideo = stream.getVideoTracks().length > 0;
      const hasAudio = stream.getAudioTracks().length > 0;
      isVideoOnRef.current = hasVideo;
      isMicOnRef.current = hasAudio;
      setIsVideoOn(hasVideo);
      setIsMicOn(hasAudio);
      setMediaPermissionMsg(null);

      if (pcRef.current) {
        for (const track of stream.getTracks()) {
          const matchingTransceiver = pcRef.current.getTransceivers().find(
            (tr) => tr.sender?.track?.kind === track.kind || tr.receiver?.track?.kind === track.kind
          );
          if (matchingTransceiver) {
            try {
              matchingTransceiver.direction = 'sendrecv';
              await matchingTransceiver.sender.replaceTrack(track);
            } catch {}
          } else {
            pcRef.current.addTrack(track, stream);
          }
        }
        if (pcRef.current.signalingState === 'stable') {
          try {
            const offer = await pcRef.current.createOffer();
            await pcRef.current.setLocalDescription(offer);
            if (wsRef.current?.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN) {
              wsRef.current.send(
                JSON.stringify({
                  type: 'webrtc-signal',
                  roomId,
                  targetPeerId: remotePeerIdRef.current,
                  signal: { sdp: offer },
                })
              );
            }
          } catch {}
        }
      }
      if (wsRef.current?.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN) {
        wsRef.current.send(
          JSON.stringify({
            type: 'room-media-state',
            isMicOn: hasAudio,
            isVideoOn: hasVideo,
          })
        );
      }
      wsMediaSenderRef.current?.ensureAudioCapture();
      unlockSecretAudio();
    } finally {
      setRequestingMedia(false);
    }
  };

  // Toggle Video Track (auto-requests camera permission if none active)
  const handleToggleVideo = async () => {
    unlockSecretAudio();
    const tracks = streamRef.current?.getVideoTracks() || [];
    if (tracks.length === 0) {
      await handleRequestSecretMedia();
      return;
    }
    const newState = !isVideoOn;
    isVideoOnRef.current = newState;
    setIsVideoOn(newState);
    tracks.forEach((track) => {
      track.enabled = newState;
    });
    if (wsRef.current?.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN) {
      wsRef.current.send(
        JSON.stringify({
          type: 'room-media-state',
          isMicOn: isMicOnRef.current,
          isVideoOn: newState,
        })
      );
    }
  };

  // Toggle Audio Track
  const handleToggleMic = async () => {
    unlockSecretAudio();
    const tracks = streamRef.current?.getAudioTracks() || [];
    if (tracks.length === 0) {
      await handleRequestSecretMedia();
      return;
    }
    const newState = !isMicOn;
    isMicOnRef.current = newState;
    setIsMicOn(newState);
    tracks.forEach((track) => {
      track.enabled = newState;
    });
    if (!newState) setMicVolumeLevel(0);
    if (wsRef.current?.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN) {
      wsRef.current.send(
        JSON.stringify({
          type: 'room-media-state',
          isMicOn: newState,
          isVideoOn: isVideoOnRef.current,
        })
      );
    }
  };

  // Seamless HD camera flip without tearing down call
  const handleSwitchCamera = async () => {
    const nextFacing = facingMode === 'user' ? 'environment' : 'user';
    try {
      const newCamStream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({
        video: {
          facingMode: nextFacing,
          width: { ideal: 1920, min: 640 },
          height: { ideal: 1080, min: 480 },
          frameRate: { ideal: 30 },
        },
      });
      const newTrack = newCamStream.getVideoTracks()[0];
      const oldTrack = streamRef.current?.getVideoTracks()[0];
      if (streamRef.current && newTrack) {
        if (oldTrack) {
          streamRef.current.removeTrack(oldTrack);
          oldTrack.stop();
        }
        streamRef.current.addTrack(newTrack);
        if (localVideoRef.current) {
          localVideoRef.current.srcObject = streamRef.current;
        }
        const sender = pcRef.current?.getSenders().find((s) => s.track?.kind === 'video');
        if (sender) {
          sender.replaceTrack(newTrack).catch(() => {});
        }
        setFacingMode(nextFacing);
      }
    } catch {
      alert('ካሜራ መቀየር አልተቻለም።');
    }
  };

  const handleToggleFullscreen = async () => {
    const next = !isVideoFullscreen;
    setIsVideoFullscreen(next);
    const el = videoBoxRef.current;
    try {
      if (next && el?.requestFullscreen) {
        await el.requestFullscreen();
      } else if (!next && document.fullscreenElement) {
        await document.exitFullscreen();
      }
    } catch {
      // Fixed CSS full-viewport fallback handles iOS Safari gracefully
    }
  };

  const handleUnlockRoom = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setErrorMsg('');
    try {
      const result = await verifySecretRoomPasscode(passcode);
      if (result.authorized && result.roomId) {
        setRoomId(result.roomId);
        setIsAuthenticated(true);
        playChurchBell();
      } else {
        setErrorMsg(result.error || 'የተሳሳተ የሚስጥር ቁጥር! ቢያንስ 3 አሃዝ የጋራ ሚስጥር ኮድ ያስገቡ።');
      }
    } catch {
      setErrorMsg('ከሰርቨር ጋር መገናኘት አልተቻለም።');
    }
  };

  const handleGrantAbsolution = () => {
    setIsBlessed(true);
    playChurchBell();
    speakBlessingPrayer(
      `በስመ አብ ወወልድ ወመንፈስ ቅዱስ አሐዱ አምላክ አሜን። ልጄ ${user.baptismalName || ''}፣ እግዚአብሔር አምላክ ኃጢአትህን ይደምስስልህ። በሰላም ሂድ፤ በረከቱ ይደርብህ!`
    );

    try {
      confetti({
        particleCount: 140,
        spread: 85,
        origin: { y: 0.6 },
        colors: ['#FFE885', '#D4AF37', '#800020', '#FFFFFF', '#10B981'],
      });
    } catch {}

    if (bookings.length > 0) {
      onCompleteConfession(bookings[0].id);
    }
  };

  const handleBurnConfessionNotes = () => {
    setConfessionNotes('');
    alert('የተናዘዙት ማስታወሻ በእሳት እንደተቃጠለ በንስሐ አባትዎ ፊት ተሰርዟል፤ በምስጢር ተደምስሷል!');
  };

  // Locked Gate Screen
  if (!isAuthenticated) {
    return (
      <div className="max-w-4xl mx-auto px-4 py-12">
        <div className="p-8 sm:p-12 rounded-3xl bg-gradient-to-b from-stone-900 via-stone-950 to-black border-2 border-red-900/50 shadow-2xl text-center space-y-6 relative overflow-hidden">
          
          <div className="absolute top-0 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 w-96 h-96 bg-red-950/30 rounded-full blur-3xl pointer-events-none" />

          <div className="w-20 h-20 mx-auto rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-br from-red-950 to-stone-900 border-2 border-amber-600/60 flex items-center justify-center text-amber-400 shadow-xl">
            <Lock className="w-10 h-10" />
          </div>

          <div className="space-y-2">
            <h2 className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-heading font-bold text-amber-100">
              የንስሐ አባትና ልጅ ብቻ የሚስጥር ቤት
            </h2>
          </div>

          <div className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-stone-950/80 border border-amber-900/40 text-xs text-amber-200/90 max-w-md mx-auto space-y-1">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 font-semibold text-amber-300">
              <Shield className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-400" />
              <span>የቀኖና እና የመጽሐፈ ንስሐ ጥብቅ ሚስጥር (WebRTC P2P Encrypted)</span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <form onSubmit={handleUnlockRoom} className="max-w-xs mx-auto space-y-4 pt-2">
            <div className="relative">
              <KeyRound className="w-5 h-5 absolute left-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-amber-400" />
              <input
                type="password"
                maxLength={8}
                value={passcode}
                onChange={(e) => setPasscode(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="የጋራ የሚስጥር ቁጥርዎን ያስገቡ"
                className="w-full bg-stone-900 border border-amber-700/60 rounded-xl pl-11 pr-4 py-3 text-center text-lg font-mono tracking-widest text-amber-200 placeholder-stone-500 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-500"
              />
            </div>

            {errorMsg && (
              <p className="text-xs text-red-400 bg-red-950/40 p-2 rounded-lg border border-red-800/40">
                {errorMsg}
              </p>
            )}

            <button
              type="submit"
              className="w-full py-3.5 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-r from-red-900 via-amber-900 to-red-900 border border-amber-500/50 text-white font-bold text-sm hover:from-red-800 hover:to-amber-800 shadow-xl transition-all hover:scale-[1.01]"
            >
              በበረከት ወደ ሚስጥር ቤቱ ግባ
            </button>
          </form>

        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  // Active Inside Sanctuary Screen
  return (
    <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-8 space-y-6">
      
      {/* Top Security Banner */}
      <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-3 p-3.5 sm:p-4 rounded-xl bg-stone-950 border border-red-900/40">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 min-w-0">
          <div className="p-2 rounded-lg bg-red-950/80 text-red-400 shrink-0">
            <Shield className="w-5 h-5 text-emerald-400" />
          </div>
          <h3 className="text-sm font-bold font-heading text-amber-200 truncate">
            የንስሐ ሚስጥር ቤት — የቀሲስ ሄኖክ እና የ{user.baptismalName} 1-ለ-1 ቪዲዮ
          </h3>
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
          {/* Real Audio Volume Visualizer Meter */}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-stone-900 border border-stone-800 text-xs shrink-0">
            <Volume2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-400" />
            <div className="w-14 h-2 bg-stone-950 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
              <div
                className="h-full bg-emerald-400 transition-all duration-75"
                style={{ width: `${micVolumeLevel}%` }}
              />
            </div>
            <span className="font-mono text-stone-300 text-xs">{micVolumeLevel}%</span>
          </div>

          {/* Real Peer-to-Peer Connection Status */}
          <span className={`px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg border text-xs flex items-center gap-1.5 ${
            remoteUserConnected
              ? 'bg-emerald-950/80 border-emerald-500 text-emerald-200'
              : 'bg-amber-950/60 border-amber-700/50 text-amber-200'
          }`}>
            <span className={`w-2 h-2 rounded-full shrink-0 ${remoteUserConnected ? 'bg-emerald-400 animate-pulse' : 'bg-amber-400'}`} />
            <span>
              {remoteUserConnected
                ? `ቀጥታ ከ${remoteUserName} ጋር ተገናኝቷል`
                : 'ሌላኛውን ሰው በመጠባበቅ ላይ...'}
            </span>
          </span>

          <button
            onClick={() => {
              if (streamRef.current) streamRef.current.getTracks().forEach((t) => t.stop());
              if (pcRef.current) pcRef.current.close();
              if (wsRef.current) wsRef.current.close();
              setIsAuthenticated(false);
            }}
            className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-red-950/80 border border-red-700/70 text-red-200 text-xs hover:bg-red-900 transition shrink-0"
          >
            <PhoneOff className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            <span>ከቤቱ ውጣ</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Video & Ritual Workspace Grid */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-6">
        
        {/* Left: Unobstructed 1-on-1 Real HD Video Interface */}
        <div className="lg:col-span-7 space-y-3">
          
          <div
            ref={videoBoxRef}
            className={`overflow-hidden bg-black border-2 border-amber-700/50 shadow-2xl ${
              isVideoFullscreen
                ? 'fixed inset-0 z-50 h-screen w-screen rounded-none'
                : 'relative rounded-2xl h-[460px] sm:h-[580px]'
            }`}
          >
            {/* Dedicated remote audio element so audio never stops even when video swaps or camera hides */}
            <audio ref={remoteAudioRef} autoPlay playsInline />

            {/* Remote Video Layer (never display:none, uses opacity-0 so video stream never freezes) */}
            <video
              ref={remoteVideoRef}
              autoPlay
              playsInline
              muted
              onLoadedMetadata={() => {
                remoteVideoRef.current?.play().catch(() => {});
                setRemoteVideoHasFrames(
                  Boolean(remoteVideoRef.current && remoteVideoRef.current.videoWidth > 0)
                );
              }}
              onLoadedData={() =>
                setRemoteVideoHasFrames(
                  Boolean(remoteVideoRef.current && remoteVideoRef.current.videoWidth > 0)
                )
              }
              onPlaying={() =>
                setRemoteVideoHasFrames(
                  Boolean(remoteVideoRef.current && remoteVideoRef.current.videoWidth > 0)
                )
              }
              onResize={() =>
                setRemoteVideoHasFrames(
                  Boolean(remoteVideoRef.current && remoteVideoRef.current.videoWidth > 0)
                )
              }
              onClick={() => {
                if (remoteUserConnected && swapVideoFeed) setSwapVideoFeed(false);
              }}
              className={`object-cover contrast-[1.03] saturate-[1.06] ${
                !remoteUserConnected || !remoteVideoHasFrames
                  ? 'opacity-0 pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 w-full h-full'
                  : swapVideoFeed
                  ? 'absolute bottom-3 right-3 w-32 sm:w-44 h-44 sm:h-52 rounded-2xl border-2 border-amber-400 shadow-2xl z-20 cursor-pointer'
                  : 'w-full h-full block'
              }`}
            />

            {/* 4G/CGNAT WebSocket Live Relay Fallback Video Image */}
            {remoteUserConnected && !remoteVideoHasFrames && remoteRelayFrame && (
              <img
                src={remoteRelayFrame}
                alt={remoteUserName || 'ተሳታፊ'}
                onClick={() => {
                  if (swapVideoFeed) setSwapVideoFeed(false);
                }}
                className={`object-cover contrast-[1.03] saturate-[1.06] ${
                  swapVideoFeed
                    ? 'absolute bottom-3 right-3 w-32 sm:w-44 h-44 sm:h-52 rounded-2xl border-2 border-amber-400 shadow-2xl z-20 cursor-pointer'
                    : 'w-full h-full block'
                }`}
              />
            )}

            {/* Honest Remote Placeholder while connecting or when remote camera is off */}
            {remoteUserConnected && !remoteVideoHasFrames && !remoteRelayFrame && !swapVideoFeed && (
              <div className="absolute inset-0 flex flex-col items-center justify-center bg-stone-950/95 text-stone-200 p-6 text-center space-y-2">
                <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-full bg-amber-950 border-2 border-amber-500/60 flex items-center justify-center text-2xl font-extrabold text-amber-200">
                  {(remoteUserName || 'ተ').charAt(0)}
                </div>
                <p className="text-base font-extrabold text-amber-200">{remoteUserName || 'ተሳታፊ'}</p>
                <p className="text-xs text-stone-300 max-w-xs">
                  {remoteIsVideoOn
                    ? 'የቪዲዮ ምስል ከሌላኛው ስልክ በመቀበል ላይ...'
                    : 'ሌላኛው ተሳታፊ ካሜራውን ዘግቷል (ድምፅ ክፍት ነው)'}
                </p>
              </div>
            )}

            {audioBlocked && remoteUserConnected && (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={unlockSecretAudio}
                className="absolute top-14 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 z-30 px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 border border-emerald-300 text-white text-xs font-extrabold flex items-center gap-2 shadow-2xl animate-pulse"
              >
                <Volume2 className="w-4 h-4" />
                <span>ድምፅ ለመስማት ይንኩ (Unmute)</span>
              </button>
            )}

            {/* Self Video Layer (Full clear main view while waiting OR when swapped; generous unobstructed PiP when remote is main) */}
            {isVideoOn ? (
              <video
                ref={localVideoRef}
                autoPlay
                playsInline
                muted
                onClick={() => {
                  if (remoteUserConnected && !swapVideoFeed) setSwapVideoFeed(true);
                }}
                className={`object-cover contrast-[1.03] saturate-[1.05] mirror ${
                  !remoteUserConnected || swapVideoFeed
                    ? 'w-full h-full block'
                    : 'absolute bottom-3 right-3 w-32 sm:w-44 h-44 sm:h-52 rounded-2xl border-2 border-amber-400/90 shadow-2xl z-20 cursor-pointer'
                }`}
              />
            ) : !remoteUserConnected ? (
              <div className="relative w-full h-full">
                <img
                  src={sacredSanctuaryImg}
                  alt="የንስሐ ሚስጥር ቤት"
                  decoding="async"
                  className="w-full h-full object-cover filter brightness-50"
                />
                <div className="absolute inset-0 flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-6 text-center space-y-3">
                  <Lock className="w-10 h-10 text-amber-400" />
                  <p className="text-sm font-heading font-bold text-amber-100">
                    የንስሐ ሚስጥር ቤት ክፍት ነው
                  </p>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={handleToggleVideo}
                    disabled={requestingMedia}
                    className="px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 border border-emerald-400 text-white text-xs font-extrabold inline-flex items-center gap-2 shadow-lg transition"
                  >
                    <Video className="w-4 h-4" />
                    <span>{requestingMedia ? 'በመክፈት ላይ...' : 'ካሜራ ክፈት / ፍቀድ'}</span>
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="absolute bottom-3 right-3 w-32 sm:w-44 h-44 sm:h-52 rounded-2xl border-2 border-amber-400/70 bg-stone-950/95 z-20 flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-3 text-center gap-2 shadow-2xl">
                <VideoOff className="w-6 h-6 text-red-400" />
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={handleToggleVideo}
                  disabled={requestingMedia}
                  className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 text-white text-xs font-extrabold transition"
                >
                  {requestingMedia ? 'በመክፈት ላይ...' : 'ካሜራ ፍቀድ'}
                </button>
              </div>
            )}

            {/* Main View Top-Left Live Badge */}
            <div className="absolute top-3 left-3 bg-stone-950/85 backdrop-blur-sm px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg border border-amber-700/50 text-xs font-bold text-amber-200 flex items-center gap-1.5 z-10">
              <span className={`w-2 h-2 rounded-full ${remoteUserConnected ? 'bg-emerald-400 animate-pulse' : 'bg-amber-400 animate-pulse'}`} />
              <span>
                {!remoteUserConnected
                  ? `${user.baptismalName || user.fullName} (ካሜራዎ ዝግጁ ነው · በመጠባበቅ ላይ)`
                  : swapVideoFeed
                  ? `${user.baptismalName || user.fullName} (እኔ)`
                  : `${remoteUserName} (በቀጥታ HD ቪዲዮ)`}
              </span>
            </div>

            {/* Fullscreen & Swap Controls Top-Right */}
            <div className="absolute top-3 right-3 z-20 flex items-center gap-1.5">
              {remoteUserConnected && (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setSwapVideoFeed((v) => !v)}
                  className="px-2.5 py-2 rounded-lg bg-stone-950/85 hover:bg-amber-900 border border-amber-600/50 text-amber-200 transition text-xs font-bold"
                  title="ዋናውንና የራስዎን ቪዲዮ አቀያይር"
                >
                  አቀያይር
                </button>
              )}
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleToggleFullscreen}
                className="p-2 rounded-lg bg-stone-950/85 hover:bg-amber-900 border border-amber-600/50 text-amber-200 transition flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-bold"
                title={isVideoFullscreen ? 'ሙሉ ስክሪን ዝጋ' : 'ሙሉ ስክሪን ክፈት'}
              >
                {isVideoFullscreen ? <Minimize2 className="w-4 h-4" /> : <Maximize2 className="w-4 h-4" />}
                <span className="hidden sm:inline">{isVideoFullscreen ? 'መደበኛ' : 'ሙሉ ስክሪን'}</span>
              </button>
            </div>

            {/* PiP Corner Name Pill (only when remote connected and inset visible) */}
            {remoteUserConnected && isVideoOn && (
              <div className="absolute bottom-4 right-4 z-30 bg-stone-950/85 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-md border border-amber-700/40 text-[10px] text-amber-200 pointer-events-none">
                {swapVideoFeed ? remoteUserName : `${user.baptismalName || user.fullName} (እኔ)`}
              </div>
            )}

            {/* Radiant Blessing Overlay when Priest Absolves */}
            {isBlessed && (
              <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-amber-500/20 backdrop-blur-[2px] flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-6 text-center animate-fade-in z-20">
                <div className="w-24 h-24 rounded-full bg-amber-400/25 border-2 border-amber-300 flex items-center justify-center text-amber-200 glow-gold mb-3">
                  <Sparkles className="w-12 h-12" />
                </div>
                <h4 className="text-2xl font-heading font-bold text-amber-100">
                  "እግዚአብሔር ይፍታህ፤ ኃጢአትህን ይደምስስልህ!"
                </h4>
              </div>
            )}

            {mediaPermissionMsg && (
              <div className="absolute top-14 left-3 right-3 z-20 p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-amber-950/90 border border-amber-500/70 text-xs text-amber-100 font-bold flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 shadow-xl">
                <span>{mediaPermissionMsg}</span>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={handleRequestSecretMedia}
                  disabled={requestingMedia}
                  className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 text-white text-xs font-extrabold"
                >
                  {requestingMedia ? 'በመክፈት ላይ...' : 'ካሜራና ማይክ ፍቀድ'}
                </button>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Clean Controls Bar Placed Below the Video Frame (Zero Face Overlap!) */}
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 p-3 rounded-2xl bg-stone-950 border border-amber-900/40 shadow-lg">
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
              <button
                onClick={handleToggleMic}
                className={`p-2.5 rounded-xl border transition flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs ${
                  isMicOn ? 'bg-emerald-950 border-emerald-600 text-emerald-300' : 'bg-red-950 border-red-700 text-red-300'
                }`}
                title={isMicOn ? 'ማይክ ክፍት ነው' : 'ማይክ ዝጋ'}
              >
                {isMicOn ? <Mic className="w-4 h-4" /> : <MicOff className="w-4 h-4" />}
                <span className="hidden sm:inline">{isMicOn ? 'ማይክ ክፍት' : 'ማይክ ዝግ'}</span>
              </button>

              <button
                onClick={handleToggleVideo}
                className={`p-2.5 rounded-xl border transition flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs ${
                  isVideoOn ? 'bg-stone-900 border-stone-700 text-stone-200' : 'bg-red-950 border-red-700 text-red-300'
                }`}
                title={isVideoOn ? 'ካሜራ ክፍት ነው' : 'ካሜራ ዝጋ'}
              >
                {isVideoOn ? <Video className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-400" /> : <VideoOff className="w-4 h-4" />}
                <span className="hidden sm:inline">{isVideoOn ? 'ካሜራ ክፍት' : 'ካሜራ ዝግ'}</span>
              </button>

              <button
                onClick={handleSwitchCamera}
                className="p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-stone-900 border border-stone-700 text-stone-300 hover:text-amber-200 transition"
                title="ካሜራ ቀይር (Flip Camera)"
              >
                <RefreshCw className="w-4 h-4" />
              </button>

              <button
                onClick={() => {
                  speakBlessingPrayer('በእግዚአብሔር ስም ሰላም ለእናንተ ይሁን። ልጄ ኃጢአትህን ያለመደበቅ ተናዘዝ፤ እግዚአብሔር መሐሪና ይቅር ባይ ነው።');
                }}
                className="px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-stone-900 border border-stone-700 text-xs text-amber-300 hover:bg-stone-800 transition flex items-center gap-1.5"
              >
                <Volume2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-400" />
                <span>ቀሲሱን አድምጥ</span>
              </button>
            </div>

            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-stone-300">
              <Flame className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400 animate-candle" />
              <span className="hidden sm:inline">የንስሐ ጧፍ በርቷል</span>
            </div>
          </div>

        </div>

        {/* Right: Canonical Confession Ritual Step-by-Step */}
        <div className="lg:col-span-5 flex flex-col h-[520px] rounded-2xl bg-stone-900/60 border border-amber-900/40 p-5 space-y-4 overflow-y-auto">
          
          <div className="border-b border-stone-800 pb-3">
            <h3 className="text-base font-semibold font-heading text-amber-200">
              የንስሐ አቀባበልና ሥርዓት (4ቱ ደረጃዎች)
            </h3>
          </div>

          {/* Stepper Tabs */}
          <div className="grid grid-cols-4 gap-1.5 p-1 bg-stone-950 rounded-xl border border-stone-800">
            {[
              { num: 1, label: 'ጸሎት' },
              { num: 2, label: 'መናዘዝ' },
              { num: 3, label: 'ቀኖና' },
              { num: 4, label: 'ፍትሐት' },
            ].map((s) => (
              <button
                key={s.num}
                onClick={() => setActiveStep(s.num as any)}
                className={`py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-medium transition ${
                  activeStep === s.num
                    ? 'bg-amber-900/70 text-amber-200 font-bold border border-amber-600/50'
                    : 'text-stone-400 hover:text-stone-200'
                }`}
              >
                {s.num}. {s.label}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>

          {/* Step 1: ጸሎተ ንስሐ */}
          {activeStep === 1 && (
            <div className="space-y-3 animate-fade-in">
              <div className="p-3.5 rounded-xl bg-stone-950/80 border border-stone-800 text-xs space-y-2">
                <p className="font-semibold text-amber-300">ደረጃ ፩፡ የንስሐ መጀመሪያ ጸሎት</p>
                <p className="text-stone-300 leading-relaxed font-serif">
                  "አቤቱ አምላኬ ሆይ፥ በሰማይና በምድር ፊት በደልሁ፤ በፊትህም ክፉ ሥራን አደረግሁ። እንደ ምሕረትህ ብዛት ይቅር በለኝ፤ በደሌንም አታስብብኝ። 
                  በካህኑ በቀሲስ ሄኖክ ፊት ኃጢአቴን እናዘዛለሁ፤ ይቅርታህን እሻለሁ።"
                </p>
              </div>
              <button
                onClick={() => setActiveStep(2)}
                className="w-full py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-amber-900/60 hover:bg-amber-900 border border-amber-600/50 text-amber-200 text-xs font-semibold transition"
              >
                ወደ መናዘዝ ሂድ (ደረጃ ፪) →
              </button>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Step 2: ኃጢአትን መናዘዝ */}
          {activeStep === 2 && (
            <div className="space-y-3 animate-fade-in flex-1 flex flex-col">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs">
                <span className="font-semibold text-amber-300">ደረጃ ፪፡ ኃጢአትን መናዘዝ</span>
                <span className="text-[10px] text-red-400">ሳይቀመጥ የሚቃጠል</span>
              </div>
              <textarea
                value={confessionNotes}
                onChange={(e) => setConfessionNotes(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="ኃጢአትህን እዚህ በግልጽ ጻፍ (የልብህን ሸክም እዚህ አውርደው)..."
                rows={5}
                className="w-full bg-stone-950 border border-stone-700 rounded-xl p-3 text-xs text-stone-100 placeholder-stone-600 focus:outline-none focus:border-amber-500"
              />
              <div className="flex gap-2">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={handleBurnConfessionNotes}
                  className="flex-1 py-2 rounded-xl bg-red-950/60 hover:bg-red-900 border border-red-800 text-red-200 text-xs transition"
                >
                  ማስታወሻውን አቃጥል (ደምስስ)
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setActiveStep(3)}
                  className="flex-1 py-2 rounded-xl bg-amber-900/60 hover:bg-amber-900 border border-amber-600 text-amber-200 text-xs transition"
                >
                  ቀኖና ተቀበል (ደረጃ ፫) →
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Step 3: ቀኖና መቀበል */}
          {activeStep === 3 && (
            <div className="space-y-3 animate-fade-in">
              <div className="p-3.5 rounded-xl bg-stone-950/80 border border-stone-800 text-xs space-y-2">
                <p className="font-semibold text-amber-300">ደረጃ ፫፡ ከአባታችን የተሰጠ ቀኖና</p>
                <div className="p-2.5 rounded-lg bg-stone-900 border border-amber-900/40 text-amber-200 font-medium">
                  {penancePrescription}
                </div>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={penancePrescription}
                  onChange={(e) => setPenancePrescription(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="ቀኖናውን ማስተካከል ከፈለጉ..."
                  className="w-full bg-stone-900 border border-stone-700 rounded-lg px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs text-stone-200"
                />
              </div>
              <button
                onClick={() => setActiveStep(4)}
                className="w-full py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-amber-900/60 hover:bg-amber-900 border border-amber-600/50 text-amber-200 text-xs font-semibold transition"
              >
                ወደ ፍትሐትና ቡራኬ ሂድ (ደረጃ ፬) →
              </button>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Step 4: ፍትሐት እና የመስቀል ቡራኬ */}
          {activeStep === 4 && (
            <div className="space-y-4 animate-fade-in text-center p-3 rounded-xl bg-stone-950/80 border border-amber-800/40">
              <div className="w-14 h-14 mx-auto rounded-full bg-amber-950/80 border border-amber-500 flex items-center justify-center text-amber-300 glow-gold">
                <Sparkles className="w-7 h-7" />
              </div>
              <div>
                <h4 className="text-base font-heading font-bold text-amber-200">ደረጃ ፬፡ የፍትሐት ጸሎትና የመስቀል ቡራኬ</h4>
              </div>

              <button
                onClick={handleGrantAbsolution}
                className="w-full py-3.5 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-900 via-amber-900 to-emerald-900 border border-amber-400 text-white font-bold text-xs shadow-lg hover:scale-[1.02] transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
              >
                <Sparkles className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-300" />
                <span>{isBlessed ? 'ቡራኬውን ተቀብለዋል (እግዚአብሔር ይፍታህ!)' : 'የመስቀል ቡራኬና የፍትሐት ጸሎት ተቀበል'}</span>
              </button>

              {isBlessed && (
                <div className="p-2.5 rounded-lg bg-emerald-950/50 border border-emerald-600/50 text-xs text-emerald-200 flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
                  <Check className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-400" />
                  <span>ንስሐዎ በክህነት ሥልጣን ተጠናቋል። በሰላም ሂዱ!</span>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          )}

        </div>

      </div>

    </div>
  );
};
