      geezText: 'ቀኖና ንስሐ ዘአዘዙ አበው ሐዋርያት ወሊቃውንት።',
      amharicText: `፩. ኃጢአትን ሳያፍሩና ሳይደብቁ ለንስሐ አባት በዕንባ መናዘዝ።
፪. በካህኑ የተሰጠውን የስግደት፣ የጾም እና የጸሎት ቀኖና በታማኝነት መፈጸም።
፫. ከቀድሞው ክፉ ጓደኛና ለኃጢአት ከሚያጋልጥ ቦታ መራቅ።
፬. በደል ያደረስንበትን ሰው ይቅርታ መጠየቅና ማካካስ።
፭. ዘወትር በመንፈሳዊ ንባብና በቅዱስ ቁርባን መዘጋጀት።`,
      description: 'የቤተክርስቲያን ቀኖና ለተነሳሂያን ያስቀመጣቸው መመሪያዎች።',
    },
    {
      id: 'pr-5',
      title: 'የአጽዋማት የቀን መቁጠሪያ (የጾም ሰሌዳ)',
      category: 'ጾም',
      geezText: 'አጽዋማት ሰብዐቱ እለ ተሠርዓ በኦሪት ወበወንጌል ለእስራኤል ዘመንፈስ።',
      amharicText: `በኢትዮጵያ ኦርቶዶክስ ተዋሕዶ ቤተክርስቲያን 7ቱ አጽዋማት፡
፩. ዐቢይ ጾም (ሁዳዴ - 55 ቀናት)
፪. ጾመ ሐዋርያት (የሰኔ ጾም)
፫. ጾመ ፍልሰታ (16 ቀናት - የነሐሴ)
፬. ጾመ ነቢያት (የገና ጾም - 43 ቀናት)
፭. ጾመ ነነዌ (3 ቀናት)
፮. ጾመ ጋድ (የብርሃነ ጥምቀት ዋዜማ)
፯. ጾመ ድኅነት (ረቡዕና ዓርብ በዓመቱ ሙሉ)`,
      description: 'በዓመት ውስጥ የሚጾሙት አጽዋማት ሙሉ ዝርዝር።',
    },
  ];

  const currentPrayer = prayers.find((p) => p.id === selectedPrayerId) || prayers[0];

  const handleSaveNotes = () => {
    localStorage.setItem('qh_offline_confession_notes', offlineNotes);
    setSavedSuccess(true);
    setTimeout(() => setSavedSuccess(false), 2000);
  };

  const handleClearNotes = () => {
    if (confirm('የተጻፈው የግል ማስታወሻ ይሰረዝ?')) {
      setOfflineNotes('');
      localStorage.removeItem('qh_offline_confession_notes');
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="max-w-6xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-8 space-y-8">
      
      {/* Offline Ready Banner */}
      <div className="p-5 rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-r from-stone-900 via-amber-950/40 to-stone-900 border border-amber-800/40 flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-1 rounded-full bg-emerald-950/80 border border-emerald-700/50 text-emerald-300 text-xs font-medium">
            <WifiOff className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-400" />
            <span>100% ያለ ኢንተርኔት የሚሰራ (Offline Ready)</span>
          </div>
          <h2 className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-heading font-bold text-amber-100 mt-2">
            የኦፍላይን መንፈሳዊ መጻሕፍትና ማስታወሻ
          </h2>
        </div>

        {/* Category Selector */}
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5 p-1 bg-stone-950 rounded-xl border border-stone-800 text-xs">
          {(['ጸሎት', 'ውዳሴ', 'ቀኖና', 'ጾም'] as const).map((cat) => (
            <button
              key={cat}
              onClick={() => setActiveCategory(cat)}
              className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg transition font-medium ${
                activeCategory === cat
                  ? 'bg-amber-900/80 text-amber-200 border border-amber-600/50 font-bold'
                  : 'text-stone-400 hover:text-stone-200'
              }`}
            >
              {cat}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Main Grid: Prayers Book vs Offline Confession Journal */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-6">
        
        {/* Left Column (8 cols): Book Reader */}
        <div className="lg:col-span-8 space-y-4">
          
          {/* Prayer Titles Selector Chips */}
          <div className="flex gap-2 overflow-x-auto pb-1">
            {prayers
              .filter((p) => p.category === activeCategory)
              .map((p) => (
                <button
                  key={p.id}
                  onClick={() => setSelectedPrayerId(p.id)}
                  className={`px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs whitespace-nowrap border transition-all ${
                    selectedPrayerId === p.id
                      ? 'bg-amber-950 border-amber-500 text-amber-200 font-bold'
                      : 'bg-stone-900 border-stone-800 text-stone-300 hover:border-stone-700'
                  }`}
                >
                  {p.title}
                </button>
              ))}
          </div>

          {/* Active Prayer Card */}
          <div className="p-6 rounded-2xl bg-stone-900/70 border border-amber-900/40 shadow-xl space-y-5">
            <div className="border-b border-stone-800 pb-3 flex items-center justify-between">
              <div>
                <h3 className="text-xl font-heading font-bold text-amber-200">{currentPrayer.title}</h3>
                <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 mt-0.5">{currentPrayer.description}</p>
              </div>
              <Bookmark className="w-5 h-5 text-amber-400 shrink-0" />
            </div>

            {/* Ge'ez Original Lead Verse */}
            {currentPrayer.geezText && (
              <div className="p-3.5 rounded-xl bg-amber-950/30 border border-amber-900/50 text-xs font-serif text-amber-300 leading-relaxed">
                <span className="font-bold block text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wider text-amber-400 mb-1">ግዕዝ (Ge'ez)</span>
                {currentPrayer.geezText}
              </div>
            )}

            {/* Amharic Text Body */}
            <div className="text-sm sm:text-base leading-relaxed text-stone-200 font-serif whitespace-pre-wrap">
              {currentPrayer.amharicText}
            </div>

            <div className="pt-3 border-t border-stone-800 text-[11px] text-stone-500 italic">
              * ይህ ጽሑፍ በስልክዎ ማህደረ-ትውስታ ውስጥ ተቀምጧል፤ በማንኛውም ጊዜ ያለ ኢንተርኔት ይነበባል።
            </div>
          </div>

        </div>

        {/* Right Column (4 cols): Personal Offline Confession Journal */}
        <div className="lg:col-span-4 space-y-4">
          
          <div className="p-5 rounded-2xl bg-stone-900/70 border border-amber-900/40 space-y-4">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <h3 className="text-base font-semibold font-heading text-amber-200">
                የግል የንስሐ ማስታወሻ (Offline Diary)
              </h3>
              <span className="text-[10px] text-emerald-400 font-mono">በስልክ ብቻ የሚቀመጥ</span>
            </div>

            <p className="text-xs text-stone-400">
              ለቀሲስ ሄኖክ ለመናገር ያሰቡትን ወይም የፈጸሙትን ቀኖና እዚህ ይመዝግቡ። ይህ ጽሑፍ ወደ የትኛውም ሰርቨር አይላክም።
            </p>

            <textarea
              value={offlineNotes}
              onChange={(e) => setOfflineNotes(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="የዕለት የንስሐ ሀሳብ፣ የስግደት ቁጥር፣ የጾም ማስታወሻ እዚህ ይጻፉ..."
              rows={8}
              className="w-full bg-stone-950 border border-stone-700 rounded-xl p-3 text-xs text-stone-100 placeholder-stone-600 focus:outline-none focus:border-amber-500"
            />

            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleSaveNotes}
                className="flex-1 py-2 rounded-xl bg-amber-900/80 hover:bg-amber-900 border border-amber-600/50 text-amber-200 text-xs font-semibold flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 transition"
              >
                <Save className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                <span>{savedSuccess ? 'ተቀምጧል!' : 'በስልክህ አስቀምጥ'}</span>
              </button>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleClearNotes}
                className="p-2 rounded-xl bg-stone-900 hover:bg-red-950/60 border border-stone-800 text-stone-400 hover:text-red-300 transition"
                title="አጥፋ"
              >
                <Trash2 className="w-4 h-4" />
              </button>
            </div>

            {savedSuccess && (
              <p className="text-xs text-emerald-400 flex items-center gap-1">
                <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                <span>ማስታወሻዎ በስልክዎ ላይ በሰላም ተቀምጧል።</span>
              </p>
            )}
          </div>

        </div>

      </div>

    </div>
  );
};


================================================================================
FILE: src/components/CommunityInviteModal.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import {
  X,
  UserPlus,
  Phone,
  Users,
  Send,
  Copy,
  Check,
  Trash2,
  Share2,
  Heart,
  Smartphone,
  MessageCircle,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { CommunityEngagement, PhoneContactRecord, UserProfile } from '../types';
import {
  addPhoneContacts,
  deleteSavedContact,
  fetchSavedContacts,
  recordWebsiteShare,
} from '../services/api';

interface CommunityInviteModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  user: UserProfile;
  engagement: CommunityEngagement;
  onEngagementChange: (next: CommunityEngagement) => void;
  onShareSite: () => Promise<void>;
}

export const CommunityInviteModal: React.FC<CommunityInviteModalProps> = ({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  user,
  engagement,
  onEngagementChange,
  onShareSite,
}) => {
  const [contacts, setContacts] = useState<PhoneContactRecord[]>([]);
  const [nameInput, setNameInput] = useState('');
  const [phoneInput, setPhoneInput] = useState('');
  const [bulkInput, setBulkInput] = useState('');
  const [showBulk, setShowBulk] = useState(false);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [statusMsg, setStatusMsg] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [copiedId, setCopiedId] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const supportsContactPicker =
    typeof navigator !== 'undefined' &&
    'contacts' in navigator &&
    typeof (navigator as any).contacts?.select === 'function';

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen) return;
    fetchSavedContacts().then(setContacts);
  }, [isOpen]);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const addedByName = user.baptismalName || user.fullName || 'የማኅበሩ አባል';

  const buildInviteLink = (contact?: PhoneContactRecord) => {
    const base = window.location.origin;
    if (!contact) return base;
    const params = new URLSearchParams();
    if (contact.name) params.set('inviteName', contact.name);
    if (contact.phone) params.set('invitePhone', contact.phone);
    return `${base}/?${params.toString()}`;
  };

  const buildInviteMessage = (contact?: PhoneContactRecord) => {
    const link = buildInviteLink(contact);
    return `ሰላም ${contact?.name || 'ወዳጄ'}! የ"ቀሲስ ሄኖክ" የኦርቶዶክስ መንፈሳዊ ጉባኤ፣ የቀጥታ ቪዲዮ ትምህርት፣ የንስሐ ሚስጥር ቤት እና የኤአይ እውቀት ማህደር ድህረገፅ ተከፍቷል። በዚህ ሊንክ ይግቡና አብረን እንሳተፍ: ${link}`;
  };

  const handlePickFromPhone = async () => {
    setStatusMsg(null);
    try {
      const selected: Array<{ name?: string[]; tel?: string[] }> = await (
        navigator as any
      ).contacts.select(['name', 'tel'], { multiple: true });

      if (!selected || selected.length === 0) return;

      const normalized = selected
        .map((c) => ({
          name: (c.name && c.name[0]) || '',
          phone: (c.tel && c.tel[0]) || '',
        }))
        .filter((c) => c.phone || c.name);

      if (normalized.length === 0) {
        setStatusMsg('የተመረጡት ሰዎች ስልክ ቁጥር የላቸውም።');
        return;
      }

      setSaving(true);
      const res = await addPhoneContacts(normalized, addedByName);
      setContacts(res.contacts);
      onEngagementChange({ ...engagement, contactsCount: res.contacts.length });
      setStatusMsg(`${res.addedCount} ሰዎች ከስልክዎ ወደ ድህረገፁ በተሳካ ሁኔታ ተጨምረዋል!`);
    } catch (err: any) {
      setStatusMsg(err?.message || 'የስልክ አድራሻ ማውጫ መክፈት አልተቻለም፤ ከታች በእጅ ማስገባት ይችላሉ።');
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  };

  const handleManualAdd = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!phoneInput.trim() && !nameInput.trim()) return;
    setSaving(true);
    setStatusMsg(null);
    try {
      const res = await addPhoneContacts(
        [{ name: nameInput.trim(), phone: phoneInput.trim() }],
        addedByName
      );
      setContacts(res.contacts);
      onEngagementChange({ ...engagement, contactsCount: res.contacts.length });
      setNameInput('');
      setPhoneInput('');
      setStatusMsg('ሰውየው ወደ ድህረገፁ ዝርዝር ተጨምረዋል! ከታች ባሉት ቁልፎች በቀጥታ መጋበዝ ይችላሉ።');
    } catch (err: any) {
      setStatusMsg(err?.message || 'ማስቀመጥ አልተቻለም።');
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  };

  const handleBulkAdd = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const lines = bulkInput
      .split('\n')
      .map((l) => l.trim())
      .filter(Boolean);
    if (lines.length === 0) return;

    const parsed = lines.map((line) => {
      const parts = line.split(/[,|\t-]+/).map((p) => p.trim());
      if (parts.length >= 2) {
        return { name: parts[0], phone: parts[1] };
      }
      const match = line.match(/^(.*?)(\+?\d[\d\s]{7,})$/);
      if (match) {
        return { name: match[1].trim() || match[2].trim(), phone: match[2].trim() };
      }
      return { name: line, phone: line };
    });

    setSaving(true);
    setStatusMsg(null);
    try {
      const res = await addPhoneContacts(parsed, addedByName);
      setContacts(res.contacts);
      onEngagementChange({ ...engagement, contactsCount: res.contacts.length });
      setBulkInput('');
      setShowBulk(false);
      setStatusMsg(`${res.addedCount} ሰዎች በአንድ ጊዜ ወደ ድህረገፁ ተጨምረዋል!`);
    } catch (err: any) {
      setStatusMsg(err?.message || 'ማስቀመጥ አልተቻለም።');
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  };

  const handleDelete = async (id: string) => {
    const next = await deleteSavedContact(id);
    setContacts(next);
    onEngagementChange({ ...engagement, contactsCount: next.length });
  };

  const handleCopyInvite = async (contact: PhoneContactRecord) => {
    const msg = buildInviteMessage(contact);
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(msg);
      setCopiedId(contact.id);
      const next = await recordWebsiteShare();
      onEngagementChange(next);
      setTimeout(() => setCopiedId(null), 2200);
    } catch {}
  };

  const handleInviteVia = async (platform: 'whatsapp' | 'telegram' | 'sms', contact: PhoneContactRecord) => {
    const text = buildInviteMessage(contact);
    const cleanPhone = contact.phone.replace(/[^\d+]/g, '');
    const next = await recordWebsiteShare().catch(() => null);
    if (next) onEngagementChange(next);

    if (platform === 'whatsapp') {
      const targetPhone = cleanPhone.startsWith('0') ? `251${cleanPhone.slice(1)}` : cleanPhone.replace('+', '');
      window.open(`https://wa.me/${targetPhone}?text=${encodeURIComponent(text)}`, '_blank', 'noopener,noreferrer');
    } else if (platform === 'telegram') {
      window.open(
        `https://t.me/share/url?url=${encodeURIComponent(buildInviteLink(contact))}&text=${encodeURIComponent(text)}`,
        '_blank',
        'noopener,noreferrer'
      );
    } else {
      window.location.href = `sms:${cleanPhone}?body=${encodeURIComponent(text)}`;
    }
  };

  return (
    <div
      onClick={(e) => {
        if (e.target === e.currentTarget) onClose();
      }}
      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 overflow-y-auto bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center p-4"
    >
      <div className="w-full max-w-2xl rounded-3xl bg-stone-950 border-2 border-amber-600/50 shadow-2xl p-5 sm:p-7 space-y-5 relative max-h-[92vh] overflow-y-auto">
        <button
          onClick={onClose}
          aria-label="ዝጋ"
          className="absolute top-4 right-4 p-2 rounded-lg bg-stone-900 text-stone-300 hover:text-white transition"
        >
          <X className="w-5 h-5" />
        </button>

        {/* Header */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 pr-10">
          <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-br from-amber-900 to-red-950 border border-amber-500/60 flex items-center justify-center text-amber-300 shrink-0">
            <UserPlus className="w-6 h-6" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <h3 className="text-lg sm:text-xl font-heading font-extrabold text-amber-100">
              ከስልክዎ ሰዎችን ወደ ድህረገፁ ይጨምሩ እና ያጋሩ
            </h3>
            <p className="text-xs text-stone-300">
              በስልክዎ ላይ ስልክ ቁጥራቸው ያለ ዘመድ፣ ጓደኛና ምዕመናንን በቀጥታ አድ አድርገው መልእክት ይላኩላቸው።
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Summary Engagement Row */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2.5">
          <div className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-stone-900/90 border border-stone-800 text-center">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-1 text-rose-400 text-sm font-extrabold">
              <Heart className="w-4 h-4 fill-rose-500" />
              <span>{engagement.likesCount}</span>
            </div>
            <p className="text-[11px] text-stone-300 mt-0.5">ጠቅላላ ላይክ</p>
          </div>
          <div className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-stone-900/90 border border-stone-800 text-center">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-1 text-amber-300 text-sm font-extrabold">
              <Share2 className="w-4 h-4" />
              <span>{engagement.sharesCount}</span>
            </div>
            <p className="text-[11px] text-stone-300 mt-0.5">ጠቅላላ ሼር</p>
          </div>
          <div className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-stone-900/90 border border-stone-800 text-center">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-1 text-emerald-400 text-sm font-extrabold">
              <Users className="w-4 h-4" />
              <span>{contacts.length}</span>
            </div>
            <p className="text-[11px] text-stone-300 mt-0.5">የተጨመሩ ሰዎች</p>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Direct Android Contact Picker + Site Share */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-2.5">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={
              supportsContactPicker
                ? handlePickFromPhone
                : () => setStatusMsg('ይህ ብራውዘር ቀጥታ የስልክ ማውጫ መክፈት ስለማይፈቅድ ከታች በስም እና በስልክ ቁጥር በቀላሉ ያስገቡ።')
            }
            disabled={saving}
            className="w-full py-3 px-4 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-700 via-amber-600 to-amber-700 hover:from-amber-600 hover:to-amber-500 text-stone-950 font-extrabold text-xs sm:text-sm flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shadow-lg transition"
          >
            <Smartphone className="w-4 h-4" />
            <span>ከስልክ ኮንታክት ምረጥ (Phonebook)</span>
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onShareSite}
            className="w-full py-3 px-4 rounded-xl bg-stone-900 hover:bg-stone-800 border border-amber-600/60 text-amber-200 font-bold text-xs sm:text-sm flex items-center justify-center gap-2 transition"
          >
            <Share2 className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400" />
            <span>ድህረገፁን ለሁሉም ሼር አድርግ</span>
          </button>
        </div>

        {statusMsg && (
          <div className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-emerald-950/50 border border-emerald-600/60 text-xs text-emerald-200 font-bold">
            {statusMsg}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Single Add Form */}
        <form onSubmit={handleManualAdd} className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-stone-900/70 border border-stone-800 space-y-3">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <span className="text-xs font-bold text-amber-200 flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <Phone className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-400" />
              <span>ስምና ስልክ ቁጥር አድ ያድርጉ</span>
            </span>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setShowBulk((v) => !v)}
              className="text-[11px] text-amber-400 hover:underline font-bold"
            >
              {showBulk ? 'ነጠላ መመዝገቢያ' : '+ ብዙ ቁጥር በአንድ ጊዜ አስገባ'}
            </button>
          </div>

          {!showBulk ? (
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-12 gap-2">
              <input
                type="text"
                value={nameInput}
                onChange={(e) => setNameInput(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="የሰውየው ስም (ምሳሌ፡ አቶ ዳዊት)"
                className="sm:col-span-5 bg-stone-950 border border-stone-700 rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 text-xs text-stone-100 placeholder-stone-500 focus:outline-none focus:border-amber-500"
              />
              <input
                type="tel"
                value={phoneInput}
                onChange={(e) => setPhoneInput(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="ስልክ ቁጥር (09...)"
                className="sm:col-span-4 bg-stone-950 border border-stone-700 rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 text-xs text-stone-100 placeholder-stone-500 focus:outline-none focus:border-amber-500"
                required
              />
              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={saving || (!phoneInput.trim() && !nameInput.trim())}
                className="sm:col-span-3 py-2.5 px-3 rounded-xl bg-amber-600 hover:bg-amber-500 text-stone-950 font-extrabold text-xs flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 disabled:opacity-50 transition"
              >
                <UserPlus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                <span>አድ አድርግ</span>
              </button>
            </div>
          ) : null}
        </form>

        {showBulk && (
          <form onSubmit={handleBulkAdd} className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-stone-900/70 border border-amber-700/40 space-y-2.5">
            <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-amber-200">
              በእያንዳንዱ መስመር ስምና ስልክ ቁጥር ያስገቡ፦
            </label>
            <textarea
              rows={4}
              value={bulkInput}
              onChange={(e) => setBulkInput(e.target.value)}
              placeholder={'አበበ ከበደ 0911223344\nማርታ ተስፋዬ 0912345678'}
              className="w-full bg-stone-950 border border-stone-700 rounded-xl p-3 text-xs text-stone-100 placeholder-stone-500 focus:outline-none focus:border-amber-500"
            />
            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={saving || !bulkInput.trim()}
              className="w-full py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-amber-600 hover:bg-amber-500 text-stone-950 font-extrabold text-xs transition"
            >
              ሁሉንም በአንድ ጊዜ አድ አድርግ
            </button>
          </form>
        )}

        {/* Saved Contacts Directory */}
        <div className="space-y-2.5">
          <h4 className="text-xs font-extrabold text-amber-200 flex items-center justify-between">
            <span>ወደ ድህረገፁ የተጨመሩ እውቂያዎች ({contacts.length})</span>
            <span className="text-[11px] text-stone-400 font-normal">በ1 ጠቅታ ግብዣ ይላኩ</span>
          </h4>

          {contacts.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="p-5 rounded-2xl bg-stone-900/50 border border-stone-800 text-center text-xs text-stone-400">
              እስካሁን የስልክ እውቂያ አልተጨመረም። ከላይ ባለው ቁልፍ ከስልክዎ ይምረጡ ወይም ስልክ ቁጥር አድ ያድርጉ።
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="space-y-2 max-h-60 overflow-y-auto pr-1">
              {contacts.map((c) => (
                <div
                  key={c.id}
                  className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-stone-900 border border-stone-800 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2"
                >
                  <div className="min-w-0">
                    <p className="text-xs font-extrabold text-amber-100 truncate">{c.name}</p>
                    <p className="text-[11px] text-stone-300 font-mono">{c.phone}</p>
                  </div>

                  <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 flex-wrap">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => handleInviteVia('whatsapp', c)}
                      className="px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-emerald-950 hover:bg-emerald-900 border border-emerald-700/60 text-emerald-200 text-[11px] font-bold inline-flex items-center gap-1"
                    >
                      <MessageCircle className="w-3 h-3" />
                      <span>WhatsApp</span>
                    </button>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => handleInviteVia('telegram', c)}
                      className="px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-sky-950 hover:bg-sky-900 border border-sky-700/60 text-sky-200 text-[11px] font-bold inline-flex items-center gap-1"
                    >
                      <Send className="w-3 h-3" />
                      <span>Telegram</span>
                    </button>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => handleInviteVia('sms', c)}
                      className="px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-amber-950 hover:bg-amber-900 border border-amber-700/60 text-amber-200 text-[11px] font-bold"
                    >
                      SMS
                    </button>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => handleCopyInvite(c)}
                      title="ግብዣ ቅዳ"
                      className="p-1.5 rounded-lg bg-stone-950 border border-stone-700 text-stone-300 hover:text-amber-200"
                    >
                      {copiedId === c.id ? (
                        <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-400" />
                      ) : (
                        <Copy className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                      )}
                    </button>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => handleDelete(c.id)}
                      title="አጥፋ"
                      className="p-1.5 rounded-lg bg-stone-950 border border-stone-800 text-stone-400 hover:text-red-400"
                    >
                      <Trash2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};


================================================================================
FILE: src/components/PWAInstallModal.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import {
  X,
  Download,
  Share2,
  PlusSquare,
  Check,
  Copy,
  ExternalLink,
  MoreVertical,
  Compass,
  Menu,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { usePWAInstall } from '../hooks/usePWAInstall';

interface PWAInstallModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
}

export const PWAInstallModal: React.FC<PWAInstallModalProps> = ({ isOpen, onClose }) => {
  const {
    isInstallable,
    isInstalled,
    isIOS,
    isAndroid,
    isInAppBrowser,
    inAppName,
    isSafariOnIOS,
    isSamsungInternet,
    install,
  } = usePWAInstall();

  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const handleInstallClick = async () => {
    const result = await install();
    if (result === 'accepted') {
      onClose();
    }
  };

  const handleCopyLink = async () => {
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(window.location.href);
      setCopied(true);
      setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2500);
    } catch {
      setCopied(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div
      onClick={(e) => {
        if (e.target === e.currentTarget) onClose();
      }}
      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 overflow-y-auto bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center p-4"
    >
      <div className="w-full max-w-md rounded-3xl bg-stone-950 border-2 border-amber-600/50 shadow-2xl p-6 sm:p-8 space-y-5 animate-scale-up relative text-center">
        <button
          onClick={onClose}
          aria-label="Close"
          className="absolute top-4 right-4 p-2 rounded-lg bg-stone-900 text-stone-400 hover:text-stone-100 transition"
        >
          <X className="w-5 h-5" />
        </button>

        {/* Real App Icon Preview */}
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-2">
          <img
            src="/pwa-192x192.png"
            alt="ቀሲስ ሄኖክ"
            className="w-16 h-16 rounded-2xl border-2 border-amber-500/60 shadow-xl"
          />
          <div>
            <h3 className="text-xl font-heading font-bold text-amber-100">
              "ቀሲስ ሄኖክ" አፕሊኬሽንን ወደ ስልክዎ ይጫኑ
            </h3>
            <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-stone-300 mt-1">
              ልክ እንደ Play Store / App Store መተግበሪያ በስልክዎ መነሻ ገጽ ላይ በቀጥታ ይቀመጣል።
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* 1. Already Installed */}
        {isInstalled ? (
          <div className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-emerald-950/60 border border-emerald-600/60 text-xs text-emerald-200 flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
            <Check className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-400 shrink-0" />
            <span>አፕሊኬሽኑ በስልክዎ ላይ በተሳካ ሁኔታ ተጭኗል!</span>
          </div>
        ) : isInstallable ? (
          /* 2. Native One-Tap Install (Android Chrome / Edge / Desktop Chrome) */
          <div className="space-y-3">
            <button
              onClick={handleInstallClick}
              className="w-full py-3.5 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-r from-red-950 via-amber-800 to-red-950 border border-amber-400 text-white font-bold text-sm shadow-xl hover:scale-[1.02] transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
            >
              <Download className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-300" />
              <span>አሁኑኑ ወደ ስልክ ጫን (1-Tap Install)</span>
            </button>
            <p className="text-[11px] text-stone-400">
              በአንድ ጠቅታ ያለ ፋይል ማውረድ በቀጥታ ወደ ስልክዎ ይጫናል።
            </p>
          </div>
        ) : isInAppBrowser ? (
          /* 3. Inside Telegram / WhatsApp / Facebook / TikTok In-App Browser */
          <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-amber-950/30 border border-amber-600/50 text-left space-y-3 text-xs text-stone-200">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-amber-300 font-bold">
              <ExternalLink className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" />
              <span>
                ከ {inAppName || 'መልእክት መተግበሪያ'} ውስጥ በመክፈትዎ ቀጥታ መጫን ታግዷል
              </span>
            </div>
            <p className="text-stone-300 text-[11px] leading-relaxed">
              Telegram ወይም WhatsApp የውስጥ ማሰሻ አፕሊኬሽን ለመጫን አይፈቅድም። በቀላሉ በChrome ወይም Safari ይክፈቱት፡
            </p>

            <div className="flex items-start gap-2.5">
              <span className="w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-amber-950 border border-amber-600/60 flex items-center justify-center text-amber-300 shrink-0 font-bold text-[10px]">
                1
              </span>
              <p>
                ከላይ በስተቀኝ ያለውን <MoreVertical className="w-3.5 h-3.5 inline text-amber-300" /> (ወይም የማጋሪያ) ምልክት ተጭነው{' '}
                <strong className="text-amber-300">
                  {isIOS ? '"Open in Safari"' : '"Open in Chrome / Browser"'}
                </strong>{' '}
                የሚለውን ይምረጡ።
              </p>
            </div>

            <div className="flex items-start gap-2.5">
              <span className="w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-amber-950 border border-amber-600/60 flex items-center justify-center text-amber-300 shrink-0 font-bold text-[10px]">
                2
              </span>
              <p>
                ከዚያም <strong className="text-amber-300">"አሁን ጫን"</strong> የሚለው ቁልፍ ወዲያውኑ ይሠራል።
              </p>
            </div>

            <button
              onClick={handleCopyLink}
              className="w-full mt-1 py-2.5 px-3 rounded-xl bg-amber-600/20 hover:bg-amber-600/30 border border-amber-500/60 text-amber-200 font-bold flex items-center justify-center gap-2 transition"
            >
              {copied ? <Check className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-400" /> : <Copy className="w-4 h-4" />}
              <span>{copied ? 'ሊንኩ ተገልብጧል — Chrome/Safari ላይ ይክፈቱት' : 'ሊንኩን ኮፒ አድርግ (Copy Link)'}</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        ) : isIOS ? (
          /* 4. iPhone / iPad Instructions */
          <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-stone-900/80 border border-stone-800 text-left space-y-3 text-xs text-stone-300">
            {!isSafariOnIOS && (
              <div className="p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-amber-950/50 border border-amber-600/50 text-amber-200 text-[11px] space-y-2">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 font-bold">
                  <Compass className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400 shrink-0" />
                  <span>በiPhone ላይ ለመጫን በSafari ብራውዘር ይክፈቱት</span>
                </div>
                <button
                  onClick={handleCopyLink}
                  className="w-full py-1.5 px-2.5 rounded-lg bg-amber-500/20 hover:bg-amber-500/30 border border-amber-500/60 text-amber-200 font-bold flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5"
                >
                  {copied ? <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-400" /> : <Copy className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />}
                  <span>{copied ? 'ተገልብጧል!' : 'ሊንኩን ኮፒ አድርጎ Safari ላይ ክፈት'}</span>
                </button>
              </div>
            )}

            <p className="font-semibold text-amber-200">በiPhone / iPad Safari ላይ ለመጫን፡</p>

            <div className="flex items-start gap-2.5">
              <span className="w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-amber-950 border border-amber-600/60 flex items-center justify-center text-amber-300 shrink-0 font-bold text-[10px]">
                1
              </span>
              <p>
                ከታች ባለው የSafari ሜኑ ላይ የ <strong className="text-amber-300">"Share" (ማጋሪያ)</strong>{' '}
                <Share2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 inline mx-0.5 text-amber-400" /> ምልክትን ይጫኑ።
              </p>
            </div>

            <div className="flex items-start gap-2.5">
              <span className="w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-amber-950 border border-amber-600/60 flex items-center justify-center text-amber-300 shrink-0 font-bold text-[10px]">
                2
              </span>
              <p>
                ወደ ታች ዝቅ ብለው <strong className="text-amber-300">"Add to Home Screen" (ወደ መነሻ ገጽ አክል)</strong>{' '}
                <PlusSquare className="w-3.5 h-3.5 inline mx-0.5 text-amber-400" /> የሚለውን ይምረጡ።
              </p>
            </div>

            <div className="flex items-start gap-2.5">
              <span className="w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-amber-950 border border-amber-600/60 flex items-center justify-center text-amber-300 shrink-0 font-bold text-[10px]">
                3
              </span>
              <p>
                ከላይ በስተቀኝ <strong className="text-amber-300">"Add"</strong> የሚለውን ሲጫኑ አፕሊኬሽኑ በስልክዎ መነሻ ገጽ ላይ ይቀመጣል!
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : (
          /* 5. Android Chrome / Samsung Internet / Desktop Browser Fallback */
          <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-stone-900/80 border border-stone-800 text-left space-y-3 text-xs text-stone-300">
            <p className="font-semibold text-amber-200">
              {isSamsungInternet
                ? 'በSamsung Internet ላይ ለመጫን፡'
                : isAndroid
                  ? 'በAndroid ስልክ (Chrome / Browser) ላይ ለመጫን፡'
                  : 'በስልክ ወይም ኮምፒውተር ላይ ለመጫን፡'}
            </p>

            {isSamsungInternet ? (
              <>
                <div className="flex items-start gap-2.5">
                  <span className="w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-amber-950 border border-amber-600/60 flex items-center justify-center text-amber-300 shrink-0 font-bold text-[10px]">
                    1
                  </span>
                  <p>
                    ከታች በስተቀኝ ያለውን <Menu className="w-3.5 h-3.5 inline mx-0.5 text-amber-400" /> (Menu) ምልክት ይጫኑ።
                  </p>
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-start gap-2.5">
                  <span className="w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-amber-950 border border-amber-600/60 flex items-center justify-center text-amber-300 shrink-0 font-bold text-[10px]">
                    2
                  </span>
                  <p>
                    <strong className="text-amber-300">"Add page to" → "Home screen"</strong> የሚለውን ይምረጡ።
                  </p>
                </div>
              </>
            ) : (
              <>
                <div className="flex items-start gap-2.5">
                  <span className="w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-amber-950 border border-amber-600/60 flex items-center justify-center text-amber-300 shrink-0 font-bold text-[10px]">
                    1
                  </span>
                  <p>
                    ከላይ በስተቀኝ ያለውን ባለ 3 ነጥብ <MoreVertical className="w-3.5 h-3.5 inline text-amber-400" /> (Menu) ምልክት ይጫኑ።
                  </p>
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-start gap-2.5">
                  <span className="w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-amber-950 border border-amber-600/60 flex items-center justify-center text-amber-300 shrink-0 font-bold text-[10px]">
                    2
                  </span>
                  <p>
                    <strong className="text-amber-300">"Add to Home screen"</strong> ወይም{' '}
                    <strong className="text-amber-300">"Install app"</strong> የሚለውን ይምረጡ።
                  </p>
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-start gap-2.5">
                  <span className="w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-amber-950 border border-amber-600/60 flex items-center justify-center text-amber-300 shrink-0 font-bold text-[10px]">
                    3
                  </span>
                  <p>
                    <strong className="text-amber-300">"Install / Add"</strong> ሲሉ ሙሉ አፕሊኬሽኑ በስልክዎ ላይ ይጫናል!
                  </p>
                </div>
              </>
            )}

            <button
              onClick={handleCopyLink}
              className="w-full mt-1 py-2 px-3 rounded-xl bg-stone-950 hover:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-700 text-stone-300 text-[11px] font-medium flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 transition"
            >
              {copied ? <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-400" /> : <Copy className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-400" />}
              <span>{copied ? 'ሊንኩ ተገልብጧል!' : 'ሊንኩን ኮፒ አድርግ (Copy Link)'}</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        )}

        <button
          onClick={onClose}
          className="w-full py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-stone-900 hover:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-800 text-stone-300 text-xs transition"
        >
          ዝጋ (Close)
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};


================================================================================
FILE: src/components/SuperDashboardDrawer.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { X, ArrowLeft, Users, Calendar, Mic, Wallet, Shield, KeyRound, User, Save } from 'lucide-react';
import { ConfessionBooking, WalletState, WalletTransaction, UserProfile, UserRole } from '../types';
import { fetchRegisteredUsers, updateUserProfile } from '../services/api';

interface SuperDashboardDrawerProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  bookings: ConfessionBooking[];
  onUpdateBookingStatus: (id: string, status: 'confirmed' | 'completed' | 'cancelled') => void;
  walletState: WalletState;
  transactions: WalletTransaction[];
  currentUser: UserProfile;
  onUpdateUser?: (updated: UserProfile) => void;
  onOpenAuthModal?: () => void;
}

export const SuperDashboardDrawer: React.FC<SuperDashboardDrawerProps> = ({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  bookings,
  onUpdateBookingStatus,
  walletState,
  transactions,
  currentUser,
  onUpdateUser,
  onOpenAuthModal,
}) => {
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<'profile' | 'children' | 'confessions' | 'assembly_control' | 'wallet_audit' | 'system'>('profile');

  const [profileForm, setProfileForm] = useState({
    fullName: currentUser.fullName || '',
    baptismalName: currentUser.baptismalName || '',
    phone: currentUser.phone || '',
    email: currentUser.email || '',
    confessorPriest: currentUser.confessorPriest || 'ቀሲስ ሄኖክ',
    confessionStage: currentUser.confessionStage || 'ጀማሪ',
    role: currentUser.role || 'child',
  });
  const [profileSaved, setProfileSaved] = useState(false);

  // Real registered users loaded from server
  const [registeredUsers, setRegisteredUsers] = useState<UserProfile[]>([]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isOpen) {
      fetchRegisteredUsers().then(setRegisteredUsers);
      setProfileForm({
        fullName: currentUser.fullName || '',
        baptismalName: currentUser.baptismalName || '',
        phone: currentUser.phone || '',
        email: currentUser.email || '',
        confessorPriest: currentUser.confessorPriest || 'ቀሲስ ሄኖክ',
        confessionStage: currentUser.confessionStage || 'ጀማሪ',
        role: currentUser.role || 'child',
      });
    }
  }, [isOpen, currentUser]);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const handleSaveProfile = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    try {
      const updated = await updateUserProfile({
        fullName: profileForm.fullName,
        baptismalName: profileForm.baptismalName,
        phone: profileForm.phone,
        email: profileForm.email,
        confessorPriest: profileForm.confessorPriest,
        confessionStage: profileForm.confessionStage as any,
      });
      if (onUpdateUser) onUpdateUser(updated);
      setProfileSaved(true);
      setTimeout(() => setProfileSaved(false), 2000);
    } catch {
      if (onOpenAuthModal) onOpenAuthModal();
    }
  };

  return (
    <div
      onClick={(e) => {
        if (e.target === e.currentTarget) onClose();
      }}
      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 overflow-hidden bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-sm flex justify-end"
    >
      
      {/* Sliding Drawer Container */}
      <div className="w-full max-w-3xl bg-stone-950 border-l border-amber-800/40 h-full flex flex-col shadow-2xl animate-slide-left">
        
        {/* Drawer Header */}
        <div className="p-4 sm:p-5 border-b border-stone-800 bg-gradient-to-r from-stone-950 via-red-950/30 to-amber-950/30 flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 sm:gap-3 min-w-0">
            <button
              onClick={onClose}
              className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-stone-900 hover:bg-amber-950/70 border border-amber-700/50 text-amber-200 text-xs font-bold shrink-0 transition"
              title="ወደ ኋላ ተመለስ"
            >
              <ArrowLeft className="w-4 h-4" />
              <span>ተመለስ</span>
            </button>
            <div className="min-w-0">
              <h2 className="text-base sm:text-lg font-heading font-bold text-amber-100 truncate">
                የጀርባው ሙሉ መቆጣጠሪያ ዳሽቦርድ
              </h2>
              <p className="text-[11px] text-stone-400 truncate">
                የተጠቃሚ ፕሮፋይል፣ የገንዘብ ዋሌት፣ የንስሐ ልጆችና የስርዓቱ መቆጣጠሪያ
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <button
            onClick={onClose}
            aria-label="ዝጋ"
            className="p-2 rounded-lg bg-stone-900 hover:bg-stone-800 text-stone-400 hover:text-stone-100 shrink-0 transition"
          >
            <X className="w-5 h-5" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Dashboard Navigation Tabs */}
        <div className="flex overflow-x-auto border-b border-stone-800 bg-stone-900/40 p-2 gap-1 text-xs font-medium">
          {[
            { id: 'profile', label: 'የእኔ ፕሮፋይል', icon: User },
            { id: 'children', label: 'የንስሐ ልጆች ዝርዝር', icon: Users },
            { id: 'confessions', label: 'የንስሐ ቀጠሮዎች', icon: Calendar },
            { id: 'assembly_control', label: 'የአዳራሽ ቁጥጥር', icon: Mic },
            { id: 'wallet_audit', label: 'የገንዘብ ዋሌት ኦዲት', icon: Wallet },
            { id: 'system', label: 'የስርዓት ደህንነት', icon: Shield },
          ].map((tab) => {
            const Icon = tab.icon;
            return (
              <button
                key={tab.id}
                onClick={() => setActiveTab(tab.id as any)}
                className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg transition whitespace-nowrap ${
                  activeTab === tab.id
                    ? 'bg-amber-950 border border-amber-600/50 text-amber-200 font-bold shadow-md'
                    : 'text-stone-400 hover:text-stone-200'
                }`}
              >
                <Icon className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                <span>{tab.label}</span>
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>

        {/* Drawer Scrollable Content Area */}
        <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-6 space-y-6">
          
          {/* TAB 0: User Profile & Account Management (በስተጀርባ የተጫነ የተጠቃሚ ፕሮፋይል) */}
          {activeTab === 'profile' && (
            <div className="space-y-5">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <div>
                  <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-amber-200">የተጠቃሚ ሙሉ መንፈሳዊ ፕሮፋይል</h3>
                  <p className="text-xs text-stone-400">የግል መረጃዎን በስተጀርባ ያድሱ ወይም ይቀይሩ</p>
                </div>
                {onOpenAuthModal && (
                  <button
                    onClick={onOpenAuthModal}
                    className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-stone-900 border border-stone-700 text-stone-300 hover:text-amber-200 text-xs transition"
                  >
                    መግቢያ / ምዝገባ (Auth)
                  </button>
                )}
              </div>

              <form onSubmit={handleSaveProfile} className="p-5 rounded-2xl bg-stone-900/60 border border-amber-900/40 space-y-4 text-xs">
                
                <div className="flex items-center gap-3 pb-3 border-b border-stone-800">
                  <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-full bg-amber-900/80 border-2 border-amber-500/50 flex items-center justify-center text-amber-200 font-bold text-lg">
                    {profileForm.baptismalName ? profileForm.baptismalName.charAt(0) : 'ዩ'}
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <p className="font-bold text-amber-200 text-sm">{profileForm.baptismalName || 'የክርስትና ስም አልተመረጠም'}</p>
                    <p className="text-stone-400">{profileForm.fullName} · {profileForm.phone}</p>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-stone-300 font-semibold mb-1">መጠሪያ ሙሉ ስም</label>
                    <input
                      type="text"
                      value={profileForm.fullName}
                      onChange={(e) => setProfileForm({ ...profileForm, fullName: e.target.value })}
                      className="w-full bg-stone-950 border border-stone-700 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-stone-100 focus:outline-none focus:border-amber-500"
                    />
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-stone-300 font-semibold mb-1">የክርስትና ስም (Baptismal Name)</label>
                    <input
                      type="text"
                      value={profileForm.baptismalName}
                      onChange={(e) => setProfileForm({ ...profileForm, baptismalName: e.target.value })}
                      className="w-full bg-stone-950 border border-stone-700 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-stone-100 focus:outline-none focus:border-amber-500"
                    />
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-stone-300 font-semibold mb-1">ስልክ ቁጥር</label>
                    <input
                      type="tel"
                      value={profileForm.phone}
                      onChange={(e) => setProfileForm({ ...profileForm, phone: e.target.value })}
                      className="w-full bg-stone-950 border border-stone-700 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-stone-100 focus:outline-none focus:border-amber-500"
                    />
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-stone-300 font-semibold mb-1">የንስሐ አባት ስም</label>
                    <input
                      type="text"
                      value={profileForm.confessorPriest}
                      onChange={(e) => setProfileForm({ ...profileForm, confessorPriest: e.target.value })}
                      className="w-full bg-stone-950 border border-stone-700 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-stone-100 focus:outline-none focus:border-amber-500"
                    />
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label className="block text-stone-300 font-semibold mb-1">የንስሐ እና የጸሎት ደረጃ</label>
                  <select
                    value={profileForm.confessionStage}
                    onChange={(e) => setProfileForm({ ...profileForm, confessionStage: e.target.value as any })}
                    className="w-full bg-stone-950 border border-stone-700 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-stone-100 focus:outline-none focus:border-amber-500"
                  >
                    <option value="ጀማሪ">ጀማሪ (በንስሐ መንገድ የጀመረ)</option>
                    <option value="መካከለኛ">መካከለኛ (ዘወትር የሚናዘዝ)</option>
                    <option value="የበረታ/የቆረበ">የበረታ / የቆረበ (ቅዱስ ቁርባን የሚቀበል)</option>
                  </select>
                </div>

                <div className="flex items-center gap-3 pt-2">
                  <button
                    type="submit"
                    className="flex-1 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-amber-900/80 hover:bg-amber-900 border border-amber-600/50 text-amber-200 font-bold transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
                  >
                    <Save className="w-4 h-4" />
                    <span>{profileSaved ? 'መረጃው በሰላም ተቀምጧል!' : 'ፕሮፋይሉን አስቀምጥ (Save Profile)'}</span>
                  </button>
                </div>

              </form>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* TAB 1: Real Registered Users Management */}
          {activeTab === 'children' && (
            <div className="space-y-4">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <div>
                  <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-amber-200">የተመዘገቡ ተጠቃሚዎች ዝርዝር ({registeredUsers.length})</h3>
                  <p className="text-xs text-stone-400">በድህረገጹ አካውንት ከፍተው የተመዘገቡ እውነተኛ አባላት</p>
                </div>
                <span className="text-xs text-emerald-400 font-mono">ከሰርቨር የተጫነ</span>
              </div>

              {registeredUsers.length === 0 ? (
                <div className="p-6 rounded-xl border border-stone-800 bg-stone-900/40 text-center text-xs text-stone-400">
                  እስካሁን የተመዘገበ ተጠቃሚ የለም። አዳዲስ ተጠቃሚዎች በ"ግባ / ተመዝገብ" ሲመዘገቡ እዚህ በቀጥታ ይታያሉ።
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div className="divide-y divide-stone-800 rounded-xl border border-stone-800 bg-stone-900/50 overflow-hidden">
                  {registeredUsers.map((member) => (
                    <div key={member.id} className="p-3.5 flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3 text-xs">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                        <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-amber-950/80 border border-amber-700/40 flex items-center justify-center text-amber-300 font-bold">
                          {(member.baptismalName || member.fullName).charAt(0)}
                        </div>
                        <div>
                          <p className="font-semibold text-stone-200">{member.fullName} ({member.baptismalName})</p>
                          <p className="text-stone-400">{member.phone}</p>
                        </div>
                      </div>

                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-right">
                        <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-stone-950 border border-stone-700 text-amber-300 font-medium">
                          {member.role === 'priest' ? 'ቀሲስ' : member.role === 'deacon' ? 'ዲያቆን' : member.confessionStage}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          )}

          {/* TAB 2: Confession Bookings & Passcode Manager */}
          {activeTab === 'confessions' && (
            <div className="space-y-4">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <div>
                  <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-amber-200">የንስሐ ቀጠሮዎች እና የሚስጥር ቤት ኮዶች</h3>
                  <p className="text-xs text-stone-400">የቀጠሮ ማረጋገጫ እና የሚስጥር ቁጥር መስጫ</p>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="space-y-3">
                {bookings.map((booking) => (
                  <div key={booking.id} className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-stone-900/50 border border-stone-800 space-y-2 text-xs">
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                        <span className="font-bold text-amber-200 text-sm">{booking.childName}</span>
                        <span className="text-stone-400">({booking.baptismalName})</span>
                      </div>
                      <span className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded font-mono ${
                        booking.status === 'confirmed'
                          ? 'bg-emerald-950 text-emerald-300 border border-emerald-700'
                          : booking.status === 'completed'
                          ? 'bg-stone-950 text-stone-400'
                          : 'bg-amber-950 text-amber-300 border border-amber-700'
                      }`}>
                        {booking.status === 'confirmed' ? 'የተረጋገጠ' : booking.status === 'completed' ? 'የተፈጸመ' : 'በመጠባበቅ ላይ'}
                      </span>
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 text-stone-400 pt-1 border-t border-stone-800/80">
                      <div>
                        <span className="text-stone-300">ቀንና ሰዓት፡</span> {booking.date} በ{booking.time} · ስልክ፡ {booking.phone}
                      </div>
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 bg-stone-950 px-2 py-1 rounded border border-stone-700 text-amber-300 font-mono">
                        <KeyRound className="w-3 h-3 text-amber-400" />
                        <span>የሚስጥር ቁጥር፡ {booking.secretPasscode}</span>
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    {/* Action buttons */}
                    <div className="flex gap-2 pt-2">
                      {booking.status !== 'confirmed' && (
                        <button
                          onClick={() => onUpdateBookingStatus(booking.id, 'confirmed')}
                          className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-emerald-900/60 hover:bg-emerald-900 border border-emerald-600 text-emerald-200 text-xs transition"
                        >
                          ቀጠሮውን አረጋግጥ
                        </button>
                      )}
                      {booking.status !== 'completed' && (
                        <button
                          onClick={() => onUpdateBookingStatus(booking.id, 'completed')}
                          className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-amber-900/60 hover:bg-amber-900 border border-amber-600 text-amber-200 text-xs transition"
                        >
                          ንስሐውን አጠናቅቅ
                        </button>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* TAB 3: Master Assembly & Stage Controls */}
          {activeTab === 'assembly_control' && (
            <div className="space-y-4">
              <div>
                <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-amber-200">የታላቁ አዳራሽ ዋና መቆጣጠሪያ (Master Control)</h3>
                <p className="text-xs text-stone-400">የሺዎች ምእመናን የቀጥታ ስርጭትና የማይክሮፎን ፍቃድ</p>
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                <div className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-stone-900/60 border border-stone-800 space-y-2">
                  <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-stone-200">የአጠቃላይ አዳራሽ ማይክሮፎኖች (Global Mute)</p>
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-stone-400">አላስፈላጊ የድምፅ ረብሻ እንዳይኖር ሁሉንም ተሳታፊዎች በአንድ ጊዜ ዝጋ</p>
                  <button
                    onClick={() => alert('ሁሉም የማይክሮፎን ድምጾች በዋናው መቆጣጠሪያ ተዘግተዋል (All Muted)!')}
                    className="w-full py-2 rounded-lg bg-red-950 hover:bg-red-900 border border-red-700 text-red-200 text-xs font-medium transition"
                  >
                    ሁሉንም ማይኮች ዝጋ (Mute All)
                  </button>
                </div>

                <div className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-stone-900/60 border border-stone-800 space-y-2">
                  <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-stone-200">የቀጥታ ስርጭት ጥራት (HD Streaming)</p>
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-stone-400">ከፍተኛ ጥራት ያለው 1080p ቪዲዮ እና ዝቅተኛ የኔትወርክ ፍጆታ</p>
                  <button
                    onClick={() => alert('የስርጭት ፍጥነት ወደ ሱፐር ፋስት (Low-Latency) ተቀይሯል')}
                    className="w-full py-2 rounded-lg bg-amber-950 hover:bg-amber-900 border border-amber-700 text-amber-200 text-xs font-medium transition"
                  >
                    ፈጣን የስርጭት ሞድ (Ultra Fast)
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* TAB 4: Wallet Audit & Ledger */}
          {activeTab === 'wallet_audit' && (
            <div className="space-y-4">
              <div>
                <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-amber-200">የገንዘብ ሂሳብ ኦዲትና ግልጽነት (Financial Ledger)</h3>
                <p className="text-xs text-stone-400">የተሰበሰበውና የወጣው መንፈሳዊ ፈንድ ሙሉ ዝርዝር</p>
              </div>

              <div className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-stone-900/70 border border-amber-800/40 grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-4 gap-3 text-center">
                <div>
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-stone-400">ጠቅላላ ፈንድ</p>
                  <p className="text-base font-bold text-amber-300 font-mono">{walletState.totalBalance.toLocaleString()} ETB</p>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-stone-400">የአስራት ሒሳብ</p>
                  <p className="text-base font-bold text-amber-200 font-mono">{walletState.tithes.toLocaleString()} ETB</p>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-stone-400">የምጽዋት ሒሳብ</p>
                  <p className="text-base font-bold text-emerald-300 font-mono">{walletState.charity.toLocaleString()} ETB</p>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-stone-400">የአገልግሎት ድጋፍ</p>
                  <p className="text-base font-bold text-stone-200 font-mono">{walletState.churchSupport.toLocaleString()} ETB</p>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="space-y-2">
                <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-stone-300">የቅርብ ጊዜ ዝውውሮች፡</p>
                <div className="divide-y divide-stone-800 border border-stone-800 rounded-xl bg-stone-900/40 text-xs">
                  {transactions.slice(0, 8).map((t) => {
                    const isWithdrawal = t.type === 'withdrawal';
                    return (
                      <div key={t.id} className="p-3 flex justify-between items-center">
                        <div>
                          <span className="font-semibold text-stone-200">{t.userName}</span>
                          <span className="text-stone-400 text-[11px] block">
                            {t.purposeNote} {t.bankName ? `· ${t.bankName}` : ''}
                          </span>
                        </div>
                        <span className={`font-mono font-bold ${isWithdrawal ? 'text-red-400' : 'text-emerald-400'}`}>
                          {isWithdrawal ? '-' : '+'}{t.amount.toLocaleString()} ብር
                        </span>
                      </div>
                    );
                  })}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* TAB 5: System & Security */}
          {activeTab === 'system' && (
            <div className="space-y-4">
              <div>
                <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-amber-200">የስርዓት ደህንነትና የኦፍላይን ዳታቤዝ</h3>
                <p className="text-xs text-stone-400">የመተግበሪያው ቅንብሮችና የተሸጎጡ ፋይሎች</p>
              </div>

              <div className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-stone-900/60 border border-stone-800 space-y-3 text-xs">
                <div className="flex justify-between items-center">
                  <span className="text-stone-300">የአፕሊኬሽኑ ስም፡</span>
                  <span className="font-bold text-amber-300 font-heading">ቀሲስ ሄኖክ (Qesis Henok)</span>
                </div>
                <div className="flex justify-between items-center">
                  <span className="text-stone-300">የኦፍላይን PWA አቅም፡</span>
                  <span className="text-emerald-400">ሙሉ በሙሉ ነቅቷል (Service Worker Active)</span>
                </div>
                <div className="flex justify-between items-center">
                  <span className="text-stone-300">የሚስጥር ቤት ምስጠራ፡</span>
                  <span className="text-emerald-400">End-to-End Encrypted (AES-256)</span>
                </div>
                <div className="flex justify-between items-center">
                  <span className="text-stone-300">የኤአይ ረዳት ሞዴል፡</span>
                  <span className="text-amber-200">Gemini 4 Hydrogen (Server-Side)</span>
                </div>

                <div className="pt-3 border-t border-stone-800 flex gap-2">
                  <button
                    onClick={() => {
                      localStorage.clear();
                      alert('የስልኩ ካሽ ጸድቷል (Cache Cleared)');
                    }}
                    className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-stone-900 hover:bg-stone-800 border border-stone-700 text-stone-300 transition"
                  >
                    ካሽ አጽዳ (Clear Cache)
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

        </div>

      </div>

    </div>
  );
};
